# LabSync LIMS — Unified Student Identity & Roster Ingestion Architecture
## Master Engineering Specification & Implementation Plan

> **Date**: 2026-10-03  
> **Status**: APPROVED FOR EXECUTION  
> **Target Environment**: Next.js 16 App Router · Supabase PostgreSQL · LabSync LIMS  
> **Institutional Grounding**: Secondary & Higher Secondary Education (Class 11 & 12, Nepal)  
> **Reference Data Source**: Real-world school student register with hardware RFID Smart Cards (`Gaindakot, Nawalpur`)  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Problem Diagnosis

### 1.1 The Fundamental Flaw of the Legacy Ingestion Modal (`img1`)
The legacy import interface in `components/roster/roster-import-modal.tsx` suffered from severe usability and architectural defects:
1. **Developer Jargon Overload**: Confronted school administrators with scary and obscure technical labels: *"Smart Sync Mid-Term"*, *"Clean Slate Fresh Cohort"*, *"Deterministic Ingestion Protocol"*, and *"Bucket 1 to Bucket 4"*.
2. **Coupling Admission ID to Mutable Roll Numbers**: Synthesized artificial admission IDs like `ADM-2083-11SCI-14`. When students changed class (11 &rarr; 12) or when rolls were re-sequenced alphabetically, their permanent institutional ID became mathematically wrong and out-of-sync.
3. **Mandatory Roll Number Barrier**: Forbade importing students unless the spreadsheet already had strict roll numbers, even though schools do not have final rolls during new admissions or before academic turnover.
4. **Rigid Column Schema**: Threw errors on real-world school spreadsheets that contain modern school ERP columns such as `RFID No.`, `Username`, `Contact`, or `Address`.

### 1.2 The Breakthrough Discovery
Analysis of authentic institutional registers in Nepal revealed that modern secondary schools and +2 colleges issue physical **RFID Smart Cards** to every student (e.g. `1641052469`). This 10-digit hardware UID is:
- **100% Unique** across the entire student body.
- **Permanent** from Class 11 admission to Class 12 graduation.
- **Completely Independent** of mutable class rolls or changing sections.
- **Hardware-Ready** for future tap-in roll-call at the Computer Lab workstation door.

---

## 2. The 3-Tier Student Identifier Architecture

Every student entity is governed by three distinct identifier layers:

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                             3-TIER STUDENT IDENTIFIER ARCHITECTURE                             │
├──────────────────────┬────────────────────────┬─────────────┬─────────────┬──────────────────────┤
│ IDENTIFIER LAYER     │ DATABASE ATTRIBUTE     │ VISIBILITY  │ LIFECYCLE   │ INSTITUTIONAL ROLE   │
├──────────────────────┼────────────────────────┼─────────────┼─────────────┼──────────────────────┤
│ 1. The System ID     │ public.students.id     │ Hidden      │ Immutable   │ Internal PostgreSQL  │
│    (Primary Key)     │ (UUID v4)              │ (Zero UI)   │ (Lifetime)  │ foreign keys for     │
│                      │                        │             │             │ attendance & logs.   │
├──────────────────────┼────────────────────────┼─────────────┼─────────────┼──────────────────────┤
│ 2. Institutional ID  │ students.admission_no  │ Visible     │ Permanent   │ Physical RFID Smart  │
│    (Human / Display) │ (e.g. 1641052469 or    │ (Official)  │ (Lifetime)  │ Card, Fee Register,  │
│                      │  ADM-2083-0042)        │             │             │ Board Transcripts.   │
├──────────────────────┼────────────────────────┼─────────────┼─────────────┼──────────────────────┤
│ 3. Operational Roll  │ enrollments.roll_number│ Visible     │ Annual /    │ Daily lab attendance,│
│    (Annual Cohort)   │ (1, 2, 3 ... N)        │ (Classroom) │ Ephemeral   │ CAD workstation seat,│
│                      │                        │             │             │ A–Z alphabetical lock│
└──────────────────────┴────────────────────────┴─────────────┴─────────────┴──────────────────────┘
```

### Invariant Rules:
1. **System ID (`id` UUID)**: Generated via `gen_random_uuid()`. Never visible to users. Links `student_enrollments`, `log_attendance`, `workstation_seat_plans`, and `lab_incidents`.
2. **Institutional ID (`admission_no`)**:
   - If the spreadsheet contains `RFID No.`, `Card No`, `Admission No`, or `Student ID` &rarr; store that exact string verbatim.
   - If left blank in the spreadsheet &rarr; auto-generate a clean, sequential, year-based institutional sequence: `ADM-[YEAR]-[0001..9999]` (e.g. `ADM-2083-0001`), **completely decoupled from class or roll number**.
3. **Operational Roll (`roll_number`)**:
   - Belongs to `public.student_enrollments`, NOT `public.students`.
   - Initial import assigns temporary collision-free provisional integers ($1 \dots N$).
   - Official roll numbers are finalized via the **"Finalize & Lock Official Rolls (A–Z)"** action on the class roster.

---

## 3. Database Schema & Optional Fields Strategy

### 3.1 Strict vs. Optional Fields
To keep the system lightweight and avoid administrative friction, only **Full Name** is mandatory. All other biographical and contact attributes remain strictly optional:

```
public.students:
  ├── id (UUID, PK) ---------------------- [REQUIRED, System Generated]
  ├── full_name (TEXT) ------------------- [REQUIRED]
  ├── admission_no (TEXT) ---------------- [OPTIONAL / Auto-Sequential fallback] (Holds RFID No.)
  ├── registration_no (TEXT) ------------- [OPTIONAL] (NEB Board Registration)
  ├── gender (TEXT) ---------------------- [OPTIONAL] ('male' | 'female' | 'other' | NULL)
  ├── guardian_phone (TEXT) -------------- [OPTIONAL] (Contact phone number)
  ├── guardian_name (TEXT) --------------- [OPTIONAL]
  ├── email (TEXT) ----------------------- [OPTIONAL]
  ├── is_active (BOOLEAN) ---------------- [DEFAULT TRUE]
  └── created_at / updated_at ------------ [TIMESTAMPS]

public.student_enrollments:
  ├── id (UUID, PK) ---------------------- [REQUIRED, System Generated]
  ├── academic_year_id (TEXT, FK) -------- [REQUIRED]
  ├── class_id (TEXT, FK) ---------------- [REQUIRED]
  ├── student_id (UUID, FK) -------------- [REQUIRED]
  ├── roll_number (INT) ------------------ [REQUIRED, 1..200, Unique per Cohort]
  ├── section (TEXT) --------------------- [OPTIONAL]
  └── status (TEXT) ---------------------- ['active' | 'transferred' | 'graduated' | 'archived']
```

---

## 4. Intelligent Spreadsheet Parser Redesign

### 4.1 Native Support for Authentic School Spreadsheets
The spreadsheet parser in `lib/roster-ingestion/parser.ts` is upgraded to recognize authentic school export formats directly:

```
Sample Input: [ Roll | Name | Username | Gender | RFID No. | Contact | Address ]
```

### 4.2 Flexible Column Matching Dictionary:
- **Student Full Name (Required)**: `['name', 'studentname', 'fullname', 'nameofstudent']`
- **Institutional / RFID ID (Optional)**: `['rfidno', 'rfid', 'rfidnumber', 'cardno', 'smartcardno', 'admissionno', 'admno', 'studentid', 'idno']`
- **Roll Number Hint (Optional)**: `['roll', 'rollno', 'rollnumber', 'sn', 'snno', 'symbolno']`
- **Gender (Optional)**: `['gender', 'sex']` (Normalized to lowercase `'male'`, `'female'`, `'other'`)
- **Contact Phone (Optional)**: `['contact', 'phone', 'phoneno', 'mobile', 'guardianphone']`
- **Board Registration (Optional)**: `['registrationno', 'regno', 'boardreg', 'symbolnumber']`
- **Extraneous Columns (Gracefully Ignored)**: `['username', 'address', 'dob', 'photo', ...]` pass through with zero errors.

---

## 5. Modern 3-Step Import Experience (Replacing `img1`)

### 5.1 Elimination of Developer Jargon
- ❌ Drop "Smart Sync Mid-Term" and "Clean Slate Fresh Cohort".
- ❌ Drop "Deterministic Ingestion Protocol".
- ❌ Drop "Bucket 1", "Bucket 2", "Bucket 3", "Bucket 4".
- ❌ Drop "Replace Occupant" and "Assign Next Roll" developer switches.

### 5.2 The Streamlined 3-Step Workflow:

#### Step 1: Drag & Drop Upload
- Clean, focused dropzone supporting `.xlsx`, `.xls`, `.csv`.
- Clear guidance banner:
  > *"Upload your class list. Only **Student Name** is required. If your sheet contains **RFID No.**, **Roll**, or **Gender**, they will be automatically captured."*
- Prominent button: `<Download className="h-4 w-4" /> Download Clean Excel Template`.

#### Step 2: High-Clarity Reconciliation Summary
A 3-card executive overview replaces the confusing bucket tabs:
```
┌─────────────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────────┐  ┌─────────────────────────┐
│ 🟢 New Admissions       │  │ 🔵 Existing Recognized  │  │ ⚪ Not in Uploaded File │
│    18 students to add   │  │    14 students matched  │  │    2 currently enrolled │
└─────────────────────────┘  └─────────────────────────┘  └─────────────────────────┘
```
- **Student Reconciliation Table**: Clean rows with visual badges:
  - `🟢 New Student`: Ready to be enrolled. Shows auto-assigned or parsed RFID/ID.
  - `🔵 Matched Profile`: Recognized by RFID or normalized name.
- **Handling Missing Students**:
  A single, plain-English setting:
  - `[✓] Keep enrolled students active (Add missing newcomers alongside them)`
  - `[ ] Mark missing students as Transferred / Left School (Reconcile master list)`

#### Step 3: Success & Roll Finalization Handoff
- Clear confirmation: *"✓ Successfully updated roster for Class 12 Science (32 students active)."*
- Action button: **"Go to Class Roster to Finalize Official Rolls (A–Z)"**.

## 6. Operational Workflows for All 4 School Scenarios

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        4 INSTITUTIONAL OPERATIONAL SCENARIOS                           │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                                        │
│  SCENARIO A: First-Time Database Setup (Fresh School Onboarding)                       │
│  ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────                       │
│  1. Admin opens Class (e.g. Class 11 Science) → clicks "Import Students".              │
│  2. Uploads existing school Excel sheet (names + RFID numbers).                        │
│  3. System creates student person records with permanent RFID IDs.                     │
│  4. Admin clicks "Finalize & Lock Official Rolls (A–Z)" on the Class Roster.           │
│     → 1-Click alphabetical 1..N roll assignment completed!                             │
│                                                                                        │
│  SCENARIO B: Annual Academic Year Turnover (Class 11 → Class 12)                       │
│  ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────                       │
│  1. Admin executes Cohort Promotion Wizard:                                            │
│     - Promoted students advance from 11 Sci → 12 Sci.                                  │
│     - Repeaters remain in 11 Sci.                                                      │
│  2. If new lateral transfer students arrive in Class 12:                               │
│     - Admin clicks "Import Students" on Class 12 → uploads their names / RFIDs.        │
│  3. Admin opens Class 12 Roster:                                                       │
│     - Promoted students + Newcomers are displayed together.                            │
│     - Admin clicks "Finalize & Lock Official Rolls (A–Z)".                             │
│     - Rolls 1..N are re-indexed across the complete unified cohort!                    │
│                                                                                        │
│  SCENARIO C: New Academic Year Class 11 Intake                                         │
│  ─────────────────────────────────────────────                                         │
│  1. In Class 11, repeaters from last year are already sitting safely with rolls.       │
│  2. Admin uploads the new SEE admission Excel sheet to Class 11.                       │
│  3. System recognizes repeaters, adds new admissions, and merges them safely.          │
│  4. Admin clicks "Finalize & Lock Official Rolls (A–Z)".                               │
│     → Blends repeaters and new intake into one official A–Z sequence!                  │
│                                                                                        │
│  SCENARIO D: Mid-Term Departures & Arrivals (TC / Late Admission)                      │
│  ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────                      │
│  • Departure (Transfer Certificate / Dropped Out):                                     │
│    - DO NOT DELETE (preserves historical lab logs & attendance compliance).            │
│    - Admin sets status to "Transferred / Archived".                                    │
│    - Physical workstation CAD seat is released immediately.                            │
│  • Late Arrival:                                                                       │
│    - Admin clicks "Add Student" or imports 1-row Excel.                                │
│    - Receives next available provisional roll without disturbing other students.       │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 7. Implementation Roadmap & Technical Deliverables

### Phase 1: Core Parsing & Identity Engine
1. Update `lib/roster-ingestion/parser.ts`:
   - Add column aliases for `RFID No.`, `Gender`, `Contact`.
   - Relax `Roll Number` requirement (treat as optional integer hint).
2. Update `lib/roster-ingestion/template.ts`:
   - Generate single unified template with columns: `[Full Name * | RFID / Admission No | Roll No | Gender | Contact]`.
3. Update `lib/roster-ingestion/validator.ts`:
   - Purge `formatAutoAdmissionNo(year, class, roll)` function.
   - Replace with sequential `ADM-[YEAR]-[SEQUENCE]` or preserve provided RFID string.

### Phase 2: Database RPC & Backend Hardening
1. Update `commit_roster_ingestion_tx` stored procedure:
   - Accept raw `admission_no` (RFID) without synthetic class/roll prefixes.
   - Support optional `gender` and `guardian_phone`.
2. Ensure collision-free provisional roll assignment for incoming batches.

### Phase 3: UI Redesign of `RosterImportModal`
1. Re-skin `components/roster/roster-import-modal.tsx`:
   - Replace old two-card strategy selector with clean drag-and-drop upload zone.
   - Build 3-card reconciliation summary (New Admissions, Matched Existing, Missing from Sheet).
   - Add clear toggle for missing students: `Keep Active` vs. `Mark as Transferred`.
   - Replace results screen with direct link to Class Roster.

### Phase 4: Verification & Automated Tests
1. Run `npx tsc --noEmit` to ensure strict 0 TypeScript errors.
2. Update and run test suites:
   - `tests/phase4-roster-ingestion.test.mjs`
   - `tests/phase6-roster-clean-slate.test.mjs`
   - `tests/phase6-roster-lifecycle.test.mjs`

# Staff & Faculty Profile Pictures Architecture

> **Date**: October 4, 2026  
> **Status**: Approved & Architected  
> **Target Roles**: Teachers (`teacher`), Lab In-Charge (`lab_incharge`), Head of Department (`hod`), and Super Admin (`super_admin` / `admin`)  
> **Target Modules**:  
> - Database: `public.profiles.photo_url` & `public.profiles.avatar_url`
> - Server Actions: `app/actions/auth.ts` (`getCurrentUserProfile`, `getAllUsers`, `updateCurrentUserPhoto`, `updateUserProfilePhoto`, `updateUserCredentials`)
> - Top Navigation: `components/layout/user-profile-menu.tsx`
> - Admin Console: `components/admin/user-access-manager.tsx` (Table, Drawer, Edit Modal)
> - Faculty State: `hooks/use-infrastructure-state.ts`
> - Touchpoints: `components/records/incident-detail-drawer.tsx`, `components/dashboard/live-session-cockpit.tsx`

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Purpose

Following the deployment of the Student Visual Identity System, institutional staff and faculty require equal visual representation across LabSync LIMS / LMR:
1. **Top Navbar Identity**: Logged-in Teachers, Lab In-Charges, HODs, and Super Admins currently see initials (`LA`, `KT`). A real face avatar personalizes the operational command center and instills institutional ownership.
2. **Staff Directory & RBAC Management**: In `/admin` Tab 04 ("User Accounts" / Staff Directory), administrators managing faculty accounts can visually identify teachers and laboratory managers.
3. **Self-Service & Admin Management**:
   - Any authenticated user can upload, change, or remove their own profile photo directly from the top-right user menu.
   - Super Admins can set or update any staff member's photo from the User Access Manager directory or drawer.
4. **Contextual Recognition**:
   - In the **Incident Detail Drawer**, the supervising Subject Teacher appears with their real face photo chip.
   - In the **Live Session Cockpit**, the ongoing session displays the instructor's photo avatar.

---

## 2. Technical Architecture & Data Models

### 2.1 Database Schema (`public.profiles`)
Add `photo_url` and `avatar_url` to `public.profiles`:
```sql
ALTER TABLE public.profiles 
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS photo_url TEXT,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS avatar_url TEXT;

COMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.photo_url IS 'Base64 data URL or storage URI for user profile avatar';
COMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.avatar_url IS 'Alias/fallback storage URI for user profile avatar';
```

### 2.2 Client-Side Compression (< 20 KB)
- Clamped to 256×256 pixels, square center-crop.
- WebP/JPEG format at 75% quality.
- Embedded as compact Data URLs, requiring zero external S3 bucket setup and loading instantaneously with 0 network roundtrips.

---

## 3. Server Actions & Security Architecture

1. `updateCurrentUserPhoto(photoUrl: string)`:
   - Validates active session via `getServerUserScope()`.
   - Updates `public.profiles` for the caller's UUID.
   - Invalidates in-memory user cache and calls `revalidatePath('/', 'layout')`.
2. `updateUserProfilePhoto(userId: string, photoUrl: string)`:
   - Guarded by Super Admin scope check.
   - Records an immutable audit event (`UPDATE` on `user_role`).
3. `updateUserCredentials(userId, data)`:
   - Accepts optional `photo_url` to support updating photo during full credential edits.
4. `getAllUsers()` & `getCurrentUserProfile()`:
   - Explicitly queries and returns `photo_url` and `avatar_url`.

---

## 4. UI Touchpoints

1. **Header User Profile Menu (`components/layout/user-profile-menu.tsx`)**:
   - Trigger button: Crisp circular photo avatar inside role-tinted ring (Amber for Super Admin, Indigo for Lab Incharge / HOD, Slate for Teacher), with initials fallback.
   - Popover card: Large profile banner avatar with hover camera overlay allowing 1-click photo upload or removal.
2. **User Access Manager (`components/admin/user-access-manager.tsx`)**:
   - Staff directory table: 34×34px avatar chip for each user row with initials fallback and click-to-upload.
   - Slide-over Drawer: Large 64×64px avatar with `<Camera className="h-4 w-4" />` upload trigger.
   - Edit Modal: Photo preview and upload button.
3. **Incident Detail Drawer (`components/records/incident-detail-drawer.tsx`)**:
   - Subject Teacher metadata card shows the teacher's photo avatar alongside their name.
4. **Live Session Cockpit (`components/dashboard/live-session-cockpit.tsx`)**:
   - Ongoing practical session card shows the instructor's photo avatar chip.

---

## 5. Verification Protocol
- `npx tsc --noEmit` must pass with 0 errors.
- Automated tests covering profile photo updates and retrieval.

FILE: doc\plans\2026-10-05-computer-lab-floor-plan-and-projection-elevation.md

# LabSync LIMS — Computer Lab Floor Plan, Live Cockpit & Projection Elevation
## Master Engineering Specification & Architecture Blueprint

> **Date**: 2026-10-05  
> **Status**: APPROVED & IN ACTIVE EXECUTION  
> **Target Environment**: Next.js 16 App Router · Supabase PostgreSQL · Tailwind CSS · React 19  
> **Institutional Context**: Academic Laboratories (50-terminal Computer Labs, Class 11 & 12 Science/Computer Science, Nepal)  
> **Target Routes**: `/computer-lab`, `/computer-lab/projector`, `/maintenance`, `/terminal/[id]`  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Vision

The laboratory floor plan is the central operational nervous system of academic computer facilities. In high-density institutions (34 to 50+ terminals), laboratory management encompasses far more than simple static desk icons:
1. **Dynamic & Manual Seat Allocation**: Automatic seating by Class Roll ($1 \dots N \to \text{PC-01} \dots \text{PC-50}$) and alphabetical order with drag-and-drop manual adjustments.
2. **Displaced Student Auto-Detection**: When a workstation suffers hardware/software faults, the system instantly detects displaced students and coordinates seamless temporary session replacement without destroying the permanent baseline plan.
3. **Wall-Mounted Big-Screen Projection Mode**: A dedicated, clutter-free wall display on the lab projector/TV (`/computer-lab/projector`) displaying the active session seating chart so students entering the lab find their seats in seconds.
4. **Machine-by-Machine Maintenance Checklists**: Fleet task checklists in `/maintenance` (e.g. VS Code rollout, OS updates, thermal paste replacement) with machine-by-machine checkboxes synced to the floor plan.
5. **Live Severity-Coded Incident Tracking**: Workstations dynamically light up according to fault severity (Critical Rose, High Amber, Low Cyan) with full diagnostic and repair history.
6. **Forensic Seating Audit in Practical Records**: Every practical log captures a frozen session seating snapshot. If unauthorized software, games, or OS tampering is discovered on a PC, administrators can inspect the historical usage timeline to identify who sat at that station across past practicals.
7. **Zero-Login Student Terminal Self-Reporting**: Students report issues directly from their terminal screen (Windows activation, missing software, crashes) without passwords, protected by a 2-tier hardware lock.

---

## 2. The 5 Core Operational Pillars

### Pillar 1: Dynamic & Manual Seating with Displaced Student Replacement
- **Auto-Populate Strategies**:
  - Sequential Roll: Roll 1 $\to$ PC-01, Roll 2 $\to$ PC-02 $\dots$
  - Alphabetical (A–Z): Alphabetical roster order.
  - Previous Cohort Template Clone.
- **Displaced Student Detection**:
  - When a workstation status becomes `under_repair` or `flagged` (Critical/High), the system checks `workstation_seat_plans`.
  - Displaced students are placed in a high-visibility Cockpit Queue:  
    `⚠️ 1 Displaced Student: Kaustuv Thapa (Roll 14) from PC-14 → [Assign Temporary PC]`
  - Highlights all available, operational terminals and allows 1-click temporary session assignment (`session_overrides`).

### Pillar 2: Wall-Mounted Big-Screen Projection Mode (`/computer-lab/projector`)
- **Purpose**: Wall projector / large monitor mounted at the front of the lab.
- **Design Philosophy**:
  - Zero admin chrome: no sidebar, no breadcrumbs, no edit buttons, no navigation tabs.
  - Ultra-high contrast dark obsidian canvas with crisp typography.
  - Large legible Roll Badges (`R-14`), full student names, and photo avatars.
  - Real-time auto-refresh (every 10s or live WebSocket/PostgreSQL changes) reflecting teacher roll-call marks immediately.
  - Color Semantics:
    - 🟢 Emerald: Assigned student marked Present.
    - 🟡 Amber: Temporary Seat Replacement.
    - ⚪ Subtle Slate: Empty / Unassigned Station.
    - 🔴 Pulsing Rose: Out of Service / Hardware Fault (Warning: Do Not Sit Here).

### Pillar 3: Multi-PC Maintenance Checklists (Fleet Operations)
- Integrated into `/maintenance`:
  - Routine task definitions (e.g., *"Install Python 3.12 & VS Code 2026"*, *"Clean CPU Dust & Re-apply Thermal Paste"*).
  - Machine checklist grid: displays PC-01 to PC-50 with 1-click status toggles: `[✓] Completed`, `[ ] Pending`, `[!] Blocked`.
  - Live progress meter (`28/34 Terminals Completed - 82%`).
  - Synced Floor Plan Overlay: Visual badge overlay on the floor plan showing task progress per PC.
  - Certified Logbook archival upon task completion.

### Pillar 4: Live Severity-Coded Incident Tracking & Workstation Linking
- Reporting an incident in `/incidents` or dashboard modal for "Computer Lab" prompts for **"Affected Workstation / PC No"**.
- Dynamically derives node status on the floor plan:
  - 🔴 **Critical**: Pulsing Rose glow (`#EF4444`) with alert siren icon (e.g., PSU failure, smoke, smashed monitor).
  - 🟡 **High**: Amber glow (`#F59E0B`) with wrench icon (e.g., Windows boot loop, blue screen, crashing OS).
  - 🔵 **Medium/Low**: Yellow/Cyan glow (e.g., mouse wheel stuck, missing keycap).
- Click-to-Diagnose drawer allows immediate triage, photo inspection, and certified resolution with technician notes.

### Pillar 5: Forensic Seating History & Session Snapshots
- When an instructor submits a practical log (`practical_logs`), the system freezes the active seating arrangement into the log record.
- **Two Investigative Workflows**:
  1. **Record Review (`/records`)**: Opening a historical practical session displays the interactive floor plan showing who was present and seated at each desk during that class.
  2. **Machine Technical Biography (`/computer-lab` $\to$ Workstation $\to$ Usage Timeline)**:
     - Chronological list of past student seatings:
       - *Yesterday 11:30 AM (Class 11 Sci A)*: Kaustuv Thapa (Roll 14)
       - *Wednesday 02:00 PM (Class 12 Sci B)*: Rohan Shrestha (Roll 22)
       - *Tuesday 10:00 AM (Class 11 Sci B)*: Aayush Poudel (Roll 05)
     - Empowers administrators to identify who altered system settings, installed unauthorized software/games, or caused physical damage.

---

## 3. Zero-Login Student Terminal Self-Reporting & Anti-Spoofing Architecture

### 3.1 The Problem
Giving 500+ students individual user accounts for lab reporting creates extreme friction (forgotten passwords, account sharing, unauthorized logins). Conversely, open links allow pranksters to submit false reports for other terminals.

### 3.2 The 2-Tier Hardware Lock Solution
```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│               2-TIER HARDWARE TERMINAL LOCK (ANTI-PRANK DEFENSE)                 │
├────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tier 1: Cryptographic Device   │ • Admin configures browser shortcut once:       │
│ Pairing Token (Browser Level)  │   `/terminal/pair?station=PC-30&secret=...`     │
│                                │ • Browser stores an encrypted persistent token.  │
│                                │ • Submitting for any other PC is rejected:       │
│                                │   "Mismatch: This terminal is registered as     │
│                                │    PC-30. You cannot submit reports for PC-12." │
├────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tier 2: Local LAN IP Subnet    │ • Workstations in academic labs have static LAN │
│ Mapping (Network Level)        │   IPs (e.g., 192.168.1.130 = PC-30).             │
│                                │ • Server verifies `client_ip`. Even if cookies  │
│                                │   are wiped or incognito opened, the network    │
│                                │   cable confirms it is PC-30.                   │
│                                │ • Mobile phones and external devices blocked.   │
└────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 3.3 Student Reporting Experience (`/terminal`)
1. Student clicks desktop shortcut "Report PC Issue".
2. Page opens pre-bound to **PC-12 (Computer Lab 01)**.
3. Automatically identifies active session and pre-fills student name from the live seat plan (*"Are you Kaustuv Thapa (Roll 14)?"*).
4. 1-Tap Quick Issue Tags:
   - 🪟 *Windows Not Activated*
   - 📄 *MS Office / Word / Excel Unlicensed*
   - 📦 *Required Software Missing (Python, VS Code, C++)*
   - 💥 *Software Crashing / Freezing*
   - 🖱️ *Mouse / Keyboard / Audio Fault*
   - 🌐 *No Internet / Network Disconnected*
   - ⚡ *PC Restarting / Blue Screen*
   - 📝 *Other (custom note)*
5. Tap **"Request Assistance"** $\to$ Real-time amber beacon glows on Teacher & Lab In-Charge Cockpit with instant audio chime/toast.

---

## 4. Multi-Phase Implementation Roadmap

| Phase | Scope | Focus Areas | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Phase 2** | **Auto-Grid Layout & Projection Screen** | • Auto-arrange presets (4 Rows, 5 Rows, Dual Islands) & batch coordinate updates.<br>• Enhanced scalable workstation nodes with high-clarity student avatars & legible typography.<br>• Fullscreen Big-Screen Projection Mode (`/computer-lab/projector`) with live 10s auto-refresh. | **Complete (100%)** |
| **Phase 3** | **Dynamic Seating & Forensic History** | • Auto-assign by roll / alphabet & displaced student auto-detection queue.<br>• Session seating snapshot in `practical_logs`.<br>• Workstation student usage chronological timeline. | **Complete (100%)** |
| **Phase 4** | **Multi-PC Maintenance Routine Checklists** | • Fleet task checklists in `/maintenance` with machine-by-machine toggles.<br>• Synchronized progress overlay on the CAD Floor Plan.<br>• Certified service logbook archiving. | **Complete (100%)** |
| **Phase 1** | **Zero-Login Student Terminal & Live Beacon** | • `/terminal` route with 1-tap issue chips.<br>• 2-Tier anti-spoofing pairing token & LAN IP binding.<br>• Real-time help beacon on Live Cockpit with toast notification. | Queued (Next Up) |

---

## 5. Architectural Invariants
1. **Rule #10 (High-Density Cards)**: No double-nested card wrappers; standalone `.glass-card` elements in direct grid.
2. **Rule #1 (Zero Literal `+`)**: Action buttons must use `<Plus className="..." />`, never raw `+` characters.
3. **Database Relational Integrity**: All positions stored in `public.workstations` (`pos_x`, `pos_y`, `row_label`).
4. **Strict TypeScript & Zero Lints**: All new code and types must pass `npx tsc --noEmit` with 0 errors.

  - Test Suite: [`tests/phase3-seating-and-forensics.test.mjs`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/tests/phase3-seating-and-forensics.test.mjs)
- **Features Implemented**:
  1. **Dual Auto-Assign Strategies & Natural Alphanumeric Sort**:
     - `Auto-Assign (Roll)`: Assigns unseated students in sequential roll order ($1 \dots N$).
     - `Auto-Assign (A–Z)`: Assigns unseated students alphabetically by full name.
     - Workstations are naturally sorted (`PC-01, PC-02 ... PC-10, PC-50`), eliminating string sort anomalies.
  2. **1-Click Cohort Plan Reset**:
     - `clearAllCohortSeats`: Allows Super Admin and Lab In-Charge to clear seat allocations for an entire cohort with safety confirmation, returning students to the unassigned tray without altering enrollment records.
  3. **Displaced Student Auto-Detection & Replacement Queue**:
     - Automatically scans active session seat plans against workstation issues (`under_repair`, `flagged` critical/high).
     - Renders an amber **Displaced Students Triage Queue** above the cockpit bar:
       `⚠️ Kaustuv Thapa (Roll 14) from PC-14 (Under Repair) → [Assign Replacement PC-28 (Operational)]`
     - 1-click trigger calls `assignSessionSeatOverride`, updating both teacher cockpit and wall projector with amber `Temp Relocation` tags.
  4. **Frozen Seating Snapshots in Practical Logs**:
     - Added column `seating_snapshot jsonb` to `public.practical_logs`.
     - `submitLiveSession` automatically freezes the complete room seating arrangement (station IDs, labels, student roll, full name, presence, and temporary relocation flags) into the practical log row.
  5. **Historical Seating Chart Viewer in `/records`**:
     - Opening any historical practical session in [`components/records/practical-detail-modal.tsx`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/components/records/practical-detail-modal.tsx) displays an expandable **"Laboratory Seating Chart Snapshot"** rendering every workstation with student name, roll badge, presence/absence color pills, and temporary relocation indicators.
  6. **Workstation Usage Forensic Timeline**:
     - Server action `getWorkstationTechnicalBiography` queries `practical_logs` where `seating_snapshot` includes that workstation.
     - Workstation Biography Drawer provides a dedicated **"Usage Timeline"** tab showing the chronological history of students who sat at that specific machine (Date, Time, Class, Subject, Student, Roll, Present/Absent, Topic Learned).
     - Empowers administrators to instantly identify who altered Windows settings, installed unauthorized games, or caused hardware damage.
- **How to Test / Verify**:
  ```bash
  node --experimental-strip-types --import ./tests/register-hooks.mjs --test tests/phase3-seating-and-forensics.test.mjs
  ```
  - In browser:
    - **Seating**: Go to `/computer-lab` &rarr; Baseline Seating &rarr; test "Auto-Assign (Roll)" vs "Auto-Assign (A–Z)" vs "Clear Plan".
    - **Displaced Triage**: In `/maintenance` or diagnostics, flag PC-01 with a Critical fault. Switch to Live Session Cockpit. Observe the amber Displaced Student Banner appear. Relocate the student with 1 click.
    - **Records Snapshot**: In Live Session, click "Save & Submit Log". Navigate to `/records` and click the practical log. Observe the interactive Laboratory Seating Chart Snapshot showing who sat at each terminal.
    - **Forensic Timeline**: Click on any workstation in the floor plan to open the Biography Drawer. Click the "Usage Timeline" tab to view historical session seatings.

## 5. Architectural Invariants Enforced

Agents resuming work on this repository **MUST** preserve these invariants:
1. **Rule #1 (No Literal `+` in Buttons)**:
   - Always use `<Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> <span>Add Item</span>`, never raw `+` text.
2. **Rule #8 (Strict TypeScript)**:
   - Every commit must pass `npx tsc --noEmit` with **0 errors**.
3. **Rule #10 (High-Density Cards)**:
   - Standalone `.glass-card` elements in direct grid, 2-row ~76px total height, zero nested parent containers.
4. **Rule #13 (Server Action Pure Functions)**:
   - Server action files (`app/actions/*`) marked with `'use server'` must strictly export `async function`s only. Pure domain math, types, and constants belong in `lib/`.
5. **Rule #16 (Zero Shadow Databases)**:
   - All reports, seating history, and forensic timelines query live PostgreSQL tables (`practical_logs`, `workstations`, `workstation_issues`, `workstation_seat_plans`). Never introduce disconnected mock caches.
6. **Rule #19 (Dynamic Workstation Status)**:
   - Operational status is not a static column in `workstations`. It is derived dynamically from active `workstation_issues`:
     - `status = 'in_progress'` &rarr; `under_repair`
     - `status = 'open'` &rarr; `flagged`
     - No open issues &rarr; `operational`


================================================================================

# Student Terminal Agent & Tamper-Proof Hardware Telemetry Architecture

> **Document**: `doc/plans/2026-10-14-student-terminal-agent-and-tamper-proof-architecture.md`  
> **Status**: Approved Master Implementation Blueprint  
> **Author**: Antigravity Pair Programming Agent & User  
> **Target Environment**: Academic Computer Labs (50+ Terminals), Windows 10/11 OS, Supabase Free Tier  
> **Current Branch**: `feature/student-terminal-agent`  
> **Date**: October 15, 2026  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Institutional Mission

In academic computer laboratories (Secondary Class 11, Class 12, and undergraduate labs), students need a friction-free way to report workstation faults (e.g., sticky keyboard keys, broken mouse clicks, missing Turbo C++ or Python, network dropouts) without requiring individual student account logins.

However, generic web URLs (e.g. `/terminal/PC-26`) expose critical vulnerabilities:
1. **Prank URL Spoofing**: Students can alter URL parameters in browser tabs to submit false reports for other students' desks.
2. **Mobile Pranks**: Students could open the URL from mobile phones or outside the lab.
3. **No Hardware Visibility**: The Lab In-Charge has zero visibility into actual physical machine specifications (RAM, CPU, Drive C free space, OS version) to verify whether reported software issues stem from low storage or uninstalled environments.

### The Unified Solution
This blueprint establishes a **two-pillar architecture**:
1. **Lightweight Windows Native Client Agent (`labsync-agent.ps1` & `setup-labsync.bat`)**: A zero-dependency PowerShell script on lab PCs that extracts machine hardware specs (CPU, RAM, Drive C storage, OS build, MAC address, LAN IP) and binds to Windows `MachineGuid`.
2. **Cryptographic One-Time Handshake & HttpOnly Cookie Protocol**: When launched, the agent requests a single-use token from `/api/terminal/handshake`, opens the default browser to `/terminal/auth?ticket=<token>`, sets an `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Strict` session cookie, and immediately redirects to a clean URL (`/terminal`) with **zero station parameters in the address bar**. Reports can only originate from that physical machine.

---

## 2. Explicit Scope Decisions & Boundaries

Based on institutional requirements and real-world high school lab constraints:

| Proposed Feature | Decision | Engineering & Operational Rationale |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **"Raise Hand" / Digital Chime** | ❌ **DROPPED** | In physical computer labs, students and instructors are in the same physical room. Students verbally ask questions or wave their hands in person. A digital button causes spam, requires audio chime handling, and adds unnecessary complexity with zero benefit. |
| **Student Mobile QR Scanning** | ❌ **DROPPED** | Mobile phones are strictly prohibited for students in Class 11 & 12 academic labs in Nepal. Requiring phone QR scans is unrealistic and obsolete for students. Reporting is desktop-native only. |
| **Dead-PC Issue Categories** | ❌ **DROPPED from Student View** | Issues like *"Monitor Blackout"* or *"PSU Won't Boot / No Power"* cannot physically be reported by a student on a dead machine. Those categories belong exclusively to the Teacher / Lab In-Charge Cockpit Drawer. |
| **Student Reporting Categories** | 🟢 **ALIVE & IN-USE ONLY** | Limited strictly to problems encountered while the desktop is running: <br>1. *Mouse / Keyboard Fault* (keys jammed, button not clicking)<br>2. *No Internet / LAN Disconnected* (network drop)<br>3. *Missing Lab Software* (Turbo C++, Python, VS Code, MySQL)<br>4. *Application Freeze / Crash* (IDE frozen, compile crash)<br>5. *Audio / Headphone Issue* (no audio output)<br>6. *Other Workstation Problem* (with custom notes). |
| **100% Unique Hardware Identifier** | 🟢 **Composite Silicon ID: `$regGuid-$macAddr`** | MachineGuid in registry (`HKLM:\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Cryptography\MachineGuid`) can be identical if lab drives are cloned without `sysprep /generalize`. Combining `$regGuid` with physical network adapter MAC address (`$macAddr`) + `$env:COMPUTERNAME` guarantees 100% uniqueness even across cloned disk images. |
| **The "LAN Disconnected" Trap** | 🟢 **Local PowerShell Diagnostic Alert** | If physical Ethernet cable is unplugged, HTTP handshake cannot reach the server. Agent catches network exceptions, tests local link, and displays a bold red/yellow console warning on the physical desktop. Web UI Category #2 is reserved for intermittent/slow connectivity or DNS issues. |
| **Strict IP Subnet Gating** | ❌ **REJECTED (Telemetry Only)** | School routers frequently reassign dynamic DHCP addresses. Enforcing strict IP whitelists breaks workstations upon DHCP lease renewal. LAN IP is collected purely as informational telemetry for the Lab In-Charge. |
| **Handshake Anti-Spoofing** | 🟢 **TOFU (Trust-On-First-Use) Lock** | Prevents student curl/Postman spoofing: Server checks if `workstation.machine_guid` is NULL on first enrollment and binds it; once set, subsequent handshakes MUST match the stored composite GUID or return `403 Forbidden`. Reset button provided in In-Charge drawer. |
| **Visible Agent Console** | 🟢 **KEPT VISIBLE FOR TESTING** | The PowerShell agent will run with a visible console window during initial development/testing so technicians can view live execution output (`[+] MachineGuid detected`, `[+] Telemetry posted`, `[+] Browser launched`). |

---

## 3. Feasibility Analysis: Running 50+ Terminals on Supabase Free Tier

A critical constraint is ensuring 50+ lab workstations run smoothly without exhausting the **Supabase Free Tier**.

### 3.1 Quota vs. Demand Comparison

| Resource Metric | Supabase Free Limit | 50+ Terminals Demand | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **PostgreSQL Database Storage** | 500 MB | ~12 MB total for 50 workstations + hardware specs JSONB + 1 full academic year of issue tickets | 🟢 **< 3% used** |
| **Direct Connections Pool** | ~15–60 connections | **0 direct connections from client PCs**. All client requests hit Next.js server actions / API routes using connection pooler | 🟢 **Zero connection contention** |
| **Egress Bandwidth** | 5 GB / month | Handshake payload is ~800 bytes; terminal view bundle cached via Next.js; monthly usage ~180 MB | 🟢 **< 4% used** |
| **Realtime WebSockets** | 200 concurrent | Workstations load static context with standard HTTP; only live faculty cockpits connect to Realtime | 🟢 **Zero WebSocket saturation** |

### 3.2 Cardinal Rule: Zero High-Frequency Write Churn
- Many naive RMM agents write a "heartbeat ping" row to PostgreSQL every 30 seconds ($50 \times 120 \text{ writes/hr} = 144,000 \text{ rows/day} = 4.32 \text{ million rows/month}$). This would instantly exhaust disk quotas and trigger auto-pause.
- **Our Architecture**:
  - Telemetry is sent **only on agent launch** (Windows boot or desktop shortcut click).
  - PostgreSQL `INSERT` is executed **only when an actual ticket is submitted**.
  - Total writes per class session: **2 to 8 rows**.

---

## 4. End-to-End Authentication & Cryptographic Handshake Flow

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                   END-TO-END HANDSHAKE WORKFLOW                                 │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

 [ Physical Lab PC (Desktop) ]
        │
        │ 1. Student double-clicks "LabSync Student Portal" shortcut (or runs on boot)
        ▼
   [ labsync-agent.ps1 ]
        │
        │ 2. Queries WMI/CIM: $regGuid + $macAddr (compositeGuid), ComputerName, CPU, RAM, Disk C, OS
        │ 3. HTTP POST /api/terminal/handshake (Header: X-LabSync-Agent-Key)
        ▼
 [ Next.js API Gateway (/api/terminal/handshake) ]
        │
        │ 4. Validates institutional key & enforces TOFU (Trust-On-First-Use) lock:
        │    - Looks up workstation by ComputerName/label.
        │    - If workstation.machine_guid IS NULL: Binds incoming compositeGuid!
        │    - If workstation.machine_guid IS ALREADY SET: Requires exact match; if mismatch, returns 403 Forbidden!
        │    - Updates `hardware_specs` (JSONB), `lan_ip`, and `last_seen_at`.
        │    - Generates 60-second single-use ticket: HMAC-SHA256(station_id + timestamp + iat)
        │ 5. Returns: { redirect_url: "http://<server>:3000/terminal/auth?ticket=<token>" }
        ▼
   [ labsync-agent.ps1 ]
        │
        │ 6. Executes: Start-Process "http://<server>:3000/terminal/auth?ticket=<token>"
        │    (If network exception occurs, executes local diagnostic & prints offline alert box)
        ▼
 [ Default Web Browser (Edge / Chrome) ]
        │
        │ 7. GET /terminal/auth?ticket=<token>
        ▼
 [ Next.js Server Route (/terminal/auth) ]
        │
        │ 8. Verifies 1-time ticket token (checks expiry & HMAC signature)
        │ 9. Sets cryptographically signed HttpOnly Cookie:
        │    `Set-Cookie: labsync_terminal_session=<signed_jwt>; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; (Secure only on HTTPS/prod)`
        │ 10. HTTP 302 Redirect to clean URL: `/terminal` (ZERO tokens/parameters in address bar!)
        ▼
 [ Student Terminal View (/terminal) ]
        │
        │ 11. Server component verifies `labsync_terminal_session` cookie.
        │     - Loads workstation info (e.g. PC-14, Row B).
        │     - Loads currently scheduled practical & enrolled student (or session relocation).
        │     - Displays Alive-Only issue reporting tiles.
        │ 12. Student clicks "Mouse / Keyboard Fault" & submits:
        │     - Request includes HttpOnly cookie.
        │     - Server guarantees report is recorded for PC-14. No student can spoof another desk!
```

---

## 5. Hardware Telemetry Extraction Specification

The PowerShell agent (`scripts/client-agent/labsync-agent.ps1`) runs natively on Windows 10 & 11 without requiring any extra runtimes, Python, or npm modules.

### 5.1 Native WMI/CIM Commands

| Telemetry Field | Source Command | Example Extracted Value |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`machine_guid`** | `(Get-ItemProperty "HKLM:\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Cryptography").MachineGuid` | `"a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef0123456789"` |
| **`computer_name`** | `$env:COMPUTERNAME` | `"PC-14"` |
| **`cpu`** | `(Get-CimInstance Win32_Processor | Select-Object -First 1).Name.Trim()` | `"Intel(R) Core(TM) i5-10400 CPU @ 2.90GHz"` |
| **`cpu_cores`** | `(Get-CimInstance Win32_Processor | Select-Object -First 1).NumberOfCores` | `6` |
| **`ram_gb`** | `[math]::Round(((Get-CimInstance Win32_PhysicalMemory \| Measure-Object -Property Capacity -Sum).Sum / 1GB), 1)` | `16.0` |
| **`disk_c_total_gb`**| `[math]::Round(((Get-CimInstance Win32_LogicalDisk -Filter "DeviceID='C:'").Size / 1GB), 1)` | `476.9` |
| **`disk_c_free_gb`** | `[math]::Round(((Get-CimInstance Win32_LogicalDisk -Filter "DeviceID='C:'").FreeSpace / 1GB), 1)` | `184.2` |
| **`os_caption`** | `(Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem).Caption` | `"Microsoft Windows 11 Pro"` |
| **`os_build`** | `(Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem).BuildNumber` | `"22631"` |
| **`mac_address`** | `(Get-NetAdapter \| Where-Object { $_.Status -eq 'Up' -and $_.PhysicalMediaType -ne 'Unspecified' } \| Select-Object -First 1).MacAddress` | `"E4-54-E8-12-34-56"` |
| **`lan_ip`** | `(Get-NetIPAddress -AddressFamily IPv4 \| Where-Object { $_.IPAddress -notlike '127.*' -and $_.IPAddress -notlike '169.254.*' } \| Select-Object -First 1).IPAddress` | `"192.168.1.114"` |

### 5.2 JSONB Storage Schema (`public.workstations.hardware_specs`)

```json
{
  "cpu": "Intel(R) Core(TM) i5-10400 CPU @ 2.90GHz",
  "cpu_cores": 6,
  "ram_gb": 16.0,
  "disk_c_total_gb": 476.9,
  "disk_c_free_gb": 184.2,
  "disk_c_used_pct": 61.4,
  "os_caption": "Microsoft Windows 11 Pro",
  "os_build": "22631",
  "mac_address": "E4-54-E8-12-34-56",
  "lan_ip": "192.168.1.114",
  "agent_version": "1.0.0",
  "last_synced_at": "2026-10-15T10:30:00Z"
}
```

---

## 6. Database Schema Invariants & Migration

We add three columns to `public.workstations`:

```sql
-- Migration: Add hardware telemetry and machine GUID binding to workstations
ALTER TABLE public.workstations 
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS machine_guid TEXT UNIQUE,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS hardware_specs JSONB DEFAULT NULL,
  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_seen_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NULL;

-- Index for instant lookup during agent handshake
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workstations_machine_guid ON public.workstations(machine_guid);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_workstations_label_lower ON public.workstations(LOWER(label));
```

### TypeScript Model Updates (`types/database.ts` & `types/workstations.ts`)
```typescript
export interface WorkstationHardwareSpecs {
  cpu: string
  cpu_cores: number
  ram_gb: number
  disk_c_total_gb: number
  disk_c_free_gb: number
  disk_c_used_pct: number
  os_caption: string
  os_build: string
  mac_address: string
  lan_ip: string
  agent_version: string
  last_synced_at: string
}

// In WorkstationRow:
machine_guid: string | null
hardware_specs: WorkstationHardwareSpecs | null
last_seen_at: string | null
```

---

## 7. Cryptographic Security Engine (`lib/security/terminal-token.ts`)

A stateless HMAC-SHA256 token engine using `process.env.TERMINAL_SIGNING_SECRET` (falling back to institutional instance key):

```typescript
// Token payload signed with HMAC-SHA256:
interface HandshakeTicketPayload {
  stationId: string
  stationCode: string
  machineGuid: string
  expiresAt: number // epoch ms (60 seconds lifetime)
}

interface TerminalSessionPayload {
  stationId: string
  stationCode: string
  machineGuid: string
  issuedAt: number
}
```

1. **`createHandshakeTicket(workstation)`**: Generates a 60-second single-use URL token `ticket=<payload_b64>.<hmac>`.
2. **`verifyHandshakeTicket(ticket)`**: Validates HMAC signature and verifies `Date.now() < expiresAt`.
3. **`createTerminalSessionCookie(workstation)`**: Generates a tamper-proof session JWT cookie.
4. **`verifyTerminalSessionCookie(cookieValue)`**: Extracts validated workstation identity on server requests.

---

## 8. Client Agent Scripts Specification

### 8.1 `scripts/client-agent/labsync-agent.ps1`
- Runs in a visible PowerShell window.
- Displays colored terminal logs:
  - `[+] Reading Windows Hardware Telemetry...`
  - `[+] Identified Station: PC-14 (Guid: a1b2c3d4-...)`
  - `[+] Hardware: Core i5-10400, 16GB RAM, 184GB SSD Free, Windows 11 Pro`
  - `[+] Sending handshake to http://192.168.1.50:3000/api/terminal/handshake...`
  - `[+] Handshake authorized! Launching secure browser session...`
- Opens default browser using `Start-Process $redirectUrl`.
- Closes console cleanly after 3-second countdown (or leaves open if errors occur so technician can diagnose).

### 8.2 `scripts/client-agent/setup-labsync.bat`
- 1-click batch setup script executed from a USB flash drive or network share by the Lab In-Charge:
  1. Creates directory `C:\LabSync\`
  2. Copies `labsync-agent.ps1` into `C:\LabSync\`
  3. Creates a Windows Desktop Shortcut **"LabSync Student Portal"** with icon targeting `powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File C:\LabSync\labsync-agent.ps1`
  4. Optionally creates a Run entry in `HKLM:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run` to launch on student login.

---

## 9. Student Reporting Portal Redesign (`/terminal`)

### 9.1 Clean Route Structure
- **`/terminal`**: The primary student interface. Automatically reads `labsync_terminal_session` cookie. If no valid session exists, displays a high-clarity **"Terminal Unauthorized"** institutional screen directing the student to launch the desktop shortcut on their lab PC.
- **`/terminal/auth`**: Internal redirect endpoint that converts a valid 1-time handshake ticket into the HttpOnly session cookie and redirects to `/terminal`.
- **`/terminal/[code]`**: Preserved exclusively for staff preview or authorized override with teacher login.

### 9.2 Alive-Only Problem Categories (`lib/types/terminal.ts`)

```typescript
export const STUDENT_TERMINAL_ISSUES = [
  {
    id: 'peripherals',
    label: 'Mouse / Keyboard Fault',
    iconName: 'MousePointer',
    severity: 'low',
    description: 'Keys jamming, mouse clicker broken, or wheel not scrolling properly.',
  },
  {
    id: 'no_internet',
    label: 'No Internet / LAN Disconnected',
    iconName: 'WifiOff',
    severity: 'high',
    description: 'Local area network unplugged or web browser shows connection timed out.',
  },
  {
    id: 'missing_software',
    label: 'Missing Lab Software',
    iconName: 'PackageX',
    severity: 'high',
    description: 'Required subject software not installed (Turbo C++, Python, VS Code, MySQL).',
  },
  {
    id: 'app_freeze',
    label: 'Application Freeze / Crash',
    iconName: 'ZapOff',
    severity: 'medium',
    description: 'Program not responding, compiler crashing, or code editor completely frozen.',
  },
  {
    id: 'audio_issue',
    label: 'Audio / Headphone Issue',
    iconName: 'VolumeX',
    severity: 'low',
    description: 'No sound output or headphone jack not recognized during audio practical.',
  },
  {
    id: 'other',
    label: 'Other Workstation Problem',
    iconName: 'HelpCircle',
    severity: 'medium',
    description: 'Custom problem encountered on this workstation.',
  },
] as const
```

### 9.3 UI Layout & Aesthetics (`StudentTerminalView`)
- Obsidian glass theme (`#0B0F19`, `.glass-card`).
- **Station Header**: `🖥️ PC-14 • Row B` | `🟢 Hardware Verified (Win 11 Pro • 16GB RAM)`.
- **Active Session Card**: Subject title, instructor name, and assigned student name (honoring Tier 2 session relocations).
- **Issue Tiles Grid**: High-density 2x3 responsive cards with micro-animations on hover and active select ring.
- **Custom Notes**: Optional textarea for specific details.
- **Submit Action**: `<Send className="h-4 w-4" /> <span>Submit Issue Report</span>` (Strictly zero literal `+`).
- **Active Tickets List**: Shows existing open tickets on this PC so student sees their ticket is recorded.

In the Lab In-Charge Cockpit (`/computer-lab`), clicking any workstation node opens the sidebar drawer. We enhance it with a **"Hardware Telemetry & Node Diagnostics"** card:

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  🖥️ HARDWARE TELEMETRY & DIAGNOSTICS            🟢 Agent Online (2m ago)│
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤

│  • Storage:   184.2 GB Free of 476.9 GB (Drive C:) [███████░░░] 39%    │
│  • System:    Windows 11 Pro (Build 22631)                             │

```

- If `disk_c_free_gb < 15.0`, displays an amber storage badge (`⚠️ Low Disk Space`).
- If `disk_c_free_gb < 5.0`, displays a rose critical badge (`🚨 Storage Critical`).
- Directly empowers the Lab In-Charge to diagnose whether software faults (e.g. VS Code crashing, compilation failing) are caused by full disk drives or insufficient memory.

# Prototype to Production Master Roadmap & Execution Strategy

> **Document**: `doc/plans/2026-10-18-prototype-to-production-master-roadmap.md`  
> **Status**: Approved Strategy & Master Blueprint  
> **Target System**: LabSync LIMS / LMR  
> **Context**: Transitioning from prototype dev scaffolding (`localStorage`, JSON blobs, and `lib/master-data.ts` mock fallbacks) into an enterprise-grade, single-source-of-truth PostgreSQL production system.

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Forensic Diagnosis

During rapid early development, many user interfaces were wired to static fallback arrays in [`lib/master-data.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/lib/master-data.ts) and client-side caching hooks in [`hooks/use-infrastructure-state.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/hooks/use-infrastructure-state.ts) and [`hooks/use-routine-state.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/hooks/use-routine-state.ts). 

Meanwhile, a normalized PostgreSQL relational database was built with real tables:
- `public.profiles` (Teacher & Admin Accounts)
- `public.subjects` (29 Real Curriculum Subjects)
- `public.classes` (9 Grade & Cohort Batches)
- `public.class_subjects` (Junction Mapping Class $\leftrightarrow$ Subject $\leftrightarrow$ Teacher)
- `public.schedules` (Institutional Weekly Timetable)
- `public.labs` (Computer, Physics, Chemistry Labs)
- `public.issue_categories` (Workstation & Equipment Faults)

### Root Causes of Reported Visual Anomalies:
1. **Messy Schedule Teachers (`COMP12-Teacher`, `CN12-Teacher`)**:
   - `lib/master-data.ts` contained an old 1,000-line prototype array (`MASTER_ROUTINE`) with dummy names.
   - `hooks/use-routine-state.ts` cached this into `localStorage['lab_master_routine_2083_v6']`.
   - `app/(dashboard)/schedules/page.tsx` merged these mock names with real profiles, creating a cluttered dropdown.
2. **Records Subject Filter Capped at 11 (`All Subjects (11)`)**:
   - `components/records/practical-filter-bar.tsx` used `useInfrastructureState().subjects`, which fell back to the 11 hardcoded subjects in `DEFAULT_SUBJECTS`.
   - The **29 active curriculum subjects** in `public.subjects` were completely bypassed.
3. **Duplicate Teacher Bug (*Dinanath Shah*)**:
   - `app/actions/logs.ts` (`getTeachers()`) queried `SELECT id, full_name FROM profiles` without filtering `approval_status = 'approved'` and `is_active = true`.
   - Dinanath had two accounts (one `rejected` test account and one `approved` active account), causing him to appear twice. Non-teachers (Super Admin, HOD) also leaked into the list.
4. **Admin Console Split-Brain**:
   - When administrators added/edited classes or subjects in `/admin` (Tab 02 Curriculum), the code saved into serialized JSON blobs in `institution_settings` (`infrastructure_subjects`, `infrastructure_classes`) and `localStorage`, rather than updating `public.subjects` or `public.classes`.

---

## 2. The 4-Pillar Production Roadmap

```mermaid
graph TD
    P1[Pillar 1: Single Source of Truth] -->|Eliminate localStorage & master-data mocks| P2[Pillar 2: Clean Database & Admin Console Control]
    P2 -->|Admin controls all relational entities| P3[Pillar 3: Mobile-First Responsive Hardening]
    P3 -->|Rock solid on teacher smartphones| P4[Pillar 4: Production Dry-Run & Role Verification]
```

### Pillar 1: Single Source of Truth (Kill the Shadow Data Layer)
- Sever all frontend hooks from `lib/master-data.ts` fallback arrays (`DEFAULT_CLASSES`, `DEFAULT_SUBJECTS`, `MASTER_ROUTINE`, `INITIAL_FACULTY`, `LAB_ROOMS`).
- Bind every dropdown, filter, and selector directly to PostgreSQL tables via typed Server Actions.
- Evict all legacy `lmr_admin_*` and `lab_master_routine_*` cache keys from browser `localStorage`.

### Pillar 2: Clean Database & Admin Console Control
- **Full Relational Mapping**: Admin Console Tab 02 (Curriculum) directly mutates `public.subjects`, `public.classes`, and the junction table `public.class_subjects`.
- **Soft Deactivation (`is_active = false`)**: Prohibit hard deletes on entities referenced by `practical_logs`, `schedules`, or `student_enrollments` to prevent PostgreSQL foreign key crashes (`23503`) or historical attendance wipeout.
- **Server Action RBAC**: Every mutating action enforces strict server-side authorization (`super_admin`, `lab_incharge`, `hod`) with Next.js cache revalidation (`revalidatePath`).

### Pillar 3: Mobile-First Responsiveness for Practical Operations
- **`/records/new` (Session Logging)**: Single-column responsive layout with sticky bottom thumb action bar ("Save & Submit Practical Log").
- **`/computer-lab` (Live Cockpit)**: High-speed mobile workstation cards with 1-tap Present/Absent pills and Quick Relocate.
- **`/records`**: Responsive card-stack view (`hidden md:table`) eliminating 800px horizontal table scrolling.
- **`/incidents`**: Standard `<input type="file" accept="image/*" />` enabling native choice between live camera snapshot and existing gallery photo.
- **`/schedules`**: Day-by-Day swipeable tab view on mobile devices.

### Pillar 4: Role-Based Dry-Run & Production Handover
- Execute end-to-end dry-run workflows across all 4 institutional roles:
  1. **Teacher**: Schedule view &rarr; Session roll-call &rarr; Save log &rarr; Report incident.
  2. **Lab In-Charge**: Attendance ledger audit &rarr; Workstation triage &rarr; Fault resolution &rarr; Official NEB Excel export.
  3. **Head of Department (HOD)**: Departmental log audit &rarr; Incident escalations &rarr; Syllabus progress check.
  4. **Super Admin**: Curriculum management &rarr; Timetable slot reconfiguration &rarr; Multi-year cohort promotion.

---

## 3. Division of Responsibilities

| Antigravity (Core AI Assistant) | User (Institutional Admin / Product Lead) |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Code Refactoring**: Write canonical server actions, delete fallback arrays, eliminate `localStorage` shadow stores. | **Institutional Policy**: Confirm real school subjects, class names, and teacher account approvals. |
| **Database Migrations & Actions**: Implement soft-delete guards, relational junction actions (`class_subjects`), and strict RBAC guards. | **Roster Verification**: Sanity check real teacher and student names once dropdowns are rewired. |
| **Mobile UX Engineering**: Refactor responsive CSS, sticky thumb bars, card views, and mobile camera pickers. | **Physical Mobile Testing**: Open routes on a smartphone to test thumb reach and usability. |
| **Automated Verification**: Run strict TypeScript checks (`npx tsc --noEmit`) and integration test suites. | **Final Sign-off**: Perform the persona dry runs before live production deployment. |

---

## 4. Master 5-Phase Implementation Breakdown

```
Phase 1: Canonical Database Actions & Relational Mapping
   ↓ (subjects.ts, classes.ts, getTeachers sanitization, class_subjects junction actions, soft-delete guards)
Phase 2: Admin Console Unification
   ↓ (/admin Tab 02 directly controls public.subjects, public.classes, public.class_subjects)
Phase 3: Client Hooks & UI Dropdown Purge
   ↓ (Filters on /records, /schedules, and session modals rewired to live DB; COMP12-Teacher eradicated)
Phase 4: Mobile Responsiveness Pass
   ↓ (/records/new, /computer-lab, /records, /schedules, /incidents hardened for smartphones)
Phase 5: Full System Dry-Run & Production Handover
     (Role-by-role end-to-end verification, test password purge, final launch sign-off)
```

# Full System Audit & Production Readiness Roadmap

> **Document**: `doc/plans/FULL_SYSTEM_AUDIT_AND_PRODUCTION_ROADMAP.md`  
> **Type**: Architecture, Security & Operational Audit Report  
> **Status**: Comprehensive Audit Completed (Read-Only Analysis — Zero Code Modified)  
> **Target Environment**: Academic Laboratory Practical & Management System (LabSync LIMS / LMR)

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Readiness Scorecard

A full architectural, security, database, and front-end audit of the **LabSync LIMS / LMR** codebase was performed. The platform contains a mature set of academic workflows—including Class 11/12 practical logs, NEB 75% attendance compliance analytics, CAD workstation layouts, dual-date Bikram Sambat integration, and preventive maintenance routines. Strict TypeScript compilation passes with **0 type errors** (`npx tsc --noEmit`).

However, the codebase currently contains **critical security bypasses, hardcoded mock datasets, direct database auth manipulation, base64 image bloat, and browser-side split-brain state** that must be resolved prior to deploying to a live production environment.

### Production Readiness Index

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬────────┬─────────────────────────┐
│ Evaluation Dimension                                   │ Score  │ Production Status       │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────┼─────────────────────────┤
│ 1. TypeScript Strictness & Type Safety                 │ 100%   │ ✅ Ready (0 errors)     │
│ 2. Database Schema & Relational Integrity              │  85%   │ ⚠️ Minor gaps (1 un-RLS)│
│ 3. Security, Authentication & Role Governance (RBAC)   │  40%   │ 🚨 High Risk (Bypasses) │
│ 4. Single Source of Truth (Zero Hardcoded Data/Mocks)  │  45%   │ 🚨 High Risk (Mocks)    │
│ 5. Media & Asset Storage (Avatars / Incident Photos)   │  35%   │ 🚨 High Risk (Base64 DB)│
│ 6. Multi-User State Synchronization & Caching          │  50%   │ ⚠️ Split-Brain Storage  │
│ 7. Scalability & Serverless Connection Pooling         │  60%   │ ⚠️ Session Pooler Limit │
│ 8. Auditability, Disaster Recovery & Compliance        │  80%   │ 🟢 Good (Audit ledger)  │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────┴─────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 2. Critical Security Vulnerabilities (P0 — Must Fix Before Production)

### 2.1 `process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'` Root Privilege Bypasses
- **Location**:
  - [`app/actions/workstations.ts:59`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/workstations.ts#L59) (`getAuthContext`)
  - [`app/actions/maintenance.ts:150`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/maintenance.ts#L150) (`getAuthorizedProfile`)
  - [`app/actions/terminal.ts:20`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/terminal.ts#L20) (`getAuthContext`)
  - [`app/actions/maintenance-fleet.ts:25`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/maintenance-fleet.ts#L25) (`getAuthorizedProfile`)
- **Vulnerability**:
  ```typescript
  const isPrivileged =
    scope.isPrivileged ||
    scope.role === 'super_admin' ||
    scope.role === 'lab_incharge' ||
    scope.role === 'hod' ||
    process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development' // 🚨 ROOT PRIVILEGE ESCALATION
  ```
- **Impact**: Any unauthenticated request or unprivileged user account (e.g. students or unauthorized teachers) is automatically granted full administrative and lab in-charge privileges if `NODE_ENV` is set to `development` or if the runtime environment does not inject `NODE_ENV=production`.
- **Production Requirement**: Remove `process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'` from all authorization helper functions. Privileges must strictly depend on verified session tokens and `public.profiles.role`.

---

### 2.2 Direct Raw SQL Manipulation of Supabase `auth.users` & Connection Leaks
- **Location**: [`app/actions/auth.ts:156-238`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/auth.ts#L156-L238)
- **Vulnerabilities**:
  1. **Direct Raw Password Encryption in DB**: The code executes raw SQL:
     ```sql
     INSERT INTO auth.users (id, email, encrypted_password, ...)
     VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), $1, crypt($2, gen_salt('bf', 10)), ...);
     ```
     This bypasses Supabase GoTrue Auth API (`supabase.auth.admin.createUser`). If Supabase upgrades its internal authentication tables, auth triggers, or MFA columns, this query will fail or create orphaned identity rows.
  2. **Connection Leak on Failure**: Lines 157–158 open an unpooled `pg.Client()` via `await client.connect()`. If any error occurs before line 260 `await client.end()`, the client is never closed because there is no `finally` block. Under high sign-up volume or network timeouts, this exhausts PostgreSQL connections.
  3. **Department Field Persistence**: Line 198 and 248 continues to insert `'department'` into metadata and profiles during signup, conflicting with Rule #5 (zero department regressions).
- **Production Requirement**: Refactor account provisioning to use Supabase GoTrue Admin client (`createAdminClient().auth.admin.createUser()`) and ensure all raw connection handlers use pooled queries (`getPgPool().query()`) with `try...finally` resource cleanup.

---

### 2.3 Row-Level Security (RLS) Blindspots & Overly Permissive Policies
Direct inspection of PostgreSQL `pg_tables` and `pg_policies` revealed:
1. **Missing RLS**: Table `public.lab_layout_templates` has `rowsecurity = false`. It is completely unprotected by PostgreSQL RLS.
2. **Public `ALL` Permission on Critical Tables**:
   - `public.labs`: Policy `'Allow insert/update labs'` has role `{public}` and command `ALL`.
   - `public.schedules`: Policy `'Allow manage schedules'` has role `{public}` and command `ALL`.
   - **Impact**: Anyone with the public Supabase Anon key can insert, modify, or delete laboratory rooms and the entire school academic timetable via direct PostgREST calls.
3. **Public Read on Student & Workstation PII**:
   - Policies `'read_students_scoped'` and `'read_student_enrollments_scoped'` permit `{public}` read access. Unauthenticated scrapers can dump all student names, admission numbers, registration numbers, and roll calls.
- **Production Requirement**:
  - Run `ALTER TABLE public.lab_layout_templates ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;`.
  - Restrict write policies on `labs`, `schedules`, and `profiles` to `{authenticated}` with role verification (`auth.uid() IN (SELECT id FROM profiles WHERE role IN ('super_admin', 'lab_incharge'))`).
  - Restrict student roster reads to `{authenticated}` faculty members.

---

### 2.4 Middleware Fail-Open Behavior
- **Location**: [`middleware.ts:130-132`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/middleware.ts#L130-L132)
- **Vulnerability**:
  ```typescript
  } catch (error) {
    // Fail-open gracefully on transient edge network errors
    console.warn('Middleware auth verification error:', error)
  }
  return response
  ```
- **Impact**: If Supabase's Auth API has high latency or returns a 502/503 during edge middleware execution, the middleware fails open, allowing unauthenticated requests into `/admin`, `/records`, and `/maintenance`.
- **Production Requirement**: Fail closed for administrative routes (`/admin/*`), redirecting to `/login?error=auth_service_unavailable` while allowing graceful fallback only for public landing pages.

---

### 2.5 Seed Scripts with Known Default Passwords
- **Location**:
  - [`scripts/sync-demo-passwords.mjs:35-41`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/scripts/sync-demo-passwords.mjs#L35-L41)
  - [`scripts/setup-new-database.mjs:524-527`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/scripts/setup-new-database.mjs#L524-L527)
  - [`scripts/test-client-login.mjs:31-36`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/scripts/test-client-login.mjs#L31-L36)
- **Vulnerability**: Hardcoded seed passwords (`Admin@12345`, `Incharge@12345`, `Hod@12345`, `Teacher@12345`) for accounts `@rrl.edu.np`.
- **Production Requirement**: Exclude or purge these scripts from production environments. In production, the initial Super Admin account must be bootstrapped via environment variables (`ADMIN_INITIAL_EMAIL`, `ADMIN_INITIAL_PASSWORD`) and forced to change credentials upon first login.

---
