| **Command Palette Search** | [`components/command-palette.tsx:55`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/components/command-palette.tsx#L55) | `MASTER_ROUTINE.filter(...)` | Search results return fake mock classes and fake teachers. | Connect command palette to PostgreSQL search action. |
| **Notification Digest Engine** | [`app/actions/notifications.ts:85`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/notifications.ts#L85) | `MASTER_ROUTINE.filter(...)` | Daily digest emails reflect mock data rather than scheduled classes. | Query `public.schedules` where `day_of_week = todayDayOfWeek`. |

---

## 4. Architectural, Scalability & Performance Weaknesses

### 4.1 Base64 Image Storage in Transactional PostgreSQL (Database Bloat)
- **Current State**:
  - [`components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx:586`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx#L586) and [`batch-photo-import-modal.tsx`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/components/roster/batch-photo-import-modal.tsx) compress student face avatars into Base64 JPEG data URLs (`data:image/jpeg;base64,...`) and store them in `public.students.photo_url` (`TEXT`).
  - Incident damage evidence is also stored as Base64 strings in `public.lab_incidents.photo_url`.
- **Capacity Analysis & Quota Utilization**:
  - **Database Size**: `32 MB / 500 MB` (actual school data is ~3.8 MB; ~28 MB is PostgreSQL system catalogs, extensions, and WAL buffers).
  - **File Storage**: `0 / 1 GB` (completely unused 1,000 MB free object storage quota).
  - Storing 1,000 student avatars as Base64 in PostgreSQL rows inflates the database by 20–30 MB (+ TOAST & audit duplicates = 100 MB+), eating valuable database space.
  - In contrast, storing 1,000 WebP images in Supabase Storage takes only **~15 MB** in the 1 GB bucket (**1.5% of storage quota**), while the database stores only an 80-byte CDN URL (80 KB total).
- **Free Tier Lifecycle Rule (7-Day Inactivity Auto-Pause)**:
  - Supabase pauses free-tier projects after 7 consecutive days of inactivity (e.g. during extended Dashain/Tihar vacations). A periodic scheduled health check or documented 1-click restore keeps the system ready.
- **Production Solution**:
  - Configure Supabase Storage buckets: `avatars` and `incidents`.
  - Upload raw image files directly to the storage bucket via Supabase Client SDK or pre-signed URLs.
  - Store only lightweight CDN HTTPS links (e.g. `https://xyz.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/avatars/student_uuid.webp`) in PostgreSQL.
  - Run a one-time migration script `scripts/migrate-base64-to-storage.mjs` during the hardening phase.

---

### 4.2 Browser `localStorage` Split-Brain & Shadow Stores
- **Current State**:
  - Multiple client hooks maintain shadow data in `localStorage`:
    - `use-incident-state.ts` &rarr; `lmr_incidents_v2`
    - `use-logs-state.ts` &rarr; `lmr_logs_v2`
    - `use-infrastructure-state.ts` &rarr; `lmr_infrastructure_v2`
    - `lib/sync-bus.ts` &rarr; `labsync_event_*`
- **Why this fails in production**:
  1. **Multi-User Desynchronization**: In a real school with 20 teachers and 2 lab in-charges operating on different laptops and mobile devices, changes made on User A's machine are invisible to User B until a hard page reload occurs.
  2. **Stale Cache Overwrite**: Optimistic updates write directly to local storage. If a user has an outdated local storage cache, it can overwrite fresh database state.
- **Production Solution**:
  - Eliminate persistent offline shadow stores in `localStorage` for transactional records.
  - Rely on standard Server Actions with Next.js `revalidatePath` and Supabase Realtime WebSocket broadcast for live multi-user synchrony.

---

### 4.3 Database Connection Pool & Serverless Concurrency Mismatch
- **Current State**:
  - [`lib/db.ts:14`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/lib/db.ts#L14) rewrites the Supabase URL to use port `5432` on `aws-0-ap-southeast-1.pooler.supabase.com` with `new Pool({ max: 10 })`.
- **Issues**:
  1. **Port 5432 is Session Mode**: Supabase's port 5432 pooler reserves a physical PostgreSQL connection for each client session. In serverless deployments (Vercel Next.js App Router lambdas), each function invocation spins up an isolated pool, quickly hitting the database's `max_connections` limit.
  2. **Hardcoded AWS Region**: Hardcoding `aws-0-ap-southeast-1.pooler.supabase.com` causes connection failures if the Supabase project is deployed in another region (e.g. `ap-south-1` Mumbai or `us-east-1`).
- **Production Solution**:
  - Connect to port **`6543` (Transaction Mode)** for all serverless queries, allowing hundreds of concurrent serverless lambdas to multiplex over a small set of backend connections.
  - Dynamically parse the pooler host directly from the user's provided `DATABASE_URL` environment variable without hardcoded region strings.

---

### 4.4 Development Time Simulator Engine
- **Current State**:
  - [`lib/time-simulator.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/lib/time-simulator.ts) allows client-side date/time spoofing (`setSimulatedTime`, `jumpToDayInActiveWeek`, `jumpToTimeOfDay`).
- **Risk**: If accessible in production, teachers or staff could manipulate their system clock or active period, creating falsified or backdated practical attendance logs.
- **Production Solution**: Wrap the time simulator UI and storage keys in strict `process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production'` and super-admin-only debug flags.

---

## 5. Step-by-Step Strategic Production Migration Plan

```mermaid
graph TD
    A[Phase 1: Security Hardening & Zero-Trust Auth] --> B[Phase 2: Master Data Purge & Canonical DB]
    B --> C[Phase 3: Object Storage Migration]
    C --> D[Phase 4: Pooler & Serverless Tuning]
    D --> E[Phase 5: State Consolidation & Realtime]
    E --> F[Phase 6: Pre-Flight Verification & Launch]
```

### Phase 1: Security Hardening & Zero-Trust Authentication
1. **Remove Development Privilege Bypasses**:
   - In `app/actions/workstations.ts`, `maintenance.ts`, `terminal.ts`, and `maintenance-fleet.ts`, remove `process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'` from `isPrivileged` calculations.
2. **Refactor Account Creation**:
   - In `app/actions/auth.ts`, replace raw SQL `crypt()` insertion into `auth.users` with the official Supabase GoTrue Admin API:
     ```typescript
     const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.createUser({
       email,
       password,
       email_confirm: true,
       user_metadata: { full_name: fullName, role },
     })
     ```
   - Ensure all database pool queries use `try...finally` to eliminate connection leaks.
3. **Audit & Seal PostgreSQL RLS Policies**:
   - Enable RLS on `lab_layout_templates`.
   - Remove `{public}` `ALL` policies on `labs` and `schedules`; restrict mutations strictly to `{authenticated}` users with `super_admin` or `lab_incharge` roles.
   - Restrict student roster queries so only authenticated institutional staff can read student details.
4. **Harden Edge Middleware**:
   - In `middleware.ts`, change catch-block behavior from fail-open to fail-closed on protected administrative paths.

---

### Phase 2: Master Data Purge & Canonical Database Alignment
1. **Deprecate `MASTER_ROUTINE` and `INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS`**:
   - Replace all imports of `MASTER_ROUTINE` in `command-palette.tsx`, `notifications.ts`, `schedules.ts`, and hooks with direct PostgreSQL queries against `public.schedules`.
   - Replace `INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS` and `PROFILE_TEACHER_MAPPING` with dynamic queries linking `profiles` to `class_subjects` and `schedules`.
2. **Universal Dynamic Academic Year Resolution**:
   - Eliminate hardcoded `'2083'` defaults across `class-roster-view.tsx`, `seat-planner-tab.tsx`, `computer-lab-workspace.tsx`, `cohort-promotion-wizard.tsx`, and `institutional-reports.ts`.
   - Implement a shared server helper / React context:
     ```typescript
     export async function getActiveAcademicYear(): Promise<string> {
       const res = await pool.query(`SELECT id FROM public.academic_years WHERE is_current = true LIMIT 1;`)
       return res.rows[0]?.id || '2083'
     }
     ```
3. **Universal Dynamic Lab Resolution**:
   - Eliminate hardcoded `'comp'` defaults and ternary chains across `records`, `logs`, `workstations`, and `incidents`.
   - Query `public.labs` where `is_active = true` to populate all facility selectors.
4. **Centralize Institutional Metadata**:
   - Resolve the discrepancy between "Ratna Rajya Laxmi" and "Radha Raman".
   - Create a database table `public.institution_settings` or use typed environment variables (`NEXT_PUBLIC_INSTITUTION_NAME`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_INSTITUTION_LOCATION`) to populate all letterheads and export signature blocks.

---

### Phase 3: Media & Object Storage Migration
1. **Provision Supabase Storage Buckets**:
   - Create buckets: `avatars` (public read, authenticated write) and `incident-photos` (private read, authenticated write).
2. **Refactor Upload Modals**:
   - Modify `components/roster/batch-photo-import-modal.tsx` and individual photo uploaders:
     - Compress images on client to WebP format.
     - Upload to `avatars/students/<student_id>.webp`.
     - Save the resulting public CDN URL in `public.students.photo_url`.
3. **Data Migration Script**:
   - Write a one-time migration script `scripts/migrate-base64-to-storage.mjs` to extract any existing Base64 strings from `students.photo_url` and upload them to Supabase Storage, updating the database with HTTPS URLs.

---

### Phase 4: Infrastructure & Connection Pooling Tuning
1. **Switch to Transaction Mode Pooler (Port 6543)**:
   - Update `lib/db.ts` to connect to Supabase port `6543` for high-concurrency serverless execution.
   - Remove the hardcoded `aws-0-ap-southeast-1` regex replacement; parse host and credentials directly from the verified environment connection string.
2. **Optimize Pool Configuration**:
   - Set pool parameters appropriate for serverless lambdas:
     ```typescript
     new Pool({
       connectionString,
       ssl: { rejectUnauthorized: false },
       max: 2, // Low max per lambda to prevent pooling explosion
       idleTimeoutMillis: 5000,
       connectionTimeoutMillis: 5000,
     })
     ```
3. **Shield Development Time Simulator**:
   - Guard `lib/time-simulator.ts` so that in production (`NODE_ENV === 'production'`), `getEffectiveDate()` always returns `new Date()`.

---

### Phase 5: Client-Server State Consolidation
1. **Purge `localStorage` Shadow Databases**:
   - Remove `localStorage` writes and reads from `use-logs-state.ts`, `use-incident-state.ts`, and `use-infrastructure-state.ts`.
2. **Leverage Next.js Server Components & Realtime Broadcast**:
   - Fetch authoritative data in React Server Components or via SWR/TanStack Query with cache keys.
   - Rely on `broadcastSync` over Supabase Realtime channel for instant cross-tab and cross-device synchronization.

---

### Phase 6: Pre-Flight Production Verification Checklist

```
[ ] 1. Clean Staging Database Migration
       - Execute supabase/schema.sql from scratch on a clean database instance.
       - Verify all 25 tables and RLS policies are applied with zero errors.

[ ] 2. Zero-Trust Access Verification
       - Attempt unauthenticated access to /admin, /records, /maintenance — verify redirection.
       - Attempt role escalation via API requests — verify 403 Forbidden.

[ ] 3. Automated Test Suite Execution
       - Run all test suites in tests/*.test.mjs with test environment credentials.
       - Verify 100% pass rate.

[ ] 4. Real Email Dispatch Verification
       - Configure production SMTP (Google Workspace / Amazon SES).
       - Test OTP verification, password reset, and daily schedule digest delivery.

[ ] 5. Official Board Export Verification
       - Generate NEB 75% Board Compliance Excel report (.xlsx) with 100+ sample students.
       - Verify 3-tier signature blocks and formatting in ExcelJS.

[ ] 6. Production Environment Variables Sealed
       - Verify NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL and NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY.
       - Verify SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is kept secure on server only.
       - Verify DATABASE_URL points to Transaction Pooler (port 6543).
```

---

## 6. Conclusion & Recommendation

The LabSync LIMS / LMR application has a rock-solid foundation, excellent TypeScript hygiene, and deep institutional domain features. By systematically executing the **5-phase production roadmap**—eliminating root privilege bypasses, retiring static mock arrays in favor of live PostgreSQL queries, migrating images to Supabase Storage, and configuring transaction connection pooling—the system will be fully enterprise-grade, secure, and ready for campus deployment.

# Reminder Plan: Admin Console Dedicated Options & Template Management

> **Initiative Context**: Captured from user feedback on October 8, 2026.
> **Goal**: Replace hardcoded system options (such as quick maintenance templates in `Image 4` and fault categories in `Image 5`) with dedicated, dynamic configuration consoles in the Super Admin workbench (`/admin`). Administrators can add, edit, rename, archive, or re-order operational taxonomies without modifying TypeScript code or triggering migrations.
> **Execution Status**: Scheduled as a dedicated separate initiative after Computer Lab Floor Plan & Layout Editing completion.

---

## 1. Targeted Feature Areas Identified

### A. Quick Maintenance Routine Templates (`Image 4`)
* **Current Behavior**: Hardcoded template buttons:
  - `PC Thermal (180d)`
  - `BIOS Firmware (120d)`
  - `PC Deep (90d)`
  - `Lab Software (60d)`
* **Future Admin Console Capability**:
  - In `/admin` &rarr; **Tab 03 (Maintenance Routines)**, add a dedicated **"Preset Template Manager"** section.
  - Form fields: Template Title, Default Cadence (`daily`, `weekly`, `monthly`, `quarterly`, `semester`, `yearly`), Cadence Days (`30d`, `60d`, `90d`, `120d`, `180d`), Category, and Checklist Items.
  - Actions: **Add Template**, **Edit Template**, **Delete / Soft-Archive Template**.
  - Storage: Store in a new table `public.maintenance_routine_templates` (or JSONB in institution settings).
  - Rule #1 Guard: Never render literal `+` in template buttons (always use `<Plus className="..." />`).

### B. Hardware & Software Fault Categories (`Image 5`)
* **Current Behavior**: Categories rendered in the workstation issue drawer (`WorkstationIssueDrawer`) and station quick popover:
  - `Mouse / Keyboard unresponsive` (`medium`)
  - `No display / Monitor won't turn on` (`high`)
  - `OS Crash / Blue Screen` (`high`)
  - `Loose Network Cable / No Internet` (`medium`)
* **Future Admin Console Capability**:
  - In `/admin` &rarr; **Facility & Workstation Settings**, add a dedicated **"Fault Category Registry"** table.
  - Backed by live table `public.issue_categories` (id, name, default_severity, icon_name, is_active, display_order).
  - Actions:
    - **Add New Fault Category**: Name, default severity (`low`, `medium`, `high`, `critical`), suggested icon.
    - **Rename / Edit Existing**: Seamlessly update title and severity without breaking past incident links.
    - **Soft-Archive**: Disable obsolete categories (`is_active = false`) to preserve historical audit trail.
    - **Drag-to-Reorder**: Adjust display order shown to teachers in the quick popover.

### C. System-Wide Dropdown & Taxonomy Management
* **General Pattern**: Eliminate hardcoded select options across all administrative workflows:
  - Subject stream types and class grade levels.
  - Incident severity escalation rules and HOD notification triggers.
  - Lab facility room types and active lifecycle statuses.

---

## 2. Technical Implementation Architecture

1. **Database Schema**:
   - Verify `public.issue_categories` CRUD actions in `app/actions/workstations.ts` are wired to the UI.
   - Create `public.maintenance_routine_templates` table:
     ```sql
     CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.maintenance_routine_templates (
       id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
       title text NOT NULL,
       cadence text NOT NULL,
       cadence_days integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 90,
       category text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'preventive',
       checklist jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
       is_active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
       created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
     );
     ```
2. **Server Actions**:
   - `getMaintenanceTemplates()`, `createMaintenanceTemplate()`, `updateMaintenanceTemplate()`, `deleteMaintenanceTemplate()`.
   - `getIssueCategories()`, `createIssueCategory()`, `updateIssueCategory()`, `archiveIssueCategory()`.
3. **Admin Console Views**:
   - Sub-panel in `/admin` with modal dialogues for creating and editing templates and categories.
   - Live synchronization across client workspaces via `revalidatePath`.

   - Update layouts across `components/computer-lab/`, `components/terminal/`, `components/maintenance/`, and `components/layout/`.

   - ⚠️ **Rule #10**: Zero double-nested card-in-card containers.
   - ⚠️ **Rule #13**: Pure RPC server action exports in `app/actions/*`.
   - ⚠️ **Rule #19**: Workstation status dynamically derived from issues, not static DB columns.
6. **Execute Automated Test Validation**:
   - Run the complete 6-suite test pipeline:
     ```bash
     node --experimental-strip-types --import ./tests/register-hooks.mjs --test \
       tests/phase1-student-terminal.test.mjs \
       tests/phase2-auto-grid-and-projector.test.mjs \
       tests/phase3-seating-and-forensics.test.mjs \
       tests/phase4-fleet-maintenance.test.mjs \
     ```

# LabSync LIMS — Unified Roster Lifecycle & Archival Architecture
## Master Engineering Specification & Implementation Plan (Version 2.1 — Final Production Blueprint)
*Synthesized from Enterprise SIS / ERP Architectural Principles (Gemini, Claude & Antigravity)*

> **Document Version**: 2.1 (Production Blueprint — Final Polish)  
> **Target Environment**: LabSync LIMS (LMR) — Academic Laboratory Management System  
> **Institutional Scope**: Secondary & Higher Secondary Education (Class 11 & Class 12, NEB Curriculum, Nepal)  
> **The Golden Rule**: *LabSync is a Laboratory Execution System, not the Master School ERP. Never overwrite or delete a real identity; only transition status and state on top of permanent UUIDs.*

---

## 1. The Unified Architectural Doctrine

### 1.1 The Golden Boundary: LIMS vs. Master School ERP
```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    CENTRAL SCHOOL ERP                        │
│   (Fee Billing, Master Admissions, Official Transcripts)     │
│   • Single Source of Truth for Admissions & Promotion        │
└──────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘
                               │ Confirmed Class Export (.xlsx)
                               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    LABSYNC LIMS                              │
│    (Workstation CAD Floor, Practical Logbook, Roll-Call,     │
│     NEB 75% Lab Exam Compliance, Chemical Safety Register)   │
│    • Execution & Auditing Engine for Practical Sessions      │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
* **The Master School ERP is the authoritative single source of truth** for who passed, who failed, who paid fees, and who was admitted.
* LabSync LIMS **does not reinvent** central school admissions, billing, or complex promotional rules.
* When school administration finalizes a class roster, the Lab In-Charge simply exports the confirmed spreadsheet from the ERP and ingests it into LabSync via **Smart Sync**.

---

### 1.2 The One Governing Principle: Decoupled Identity vs. Annual Enrollment
Enterprise Student Information Systems (SIS) strictly decouple the permanent human being from their temporal academic standing:

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 1. THE PERMANENT IDENTITY (public.students)                                            │
│    • Surrogate Primary Key: id (UUID, system-generated, immutable, permanent).         │
│    • Business Identifier: admission_no (Human ID, UNIQUE constraint, editable).        │
│    • Person Attributes: full_name, gender, dob.                                        │
│    • Invariant: Created once upon school admission; NEVER reset on academic year change.│
└───────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                            │ 1 : N (One student, many annual years)
                                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 2. THE ANNUAL ENROLLMENT (public.student_enrollments)                                  │
│    • Foreign Key: student_id (UUID references public.students.id).                     │
│    • Academic Cycle: academic_year_id ('2083', '2084'), class_id ('11sci', '12eng').   │
│    • Positional Label: roll_number (Derived alphabetical A-Z integer, NOT an ID).      │
│    • Lifecycle State: status ('active' | 'archived' | 'transferred' | 'completed').    │
└───────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                            │ 1 : N (One enrollment, many practical logs)
                                            ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 3. LABORATORY AUDIT TRAIL (public.log_attendance, workstation_seat_plans)              │
│    • Foreign Key: enrollment_id (UUID references public.student_enrollments.id).       │
│    • Database Defense: ON DELETE RESTRICT (Database forbids deletion if logs exist).   │
│    • Invariant: Name edits or roll shifts NEVER orphan or break past practical logs.   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### 1.3 Core Architectural Invariants
1. **Surrogate UUID vs. Business Admission Number**:
   - `public.students.id` is the internal UUID. All downstream relations (`student_enrollments`, `log_attendance`, `workstation_seat_plans`) reference this UUID.
   - `admission_no` is a human business identifier (e.g. `ADM-00101` or the ERP student ID). It carries a `UNIQUE` constraint in `public.students`, is fully editable if mistyped, and never cascades.
   - **Zero Year-Coded Synthetic Formats**: The system forbids synthesizing transient strings like `ADM-2083-12ENG-01`. An admission number belongs to the human being, not to a grade or year.
2. **Roll Number as a Derived Positional Label**:
   - `roll_number` is purely an integer positional label ($1 \dots N$) derived by sorting the active class list alphabetically (A–Z).
   - It is stored **only** on `public.student_enrollments` and is never used as a foreign key anywhere in the database.
3. **Automatic Archival via Invariant**:
   - Transitioning to a new academic year (e.g. 2084) creates new enrollments for that year. The untouched 2083 enrollments automatically become historical archives. Past attendance logs remain permanently readable and printable under Year `2083`.

---

## 2. Ten Real-World Architectural Guardrails (v2.1)

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                 CORE ARCHITECTURAL GUARDRAILS                                   │
├────┬──────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 01 │ Tiered Smart Sync Matching       │ Bucket 1 (admission_no) vs Bucket 2 (Ambiguous Name)    │
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 02 │ Contextual Disambiguation Table  │ Side-by-side comparison (DOB, Gender, History) for B2   │
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 03 │ Pre-Flight Collision Defense     │ Friendly UI interception for duplicate admission_no     │
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 04 │ Workstation Seat Release Warning │ Explicit physical PC count warning when archiving missing│
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 05 │ Pre-Save Roll Collision Check    │ Friendly toast if manual roll edit collides with student│
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 06 │ Latecomer Escape Hatch           │ Single-row Edit Student modal remains unlocked mid-year │
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 07 │ Mid-Year Archival Roll Gap Lock  │ Never shift remaining rolls down when a student leaves  │
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 08 │ Active (Unseated) Restore State  │ Restored students get prominent [ Assign PC ] prompt   │
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 09 │ Detailed JSONB Audit Receipts    │ Forensic JSONB payload logged on Danger Zone wipe       │
├────┼──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 10 │ Principal Audit Ledger UI        │ Consumption table in /admin Tab 04 parsing JSONB logs   │
└────┴──────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### Guardrail 1: Smart Sync Split Match (Duplicate Name Hazard Defense)
* In Nepali schools, duplicate names (e.g., two different students named "Bibek Thapa" in Class 12) are very common.
* **Bucket 1 (Auto-Confirmed)**: Matched by permanent `admission_no` &rarr; 100% safe to merge/update in-place automatically.
* **Bucket 2 (Manual Review)**: Matched by `full_name` only &rarr; Flagged in amber with mandatory human choices:
  1. **"Confirm Link & Update"**: Confirms this row is a spelling correction or known occupant.
  2. **"Create as New Student"**: Confirms this is a distinct student who happens to share the same name (creates a fresh `students` UUID).
* Prevents silent duplicate-name identity corruption.

---

### Guardrail 2: Disambiguating Bucket 2 (The Side-by-Side Context Table)
* To prevent admins from guessing blindly when reviewing a name match in Bucket 2, each card renders a side-by-side contextual comparison table:

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ INCOMING SPREADSHEET ROW             │ EXISTING DATABASE OCCUPANT                              │
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Full Name:    Bibek Thapa            │ Full Name:    Bibek Thapa                               │
│ Admission No: ADM-00109              │ Admission No: ADM-00084                                 │
│ Gender:       Male                   │ Gender:       Male                                      │
│ DOB:          2062-04-12 B.S.        │ DOB:          2061-11-28 B.S.                           │
│ Prior Grade:  —                      │ Status:       Archived (Class 11 Eng, 2082 B.S.)        │
└──────────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
* With DOB, Gender, and prior academic history exposed side-by-side, the administrator can confidently click **"Yes, Link/Merge"** or **"No, Create New"**.

---

### Guardrail 3: Pre-Flight `admission_no` Collision Interception
* If an Excel file or edit form attempts to assign an admission number that already belongs to another student UUID in the database, the server intercepts it before PostgreSQL crashes:
  > ⚠️ **Conflict Detected**: Admission Number `ADM-00102` is already assigned to **"Sandesh Acharya"** (UUID: `...`). Resolve this collision in your file or assign a distinct admission number before committing.

---

### Guardrail 4: Workstation Seat Release Warning
* When archiving missing students in Bucket 4 (students in DB but missing from the uploaded file), the UI explicitly warns:
  > ⚠️ **Workstation Seat Release Warning**:  
  > Archiving these **3 missing students** will permanently preserve their practical logs, but will **immediately release 3 assigned physical workstation seats** (PC-04, PC-12, PC-19) on the CAD workstation floor.

---

### Guardrail 5: Pre-Save Roll Collision Check (Manual Roll Editing)
* When an admin manually edits a single student to assign a roll number (e.g. mid-year latecomer), the server action performs a pre-save check against `UNIQUE (academic_year_id, class_id, roll_number)`:
  * If Roll 15 is entered, but Roll 15 is already occupied by "Chirag Shrestha", the server intercepts it and returns a clean, friendly message:  
    `"Roll 15 is already occupied by Chirag Shrestha. Please choose an empty roll number."`
  * Completely eliminates raw PostgreSQL 500 constraint crashes.

---

### Guardrail 6: Roll Number Governance — The Latecomer Escape Hatch
* While bulk **"Sort Rolls (A–Z)"** strictly locks once practical logs exist, the **Single-Row "Edit Student" Modal** remains permanently unlocked.
* Admins can always manually assign an empty roll number (e.g. Roll 41) to a mid-year latecomer without renumbering or shifting the rest of the class.

---

### Guardrail 7: Mid-Year Archival Roll Gap Invariant
* When student Roll 15 leaves school mid-year and is archived, their roll number stays frozen on their archived record.
* We do **not** shift Rolls 16–40 down, preserving physical paper logbook and signed attendance register continuity.

---

### Guardrail 8: Archival Lifecycle — The "Active, Unseated" State on Restore
* Restoring an archived student sets their enrollment status back to `'active'`, but because their seat was vacated, assigns them **"Active (Unseated)"**.
* The Live Lab Cockpit displays an amber badge: `[ Unseated — Click to Assign Workstation ]`, opening the CAD floor plan seat assigner.

---

### Guardrail 9: Danger Zone — Detailed Structured JSONB Audit Receipts
* When a Super Admin executes a cascading class wipe under **Precaution Active**, the action logs an exhaustive JSONB payload directly into `public.audit_ledger`:
  ```json
  {
    "action": "WIPE_COHORT_DANGER_ZONE",
    "entity_type": "cohort",
    "entity_id": "2083-12eng",
    "actor_id": "9b1deb4d-3b7d-4bad-9bdd-2b0d7b3dcb6d",
    "actor_name": "System Administrator",
    "metadata": {
      "class_id": "12eng",
      "class_name": "12 Eng",
      "academic_year_id": "2083",
      "practical_logs_deleted": 142,
      "attendance_records_deleted": 3124,
      "workstation_seats_freed": 24,
      "session_seat_overrides_deleted": 18,
      "enrollments_deleted": 35,
      "orphaned_students_deleted": 0,
      "confirmation_phrase_typed": "WIPE 12 ENG",
      "reason": "Administrative cohort wipe of corrupted test data prior to ERP sync"
    }
  }
  ```

---

### Guardrail 10: Principal's Audit Ledger Consumption UI
* In the Super Admin `/admin` Console (Tab 04 - System Maintenance), an interactive **Audit Trail & Ledger** data table parses the JSONB payloads into clean, human-readable columns:
  * **Timestamp** (`2026-09-26 19:30:15`)
  * **Actor** (`Admin Aarav Sharma`)
  * **Action** (`WIPE_COHORT_DANGER_ZONE`)
  * **Details** (`Wiped Class 12 Eng (2083): 105 logs, 24 seats, 22 enrollments`)
  * **Inspection Modal**: 1-click JSON inspector for forensic deep-dives by the school Principal or auditor.

---

## 3. Database Invariants & Structural Safeguards

### 3.1 Database-Enforced Defense: `ON DELETE RESTRICT`
To guarantee that software bugs, script errors, or accidental button clicks cannot delete students with active practical records:
* The foreign key from `public.log_attendance` to `public.student_enrollments` strictly enforces:
  ```sql
  ALTER TABLE public.log_attendance
    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS log_attendance_enrollment_id_fkey,
    ADD CONSTRAINT log_attendance_enrollment_id_fkey
      FOREIGN KEY (enrollment_id)
      REFERENCES public.student_enrollments(id)
      ON DELETE RESTRICT;
  ```
* **Guarantee**: If a student has even 1 attendance record in a practical session, PostgreSQL will reject any `DELETE` statement at the database engine level. Deletion is physically impossible until an authorized administrator explicitly purges the session logs in the Danger Zone.

---

## 4. Concrete Implementation Blueprint

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              FIVE CORE IMPLEMENTATION MODULES                          │
├───────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Module 1: Roster UI Tabs      │ • Active | Archived | All segmented filter tabs       │
│ & Archive Browser             │ • "Restore to Active" action with roll conflict check  │
│                               │ • Automatic workstation seat release on archival       │
│                               │ • "Active, Unseated" status badge & CAD seat prompt    │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Module 2: Danger Zone         │ • Super Admin "Wipe Class Data" under Precaution Active │
│ Class Purge Tool              │ • Cascading test data obliteration with typed confirm  │
│                               │ • Comprehensive structured JSONB audit ledger receipt  │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Module 3: Smart Sync          │ • Tiered Match: Bucket 1 (admission_no) vs Bucket 2    │
│ ERP Reconciliation            │ • Side-by-side comparison table (DOB, Gender, Class)   │
│                               │ • Pre-flight collision warning & Seat Release warning  │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Module 4: Roll Governance     │ • 1-click "Sort Rolls Alphabetically (A–Z)" utility    │
│ & Latecomer Escape Hatch      │ • Atomic renumbering 1..N when practical logs = 0      │
│                               │ • Pre-save duplicate roll collision check on edit      │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Module 5: Principal Audit     │ • Read-only Audit Trail & Ledger table in /admin Tab 04 │
│ Ledger Consumption UI         │ • Parsed human-readable columns & JSONB inspector modal│
└───────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### Module 1: Roster UI Segmented Tabs & Universal Archival Browser
* **Files**: `components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx`, `app/actions/students.ts`
* **Changes**:
  1. In `app/actions/students.ts`, update `getStudentsByClass`:
     ```typescript
     export async function getStudentsByClass(params: {
       classId: string
       academicYearId?: string
       statusFilter?: 'active' | 'archived' | 'all'
     })
     ```
     - `statusFilter === 'active'` (default): queries `status = 'active'`.
     - `statusFilter === 'archived'`: queries `status IN ('archived', 'transferred', 'completed')`.
     - `statusFilter === 'all'`: queries all enrollments regardless of status.
  2. In `components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx`:
     - Add a clean segmented tab control above the table:
       `[ Active (22) ]` `[ Archived (3) ]` `[ All (25) ]`.
     - In the **Archived** tab:
       - Displays student names, original roll numbers, admission numbers, and archive reason/status badge.
       - Each row provides a **"Restore to Active"** button (`<RotateCcw className="h-3 w-3" />`).
       - Allows full CSV and Excel export of historical/archived student records.
  3. **Workstation Seat Release on Archival**:
     - When an enrollment's status transitions to `'archived'`, execute:
       ```typescript
       await supabase.from('workstation_seat_plans').delete().eq('enrollment_id', enrollmentId)
       ```
     - This immediately frees their physical desk (e.g. PC-15) on the CAD workstation floor for new incoming students.
  4. **The "Active, Unseated" State on Restore**:
     - When restored, the student has no row in `workstation_seat_plans`.
     - Render an amber status pill: `Active (Unseated)`.
     - Provide an action button: `[ Assign PC ]` opening the CAD floor plan seat assigner.

---

### Module 2: Super Admin "Wipe Class Data" Danger Zone
* **Files**: `app/actions/admin-maintenance.ts`, `components/admin/admin-console.tsx`
* **Changes**:
  1. Create Server Action `wipeClassCohortDataAction`:
     ```typescript
     export async function wipeClassCohortDataAction(params: {
       classId: string
       academicYearId: string
       confirmationPhrase: string
     }): Promise<{ success: boolean; deletedCounts?: Record<string, number>; error?: string }>
     ```
     - Strictly guards execution to `super_admin` role.
     - Validates that `confirmationPhrase` exactly equals `WIPE <CLASS_NAME>`.
     - Executes a single, atomic PostgreSQL transaction:
       1. Deletes `log_attendance` records attached to logs of that class/year.
       2. Deletes `practical_logs` for that class/year.
       3. Deletes `session_seat_overrides` and `workstation_seat_plans` for that class/year.
       4. Deletes `student_enrollments` for that class/year.
       5. Deletes orphaned `students` records that have zero enrollments remaining in any year.
       6. Writes an exhaustive JSONB receipt to `public.audit_ledger` (Guardrail 9).
  2. Embed in the Super Admin Console under Tab 04 (Database & System Maintenance), guarded by the **Precaution Active** safety switch.

---

### Module 3: Smart Sync ERP Reconciliation Ingestion Engine
* **Files**: `lib/roster-ingestion/validator.ts`, `components/roster/roster-import-modal.tsx`
* **Changes**:
  1. **Tiered Match Ladder**:
     - Match primarily by `admission_no` &rarr; **Bucket 1 (Auto-Confirmed)**.
     - Match by `full_name` only &rarr; **Bucket 2 (Manual Review)** with side-by-side contextual table rendering DOB, Gender, and prior class (Guardrail 2).
  2. **Pre-Flight `admission_no` Collision Interception**:
     - Pre-checks whether incoming admission numbers are already occupied by another student UUID in PostgreSQL, displaying an inline warning banner instead of crashing on commit (Guardrail 3).
  3. **Workstation Seat Release Warning**:
     - In **Bucket 4 (Unmatched in File)**, clearly warn the user:
       *"Archiving these N students will immediately release N assigned physical workstation seats."* (Guardrail 4).

---

### Module 4: Alphabetical A-Z Roll Re-Sequencing Engine
* **Files**: `app/actions/students.ts`, `components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx`
* **Changes**:
  1. Create Server Action `resequenceClassRollsAlphabeticallyAction`:
     ```typescript
     export async function resequenceClassRollsAlphabeticallyAction(params: {
       classId: string
       academicYearId: string
     }): Promise<{ success: boolean; count?: number; error?: string }>
     ```
     - Verifies that `COUNT(log_attendance) === 0` for this class in this academic year.
     - If practical logs exist: rejects renumbering with error:  
       `"Cannot renumber rolls: Active practical logs have already been recorded and signed for this cohort."`
     - If 0 logs: queries all active enrollments, sorts by `full_name ASC`, and updates `roll_number = 1, 2, 3... N` in an atomic transaction.
  2. In `components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx`:
     - Add a **"Sort Rolls (A–Z)"** button in the header toolbar.
     - Enabled when 0 practical logs exist; cleanly disabled with a descriptive tooltip once sessions begin.
  3. **Latecomer Escape Hatch & Pre-Save Roll Collision Check**:
     - In `updateStudentDetailsAction`, check if the requested roll number is already occupied by another student in that cohort.
     - If occupied: surfaces friendly UI toast: `"Roll 15 is already occupied by [Name]. Please choose an empty roll number."` (Guardrail 5 & 6).

---

### Module 5: Principal's Audit Ledger Consumption UI
* **Files**: `app/actions/audit.ts`, `components/admin/admin-console.tsx`
* **Changes**:
  1. Create Server Action `getAuditLedgerAction`:
     ```typescript
     export async function getAuditLedgerAction(params?: {
       limit?: number
       entityType?: string
     }): Promise<{ success: boolean; data?: AuditLedgerRow[]; error?: string }>
     ```
  2. In `components/admin/admin-console.tsx` (Tab 04 - System Maintenance):
     - Embed the read-only **Audit Trail & Ledger** data table.
     - Columns: `Timestamp`, `Actor`, `Action`, `Target Entity`, `Summary Details`.
     - Clicking any row opens a clean JSON Inspector modal revealing the full `metadata` diff (Guardrail 10).

---

## 5. Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs)

### SOP A: Academic Year Rollover (Transitioning 2083 &rarr; 2084)
1. **End of Year**: Super Admin creates the new Academic Year `2084 B.S.` in Settings and sets it as active.
2. **ERP Finalization**: School administration finalizes passing lists, repeaters, and new admissions in the central School ERP.
3. **ERP Export**: Admin exports the confirmed Class 10/11/12 roster from the School ERP (already sorted alphabetically with permanent admission numbers).
4. **LabSync Ingestion**:
   - In LabSync, select Academic Year `2084 B.S.`.
   - Open Class Roster &rarr; Click **"Import Excel"**.
   - Upload the ERP export.
   - LabSync automatically:
     - Recognizes returning students by `admission_no` and creates their 2084 enrollments.
     - Creates new identities for fresh admissions.
     - Assigns the ERP's alphabetical roll numbers.
   - 2083 enrollments and logs remain 100% intact as historical archives.

### SOP B: Mid-Term Student Departure & Seat Release
1. A student leaves or transfers mid-semester.
2. In Class Roster View, locate the student &rarr; click **Delete / Remove**.
3. LabSync automatically:
   - Sets enrollment status to `'archived'` (preserving past practical attendance for board inspection).
   - Deletes their `workstation_seat_plans` entry, immediately freeing their lab PC.
   - Preserves their roll number on the archived record (does NOT shift remaining students down).
   - The student moves to the **"Archived"** tab and disappears from the active roll-call cockpit.

### SOP C: Purging a Mistaken Test Cohort
1. You tested the system with dummy students and fake logs in `12 Eng`.
2. Navigate to `/admin` &rarr; Tab 04 (System Maintenance).
3. Toggle **Precaution Active** to unlock the Danger Zone.
4. Select Class `12 Eng` & Year `2083` &rarr; Click **"Wipe Class Data"**.
5. Type `WIPE 12 ENG` to confirm.
6. The test logs, seat plans, and dummy enrollments are cleanly obliterated.
7. An exhaustive JSONB receipt is written to `public.audit_ledger`.
8. Clean Slate mode is now 100% unlocked to ingest the true class roster.

---

## 6. Verification & Quality Assurance Gate

Prior to deployment, the system must satisfy the following checklist:
* [ ] **TypeScript Check**: `npx tsc --noEmit` passes with **0 errors**.
* [ ] **Test Suite Parity**: `npm test` passes all 21 test suites and 163+ unit/relational tests.
* [ ] **Relational Constraint Test**: Verifies that deleting an enrollment with active logs is strictly rejected by the database via `ON DELETE RESTRICT`.
* [ ] **Zero Data Loss Verification**: Verifies that editing names via Smart Sync preserves all existing `log_attendance` records.
* [ ] **Roll Collision Test**: Verifies that editing a student to an occupied roll number triggers a clean validation message instead of a database crash.
* [ ] **Disambiguation Context Test**: Verifies that Bucket 2 cards render DOB, gender, and past class status side-by-side.
* [ ] **Audit Trail Ledger Test**: Verifies that the Principal's Audit Ledger in `/admin` correctly renders and parses `WIPE_COHORT_DANGER_ZONE` receipts.

# Roster & Cohort Lifecycle Promotion Wizard — Specification & Plan

> **Document**: `doc/plans/ROSTER_PROMOTION_WIZARD_SPEC.md`  
> **Target Tab**: `/admin` &rarr; Tab "Roster & Cohort Lifecycle"  
> **Scope**: All Grades & Classes in LabSync LIMS  
> **Status**: Approved Blueprint  

---

## 1. Core Objectives
1. **Interactive Class Promotion**: Enable administrators to transition student cohorts from any source class/year (e.g. 11 Science 2083) to a target class/year (e.g. 12 Science 2084).
2. **Exception Handling**:
   - **Promote** &rarr; Enrolls student into target class with new roll number.
   - **Repeat** &rarr; Retains student in the same grade for the new academic year.
   - **Transferred / Left** &rarr; Marks student enrollment as `transferred`; does not create a new enrollment.
   - **Graduated** &rarr; For terminal grades (Class 12), marks students as `graduated`.
   - **New Comers (Lateral Admissions)** &rarr; Allows adding newly enrolled students into the target roster.
3. **Roll Number Numbering Engine**:
   - 1-click **Auto-Assign Alphabetical (A-Z by student full name)** to generate sequential roll numbers `1, 2, 3...`.
   - Editable numeric input field for every student row for manual adjustments.
4. **Safety & Disaster Recovery**:
   - Guarded by the system's `"Precaution Active"` toggle.
   - Atomic database transactions.
   - Batch rollback support recorded in `audit_ledger`.

---

## 2. Component Architecture

```
components/admin/roster-lifecycle/
├── roster-lifecycle-tab.tsx          # Main container inside /admin
├── cohort-promotion-wizard.tsx       # 3-step promotion workflow container
├── promotion-student-table.tsx       # Live interactive staging grid with action dropdowns & roll inputs
├── lateral-entry-modal.tsx           # Modal for adding new students to the upcoming cohort
└── batch-rollback-drawer.tsx         # Audit & reversal drawer for undoing accidental promotions
```

---

## 3. Server Actions & Backend Invariants (`app/actions/academic-years.ts`)

1. `getStudentsByClass({ classId, academicYearId, statusFilter: 'active' })`:
   - Single canonical query from `app/actions/students.ts` joining `student_enrollments` and `students` with workstation metadata.
2. `executeCohortPromotionBatch(payload)`:
   - Validates `"Precaution Active"` authorization.
   - Runs in a PostgreSQL transaction:
     - Sets source enrollments to `'archived'`, `'transferred'`, or `'graduated'`.
     - Creates new enrollments for target year/class with specified roll numbers.
     - Logs operation in `public.audit_ledger` with batch metadata.
3. `rollbackPromotionBatch(batchId)`:
   - Reverts new target enrollments and restores source enrollments to `'active'`.

---

## 4. User Approved Architecture & Decisions
1. **Repeater Lifecycle (A1)**:
   - When a student repeats a grade, their previous year enrollment is marked `'archived'`, and a new enrollment is created for the target academic year in the **same class** with a new roll number.
2. **Roll Number Allocation (A2)**:
   - **Unified Alphabetical Sort**: Sorts ALL students (promoted, repeaters, and newcomers) together alphabetically (A to Z by student full name) and assigns sequential rolls `1, 2, 3...`.
   - Every row provides an inline editable input field for manual override when necessary.
3. **Layer 2 Dynamic Database Binding (A3)**:
   - **Single Source of Truth**: Query `public.classes` directly. Hardcoded static arrays are eliminated.
   - **State Caching**: Classes fetched once at the layout/page level and distributed down to rosters and dropdowns.
   - **Strict Empty State**: If `public.classes` is empty, render: *"System unconfigured. Please add classes in the Admin Dashboard to continue."* with an action button to navigate to Curriculum &rarr; Classes.
4. **Disaster Recovery & Safety**:
   - Guarded by `"Precaution Active"` safety toggle in Admin.
   - Every promotion batch logs an audit entry in `public.audit_logs` with a unique `batch_id`.
   - **24-Hour Rollback Drawer**: 1-click revert reverses newly created enrollments and restores previous ones to `'active'`.
   - **Terminal Classes (Class 12)**: Provides a "Mark as Graduated" action (`status = 'graduated'`, archived, no destination class needed).

# Real-World School ERP Database Architecture & Student Lifecycle Guide

> **Target Audience**: LabSync LIMS (LMR) Engineering & Administration  
> **Status**: Canonical Reference Guide  
> **Location**: `doc/plans/SCHOOL_ERP_DATABASE_ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md`  

---

## 1. The Core Philosophy: Identity vs. Temporal Enrollment

In amateur database designs, student tables often have:
```sql
-- ❌ BAD DESIGN (Breaks historical data)
CREATE TABLE students (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    name TEXT,
    grade INT,       -- e.g. 9
    roll_number INT  -- e.g. 14
);
```
**Why this fails:** When a student passes to Grade 10, changing `grade = 10` and `roll_number = 12` destroys their Grade 9 attendance, report cards, practical logs, and exam history.

### The Enterprise SIS Solution: 2 Decoupled Tables
```
┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
│  public.students (Permanent Person)  │
├──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ id (UUID, permanent)                 │
│ admission_no (Permanent School ID)   │
│ full_name, gender, dob               │
│ created_at                           │
└──────────────────┬───────────────────┘
                   │ 1 : Many
                   ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────┐
│ public.student_enrollments (Annual)  │
├──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ id (UUID, per academic year)         │
│ student_id (FK -> students.id)       │
│ academic_year_id ('2083', '2084')    │
│ class_id ('11sci', '12sci')          │
│ roll_number (Roll in THAT year)      │
│ status ('active' | 'archived' | ...) │
└──────────────────────────────────────┘
```

- **`students`** is created **once** upon admission and is never deleted.
- **`student_enrollments`** creates a new row **every year**. 
- A student who spends 2 years in school has **1 student record** and **2 enrollment records**.

---

## 2. How Everything Links Together (The Relational Web)

```
[ Academic Year ] (e.g. 2083/84)
       │
       ├──► [ Classes ] (e.g. Grade 11 Science)
       │         │
       │         ├──► [ Class Subjects ] (Physics, Chemistry, Computer)
       │         │           │
       │         │           └──► [ Timetable / Schedules ] ◄── [ Teachers ]
       │         │
       │         └──► [ Student Enrollments ] ◄── [ Students (Identity) ]
       │                     │
       │                     ├──► [ Roll Attendance / Practical Logs ]
       │                     └──► [ Workstation Seat Plans ]
```

1. **Subjects**: `subjects` contains master subjects (e.g., Computer Science, Physics).
2. **Class Curriculum**: `class_subjects` connects subjects to classes (`class_id = '11sci'`, `subject = 'Computer'`).
3. **Teacher Assignment**: `schedules` links `(teacher_id, class_subject_id, lab_id, day_of_week, time_slot)`.
4. **Student Daily Operations**: Practical logs and attendance point to `enrollment_id`. If someone asks "Who was present on 2083-05-12 in Grade 11?", the system looks at the 2083 enrollment record, preserving their 2083 roll number forever.

---

## 3. How Upgrades / Promotions Actually Work (The 3 Cases)

At the end of an academic year (e.g., transitioning from Year 2083 to Year 2084):

### Case A: Standard Promotion (Passed)
- Student enrolled in Grade 9 (2083) passes.
- Old enrollment (2083) marked `status = 'completed'` or `'archived'`.
- New enrollment row inserted: `student_id = Same UUID`, `class_id = 'Grade 10'`, `academic_year_id = '2084'`, `status = 'active'`.

### Case B: Repeating the Grade (Failed / Retained)
- Student does not meet promotion criteria.
- Old enrollment (2083) marked `status = 'archived'`.
- New enrollment row inserted: `student_id = Same UUID`, `class_id = 'Grade 9'` (Same grade!), `academic_year_id = '2084'`, `status = 'active'`.

### Case C: Leaving School (Transfer Certificate / Dropout / Graduated)
- Student moves to another school or graduates Class 12.
- Old enrollment marked `status = 'transferred'` or `'graduated'`.
- **No new enrollment row is created** in Year 2084.
- Their permanent student record remains for historical transcripts, certificates, and logbook audits.

### Case D: New Comer (Lateral Entry)
- A new student joins Grade 10 in Year 2084.
- Create new row in `public.students` (new UUID + admission number).
- Create new row in `public.student_enrollments` (`academic_year_id = '2084'`, `class_id = 'Grade 10'`).

---

## 4. Manual Admin Workflow in a School ERP

The admin doesn't update SQL directly. They follow a standard 5-step end-of-year wizard:

```
[ Step 1: Create Academic Year ]
Admin defines Year 2084 (Start: Baishakh 1, End: Chaitra 30).
                  │
                  ▼
[ Step 2: Open Promotion Wizard ]
Select Source Year (2083) and Source Class (Grade 9).
Select Target Year (2084) and Target Class (Grade 10).
                  │
                  ▼
[ Step 3: Flag the Exceptions (Grid with Dropdowns) ]
By default, all 40 students are pre-selected as "Promote to Grade 10".
Admin changes:
• Roll 07 -> "Repeat Grade 9"
• Roll 18 -> "Left School (TC Issued)"
• Roll 25 -> "Promote to Grade 10"
                  │
                  ▼
[ Step 4: Atomic Execution Batch ]
Admin clicks "Execute Cohort Promotion".
In a single database transaction:
- Existing 2083 enrollments set to 'archived'.
- New 2084 enrollments created for all active/repeating students.
                  │
                  ▼
[ Step 5: Roll Number Allocation & Roster Finalization ]
Admin clicks "Auto-Assign Roll Numbers" (alphabetical A-Z by full name)
or enters custom roll numbers for the new class.
```

---

## 5. Implementation in LabSync LIMS (LMR)

In LMR, this architecture is already backed by:
- `public.students` (UUID, `admission_no`, `full_name`)
- `public.student_enrollments` (Constraint: `status IN ('active', 'transferred', 'graduated', 'archived', 'completed')`)
- `app/actions/academic-years.ts` (`promoteCohort()`, `enrollStudent()`)
- `lib/types/students.ts`

# System Integrity Audit & Core Redesign Roadmap

> **Document**: `doc/plans/SYSTEM_INTEGRITY_AUDIT_AND_REDESIGN_ROADMAP.md`  
> **Status**: Approved Master Architectural Roadmap  
> **Target**: Unified Academic Year, Dynamic Classes, and Roster Lifecycle  

---

## 1. Audit Findings: The Disconnect Between Specs & Code

| Area | Stated Architecture | Actual Code State | Required Fix |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Academic Years UI** | Admins manage cycles & switch current year | Server actions exist in `academic-years.ts`, but are called 0 times in UI | Build dedicated Academic Year Hub in `/admin` (View, Create, Switch Active) |
| **Class Rosters** | Dynamic multi-grade classes from Postgres | `class-roster-view.tsx` uses hardcoded `AVAILABLE_CLASSES` array | Query `public.classes` dynamically via server action |
| **Cohort Promotion** | Interactive wizard with exceptions & rolls | `cohort-promotion-modal.tsx` was orphaned and never wired to tabs | Build full visual Wizard with A-Z rolls, repeat/transfer/graduate, and rollback |
| **Safety & Rollback** | Reversible batch transitions with audit trail | Partial database transactions, no UI rollback interface | Add `audit_ledger` batch tracking and 1-click Revert card |

---

## 2. The 3-Phase Redesign Execution Order

### Phase 1: Academic Year Management Hub (Foundation)
- **Location**: `/admin` &rarr; Tab "Curriculum" or "Operations" (Dedicated Sub-tab: **Academic Years**).
- **Features**:
  - List all academic cycles (e.g. `2081/82`, `2082/83`, `2083/84`).
  - Active cycle indicator (`is_current = true`).
  - 1-click action: **"Set as Active Academic Year"** (atomic switch).
  - Modal: **"Create New Academic Year"** with Nepali BS date range picker.

### Phase 2: Dynamic Classes & Curriculum Unification
- Purge static `AVAILABLE_CLASSES` from `components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx`.
- Connect all dropdowns across Roster, Routine, and Attendance directly to `public.classes`.

### Phase 3: Cohort Promotion Wizard & Disaster Recovery
- Build `components/admin/roster-lifecycle/cohort-promotion-wizard.tsx`.
- Wire into `/admin` both as a dedicated subtab and as an action button in Class Rosters.
- Terminal grade support: Class 12 &rarr; **Mark as Graduated**.
- Auto-assign rolls alphabetically (A-Z) with inline numeric edit overrides.
- Bottom card: **"Recent Promotions & Rollback"** (1-click revert within 24 hours).

# System Loopholes, Logical Flaws & Post-Feature Remediation Register

> **Document**: `doc/plans/SYSTEM_LOOPHOLES_LOGICAL_FLAWS_AND_REMEDIATION_REGISTER.md`  
> **Status**: Comprehensive Inventory Compiled — Deferred for Post-Feature Hardening Phase  
> **Target Institution**: Ratna Rajya Laxmi Secondary School (Gaindakot-10, Pitauji, Nawalpur)  
> **Platform**: LabSync LIMS / LMR

---

## 1. Executive Summary

During an in-depth audit across the database schemas, server actions, client hooks, and UI interfaces of the **LabSync LIMS** platform, multiple edge-case loopholes, logical flaws, UI/UX gaps, and security risks were identified.

Per institutional guidance, **active feature development remains the top priority**. This register serves as the authoritative, permanent inventory of all identified loopholes and architectural gaps to be systematically remediated once all core feature capabilities are complete.

---

## 2. Timetable, Schedules & Session Lifecycle Flaws

### 2.1 Stale Merged End Time & Title Duplication *(RESOLVED in Task 24)*
- **Loophole**: Merging two consecutive 1-period slots (e.g. `CHEM-12` Period 1 at `10:10 - 11:00` and Period 2 at `11:00 - 11:45`) updated `span = 2`, but left `end_time` at `'11:00'`. Titles were concatenated as `CHEM-12 - Chemistry Lab & CHEM-12 - Chemistry Lab`.
- **Status**: **RESOLVED** via [Task 24](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/doc/tasks/2026-10-11-task-24-schedule-merge-end-time-and-title-deduplication.md).
- **Fix Applied**: `mergeSchedules` now computes and updates `end_time = '11:45'`. Automatic deduplication cleans identical titles. `unmergeSchedule` restores the deleted secondary slot atomically from `metadata.original_parts`.

### 2.2 Teacher Substitution Local vs Multi-Device Synchrony
- **Loophole**: When a proxy instructor is assigned via `SessionActionModal`, `useSubstitutionState` records the substitution in `localStorage` (`lmr_substitutions_v2`) with optimistic broadcast. If another faculty member logs in from a different laptop, they may not immediately see that they are assigned as proxy unless a server query syncs it.
- **Remediation**: Migrate proxy assignments into a dedicated PostgreSQL table `public.teacher_substitutions` with Realtime subscription.

### 2.3 Facility Under Maintenance Server Guard
- **Loophole**: While the UI disables booking slots in rooms marked `'Under Maintenance'`, a direct API call to `createSchedule` must enforce a server-side check querying `public.labs.status` and reject any insert if `status !== 'Operational'`.
- **Remediation**: Add explicit check constraint validation in `createSchedule` and `submitLiveSession`.

---

## 3. Security, Authentication & Role Governance (RBAC) Loopholes

### 3.1 `process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'` Root Privilege Escalation
- **Location**:
  - [`app/actions/workstations.ts:59`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/workstations.ts#L59) (`getAuthContext`)
  - [`app/actions/maintenance.ts:150`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/maintenance.ts#L150) (`getAuthorizedProfile`)
  - [`app/actions/terminal.ts:20`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/terminal.ts#L20) (`getAuthContext`)
  - [`app/actions/maintenance-fleet.ts:25`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/maintenance-fleet.ts#L25) (`getAuthorizedProfile`)
- **Loophole**:
  ```typescript
  const isPrivileged =
    scope.isPrivileged ||
    scope.role === 'super_admin' ||
    scope.role === 'lab_incharge' ||
    scope.role === 'hod' ||
    process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development' // 🚨 ROOT PRIVILEGE ESCALATION
  ```
- **Risk**: Any unauthenticated request or unprivileged user is automatically granted full Super Admin privileges if `NODE_ENV` is unset or development.
- **Remediation**: Remove `process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'` from all authorization checks; verify strictly against `public.profiles.role`.

### 3.2 Direct Raw SQL Password Hashing & Connection Leaks in `app/actions/auth.ts`
- **Location**: [`app/actions/auth.ts:156-238`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/auth.ts#L156-L238)
- **Loophole**:
  - Account provisioning executes raw SQL: `INSERT INTO auth.users ... crypt($2, gen_salt('bf', 10))` directly touching Supabase internal authentication tables.
  - Opens unpooled `pg.Client()` without a `finally` block, risking connection exhaustion under error scenarios.
  - Inserts legacy `'department'` field during signup, violating Rule #5.
- **Remediation**: Refactor to official Supabase GoTrue Admin API (`createAdminClient().auth.admin.createUser()`) with `try...finally` connection pool safety.

### 3.3 Row-Level Security (RLS) Policy Gaps
- **Location**: PostgreSQL `pg_policies`
- **Loophole**:
  - `public.lab_layout_templates` has RLS disabled (`rowsecurity = false`).
  - `public.labs` and `public.schedules` have public `ALL` policies permitting anonymous modifications via Supabase Anon keys.
  - Student roster read policies permit `{public}` read access.
- **Remediation**: Enable RLS on `lab_layout_templates`, restrict mutations to `{authenticated}` staff with `super_admin`/`lab_incharge` roles, and seal student roster queries to authenticated faculty.

### 3.4 Middleware Fail-Open Behavior
- **Location**: [`middleware.ts:130-132`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/middleware.ts#L130-L132)
- **Loophole**: Catches auth errors and continues instead of redirecting to login on administrative routes (`/admin/*`).
- **Remediation**: Fail closed for `/admin/*` routes while maintaining graceful fallbacks for public landing pages.

---

## 4. UI, UX & Data Synchronization Flaws

### 4.1 Mock Datasets & Hardcoded Arrays (`MASTER_ROUTINE`)
- **Location**: [`lib/master-data.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/lib/master-data.ts)
- **Loophole**: `MASTER_ROUTINE` contains ~1,000 lines of mock schedules. If a fresh database has no schedules or a new semester begins, components like Command Palette search (`command-palette.tsx`) and notification digests (`notifications.ts`) fall back to static mock classes instead of live database state.
- **Remediation**: Purge static mock schedule arrays; query `public.schedules` dynamically.

### 4.2 Hardcoded Academic Year String `'2083'`
- **Location**: Over 20 files (`class-roster-view.tsx`, `seat-planner-tab.tsx`, `cohort-promotion-wizard.tsx`, `schedules.ts`, `institutional-reports.ts`)
- **Loophole**: Year `'2083'` is hardcoded as fallback. When the academic year advances to `2084`, UI components will revert to 2083 if not explicitly specified.
- **Remediation**: Centralize active academic year resolution to a server helper querying `SELECT id FROM academic_years WHERE is_current = true`.

### 4.3 Multi-Device `localStorage` Split-Brain
- **Location**: `use-logs-state.ts` (`lmr_logs_v2`), `use-incident-state.ts` (`lmr_incidents_v2`), `use-infrastructure-state.ts` (`lmr_infrastructure_v2`)
- **Loophole**: Persisting operational records in client `localStorage` creates split-brain state between different laptops/tablets used by instructors and lab in-charges.
- **Remediation**: Transition to server-first data fetching via React Server Components and SWR/TanStack Query with Supabase Realtime broadcast.

### 4.4 Development Time Simulator Visibility
- **Location**: [`lib/time-simulator.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/lib/time-simulator.ts)
- **Loophole**: Allows spoofing system time and days. In production, this must be restricted exclusively to Super Admin debug mode to prevent falsified or backdated practical attendance logs.
- **Remediation**: Guard simulator controls behind `process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production'` or `role === 'super_admin'`.

---

## 5. Media & Database Bloat Loopholes

### 5.1 Base64 Image Storage in Transactional PostgreSQL
- **Location**: `components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx:586`, `public.students.photo_url`, `public.lab_incidents.photo_url`
- **Loophole**: Student face avatars and incident damage photos are compressed as Base64 JPEG data URLs (`data:image/jpeg;base64,...`) and stored directly in PostgreSQL `TEXT` columns.
- **Risk**: Inflates row sizes by ~33%, drastically balloons database backups, increases WAL traffic, and risks hitting Next.js Server Action 1MB payload limits.

### 5.2 Supabase Free Tier Capacity Breakdown & Storage Quota Optimization
- **Backend Baseline Metrics (Audited October 2026)**:
  - **Database Size**: `32 MB / 500 MB` (6.4% used). Actual institutional data is only ~3.8 MB; the remaining ~28 MB consists of PostgreSQL system catalogs (`pg_catalog`), extensions (`pgcrypto`, `supabase_realtime`), and WAL buffer allocation.
  - **File Storage**: `0 / 1 GB` (0% used). The platform currently utilizes zero object storage, completely ignoring the 1,000 MB free bucket allocation while burdening the 500 MB transactional database.
  - **Monthly Active Users**: `6 / 50,000` (negligible).
  - **Egress**: `0.23 / 5 GB` (4.6% used).
- **Mathematical Impact Analysis (1,000 Students + Incidents)**:
  - *Scenario A: Retaining Base64 in PostgreSQL*:
    - 1,000 avatars × ~20 KB = ~20–30 MB raw text.
    - PostgreSQL TOAST tables, WAL duplication, audit logs, and incident photos push database consumption to **100 MB+**, rapidly consuming 20%+ of the database limit.
    - Every roster query (`SELECT * FROM students`) must transfer megabytes of image strings over the wire, causing latency and GPU/RAM lag on classroom devices.
  - *Scenario B: Migrating to Supabase Object Storage (Phase 3)*:
    - 1,000 avatars stored as WebP files in `avatars` bucket = **~15 MB total** in Supabase Storage (**only 1.5% of the 1,000 MB free quota**).
    - Database `photo_url` column stores only an 80-character public CDN URL (`https://xyz.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/avatars/students/<uuid>.webp`), consuming just **80 KB in PostgreSQL**.
    - Browser caches avatar assets automatically (`Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000`), reducing egress and eliminating database query bloat.

### 5.3 Supabase Free Tier Lifecycle: 7-Day Inactivity Auto-Pause Guard
- **Free Tier Policy**: Supabase automatically pauses inactive free-tier projects if they receive **zero API/database requests for 7 consecutive days**.
- **Institutional Operational Impact**:
  - During extended institutional breaks (e.g. 15-day Bada Dashain / Tihar recess or winter vacation), zero school traffic could cause the Supabase project to enter a paused state.
  - When school resumes, the project requires a manual "Restore Project" action in the Supabase dashboard (~1–2 minutes).
- **Remediation & Defense**:
  - Deploy a lightweight, automated scheduled health-check ping (e.g. a free GitHub Action cron job or Vercel cron hitting `/api/health` once every 72 hours) to maintain active status during school breaks.
  - Document the simple 1-click restore procedure in the administrator disaster recovery guide.

### 5.4 Object Storage Architecture & Phased Implementation Plan
1. **Bucket Configuration**:
   - Bucket `avatars`: Public read access, authenticated write for staff/admin.
   - Bucket `incidents`: Public or signed read access, authenticated write for incident reporters.
2. **Client-Side Direct Upload Flow**:
   - Refactor `components/roster/batch-photo-import-modal.tsx`:
     - Client compresses cropped image to WebP (`image/webp`, quality 0.82, max dimensions 256×256 px).
     - Directly uploads file to Supabase Storage: `supabase.storage.from('avatars').upload(`students/${studentId}.webp`, file, { upsert: true })`.
     - Updates database record with the resulting public CDN URL.
3. **Automated Migration Script (`scripts/migrate-base64-to-storage.mjs`)**:
   - A one-time script will query all existing students and incidents where `photo_url LIKE 'data:image%'`.
   - Converts each Base64 payload into a binary Buffer.
   - Uploads to the respective storage bucket.
   - Rewrites the database `photo_url` column with the permanent HTTPS CDN link.

---

## 6. Multi-Tenant Institutional Configuration Standard

### 6.1 Centralized Multi-Client Standard (`lib/institution.ts`)
- **Official Institution**: **Ratna Rajya Laxmi Secondary School**
- **Official Campus Location**: **Gaindakot-10, Pitauji, Nawalpur, Gandaki Province, Nepal**
- **Architecture**: Centralized in [`lib/institution.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/lib/institution.ts) with full support for environment variable overrides (`NEXT_PUBLIC_INSTITUTION_NAME`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_INSTITUTION_LOCATION`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_INSTITUTION_CODE`), ensuring zero hardcoded school names in mailers or exports and seamless adaptability for multiple institutional clients in the future.

---

## 7. Execution Checklist for Post-Feature Hardening Phase

```
[ ] Phase 1: Security & RBAC Hardening
```

# Task Log: Layer 2 Dynamic Classes & Layer 3 Cohort Promotion Wizard

> **Date**: 2026-10-02  
> **Target Subsystem**: SIS Roster Lifecycle & Admin Curriculum  
> **Status**: Complete & Validated  
> **Author**: Antigravity  

---

## 1. Overview & Objectives
Replaced all hardcoded `AVAILABLE_CLASSES` mock arrays with a pure dynamic database architecture backed by `public.classes`, and built the full visual **Cohort Promotion & Lifecycle Wizard** with atomic transactions and 24-hour disaster recovery.

---

## 2. Key Accomplishments

### Layer 2: Pure Dynamic Database Classes Binding
- **Direct PostgreSQL Query**: Exported `getClassesAction()` in [`app/actions/classes.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/classes.ts) returning strongly typed `ClassRecord[]` ordered by grade descending and name ascending.
- **Eliminated Hardcoded Arrays**: Completely purged `AVAILABLE_CLASSES` mock data from [`components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/components/roster/class-roster-view.tsx).
- **Strict Empty State Alert**: If `classes.length === 0`, renders:
  > *"System Unconfigured: No active classes found in the database. Please add classes in the Admin Dashboard under Curriculum to activate the student roster."*
- **Toolbar Promotion Trigger**: Added `<GraduationCap className="h-4 w-4" /> <span>Cohort Promotion</span>` button to the class roster toolbar.

- **Unified Student Query**: Reuses the canonical `getStudentsByClass` in [`app/actions/students.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/students.ts) to eliminate duplicate queries and ensure zero shadow data fetching.
- **Atomic Server Actions** in [`app/actions/academic-years.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/academic-years.ts):
  - `executeCohortPromotionBatchAction`: Runs atomic PostgreSQL transaction (`BEGIN ... COMMIT / ROLLBACK`).
    - **Repeater Lifecycle (A1)**: Old year enrollment set to `'archived'`, new active enrollment created in the **same class** for the new academic year.
    - **Terminal Class Graduation**: Class 12 marked as `'graduated'`, archived with zero destination class requirement.
    - **Lateral Entries (Newcomers)**: Inserts new students into `public.students` and assigns target enrollments.
    - **Audit Log Trail**: Logs unique `batchId` to `public.audit_logs` with snapshot diffs.
  - `getRecentPromotionBatchesAction`: Fetches batches from the last 48 hours.
  - `rollbackCohortPromotionBatchAction`: 1-click batch reversal restoring source enrollments to active and removing target enrollments.
- **Visual Wizard Interface** in [`components/admin/cohort-promotion-wizard.tsx`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/components/admin/cohort-promotion-wizard.tsx):
  - **Step 1: Setup**: Source/target academic cycles and classes with Class 12 Terminal Graduation switch.
  - **Step 2: Staging Grid**:
    - **Unified Alphabetical Sort (A2)**: 1-click A-Z sort across all students (promoted, repeaters, newcomers) re-sequencing rolls 1, 2, 3...
    - **Inline Editable Roll Inputs**: Allows administrators to fine-tune any roll number manually.
    - **Add Lateral Student Modal**: Enrolls newcomers directly into the upcoming cohort.
    - **Bulk Actions**: Fast 1-click marking.
  - **Step 3: Pre-Flight Review**: High-density KPI cards and safety guard.
  - **24-Hour Rollback Drawer**: Displays recent batches with 1-click reversal.
- **Admin Tab Integration** in [`app/(dashboard)/admin/page.tsx`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/(dashboard)/admin/page.tsx):
  - Added dedicated sub-tab: `[ Academic Years | Subjects | Periods | Classes | Routine | Rosters | 🎓 Cohort Promotion ]`.
  - Guarded strictly by the `"Precaution Active"` safety toggle.

# Task Log: Next.js Font Network Timeout Resolution

- **Date**: 2026-10-02
- **Author**: Antigravity Assistant & User
- **Status**: Completed & Verified
- **Parent Plan**: [SYSTEM_INTEGRITY_AUDIT_AND_REDESIGN_ROADMAP.md](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/doc/plans/SYSTEM_INTEGRITY_AUDIT_AND_REDESIGN_ROADMAP.md)

---

## 1. Issue Identified
The terminal running `next dev` (Process 21852) was continually logging resource connection timeouts:
```
⚠ [next]/internal/font/google/inter_1da50b95.module.css
Warning: Error while requesting resource
Connection timed out when requesting https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@100..900&display=swap
```
This was caused by Turbopack attempting to fetch Google Fonts stylesheets over the network on the server-side during SSR. Because server-side network requests to Google Fonts API timed out, it logged warnings on every request and stalled page compilation by over 1 second per request.

---

## 2. Changes Made
1. **[app/layout.tsx](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/layout.tsx)**:
   - Removed blocking `next/font/google` server-side fetch calls.
   - Replaced with preconnected `<link rel="stylesheet">` in `<head>` loading Inter, Plus Jakarta Sans, Noto Sans Devanagari, JetBrains Mono, and Outfit directly in the browser via Google CDN.
2. **[app/globals.css](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/globals.css)**:
   - Updated `--font-display`, `--font-heading`, `--font-sans`, `--font-devanagari`, and `--font-mono` to declare robust font stacks with instant system-ui fallbacks.

---

## 3. Results & Invariants
- Zero server-side font network timeouts in Turbopack / Next dev.
- Terminal is clean and quiet.
- Page rendering latency dropped by ~1,000ms.
- Passed `npx tsc --noEmit` with 0 errors.
