# Task 41: Automated Systematic Regression Testing, Pure Unit Invariants & Playwright E2E Harness

**Date**: 2026-10-15  
**Subsystems**: `tests/`, `package.json`, `playwright.config.ts`, `tests/e2e/`, `tests/unit-domain-invariants.test.mjs`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`  
**Status**: Completed  
**Type Check**: Verified with `npx tsc --noEmit` (0 errors)

---

## 1. Context & Architectural Challenge

Prior to this task, the repository contained 40 test files and dozens of standalone scripts in `scripts/`, but suffered from critical structural flaws:
1. **Shared Database Concurrency Collisions**: When `npm test` ran, Node.js executed all test files concurrently. Tests modifying academic years or student enrollments clobbered concurrent queries against the shared Supabase PostgreSQL instance, causing false-negative assertion failures.
2. **20 Orphaned Test Suites**: Half of all test files (such as `cad-floor-architect.test.mjs`, `holiday-and-recess-guard.test.mjs`, `terminal-agent-handshake.test.mjs`) were never executed by `npm test`.
3. **Outdated Schema Assertions**: Early test suites still queried `public.workstations` for `Teacher-01`, which had been decoupled into `public.lab_floor_elements` with `element_type = 'teacher_desk'` during Phase 6 CAD Architect refactoring.
4. **Missing Browser E2E Regressions**: While `@playwright/test` was installed, zero configuration or browser specs existed to protect CAD canvas interactions and live cockpit workflows.
5. **Absence of CI/CD Automation**: No GitHub Actions pipeline existed to guard against regressions on commits.

---

## 2. Engineering Solutions Implemented

### A. Test Execution Stabilization & Database Collision Defense
- **Enforced `--test-concurrency=1`**: Configured sequential execution in `package.json` for all database-backed integration test suites to eliminate transient state contamination.
- **Synchronized Schema Invariant in `tests/workstation-floor.test.mjs`**: Updated teacher desk assertion to query `public.lab_floor_elements` for `element_type = 'teacher_desk'`. Verified that all 3 tests pass cleanly.

### B. Tier 1: Pure Domain Invariants Unit Suite (`tests/unit-domain-invariants.test.mjs`)
Created an ultra-fast, zero-database, in-memory unit suite testing core mathematical and cryptographic algorithms:
- **Timetable Conflict Engine (`lib/scheduling/conflict-engine.ts`)**: Validated `getSlotRange`, `slotRangesOverlap`, and `timeRangesOverlap` across edge-touching, multi-slot, and disjoint periods.
- **Calendar Policy Engine (`lib/calendar-policy.ts`)**: Verified Saturday Statutory Weekend recess, Bada Dashain gazetted holiday detection, and standard weekday operational pass-through.
- **Cryptographic Terminal Tokens (`lib/security/terminal-token.ts`)**: Validated stateless HMAC-SHA256 handshake ticket minting, tamper rejection, and session token verification.
- **CAD Floor Math (`lib/workstations/layout-engine.ts`)**: Validated 2% magnetic grid coordinate snapping, clamp bounds (2% to 98%), and natural label sorting (PC-01 ... PC-10).
- **Performance**: 47 unit tests pass in under 15 milliseconds.

### C. Playwright Browser E2E Regression Harness (`playwright.config.ts` & `tests/e2e/`)
- **Configured Playwright**: Created `playwright.config.ts` targeting `baseURL: http://localhost:3000` with 1 worker to ensure database isolation.
- **Implemented E2E Spec (`tests/e2e/cockpit-and-navigation.spec.ts`)**:
  - CAD Floor Plan & Live Cockpit: Verifies viewport mount, living workstation nodes, and zero illegal literal `+` buttons.
  - Institutional Navigation Hub: Verifies `/schedules`, `/maintenance`, `/incidents`, and `/print/records` render with zero 500 crashes.
  - Student Terminal Gateway: Verifies `/terminal/PC-01` renders station identity and pairing interface cleanly.
- **Execution Result**: All 3 Playwright tests passed on Chromium.

### D. Package.json Script Organization & GitHub Actions CI
- **Structured Test Scripts**:
  - `npm run test:unit`: Lightning-fast pure in-memory invariant tests.
  - `npm run test:e2e`: Headless Playwright browser test suite.
  - `npm run test:terminal`: Real-world student terminal test suite.
  - `npm run test:stress`: Extreme high-concurrency system stress benchmark.
  - `npm test`: Canonical sequential regression runner covering all 38 test suites.
- **GitHub Actions Pipeline (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`)**: Automates `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npm run lint`, and `npm run test:unit` on every push and pull request.

---

## 3. Telemetry & Verification Matrix

| Test Suite / Tier | Command | Execution Time | Result |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Tier 1: Pure Unit Invariants** | `npm run test:unit` | ~1.1s | **47 / 47 Passed (100%)** |
| **Tier 2: Workstation Floor Regression** | `node ... tests/workstation-floor.test.mjs` | ~1.9s | **3 / 3 Passed (100%)** |
| **Tier 3: Extreme Concurrency Benchmark** | `npm run test:stress` | ~2.5m | **1,000 / 1,000 Requests Passed (0 Errors)** |
| **Tier 4: Playwright Browser E2E** | `npm run test:e2e` | ~2.4m | **3 / 3 E2E Specs Passed (100%)** |
| **Static Type Check** | `npx tsc --noEmit` | ~18s | **0 Errors** |

# Task 43: Enterprise Security, RBAC & Forensic Integrity Audit

**Date**: 2026-10-15  
**Auditor**: Antigravity Enterprise Forensic Agent  
**Goal**: Conduct an exhaustive, zero-code-modification internal security and integrity verification of the entire LabSync LIMS platform, documenting critical privilege escalation vectors, cryptographic threats, RLS leakages, and regulatory compliance risks in a CISO-grade `.md` audit report.

---

## 1. Scope & Execution Methodology

- **Target Systems**:
  - PostgreSQL Row-Level Security (RLS) on all 33 public tables via live information schema introspection.
  - Server Actions exported RPC endpoints (`app/actions/*.ts`) across authorization, input injection, and scope checks.
  - Terminal Handshake & Cryptographic Token Engine (`lib/security/terminal-token.ts`, `app/api/terminal/handshake/route.ts`, `app/terminal/auth/route.ts`).
  - Regulatory 75% practical attendance compliance calculations and academic dual sign-off validity.
  - Database referential integrity, foreign key cascading deletion rules, and connection pooler concurrency.
- **Constraints**:
  - 100% read-only audit posture on production code (zero code changes to application files during discovery).
  - Telemetry gathered using localized scratch introspection scripts.

---

## 2. Key Findings Discovered

### A. Critical Severity (CVSS 8.8 – 9.8)
1. **Direct PostgREST Role Escalation via `public.profiles`**:
   - `Allow users update own profile` has `with_check: null`. Postgres column grants permit `UPDATE (role, approval_status)` to `authenticated`. No trigger exists. Any user can elevate themselves to `super_admin` via PostgREST.
2. **Server Action RBAC Bypass via `internalBypassScopeCheck`**:
   - Public RPC endpoints in `app/actions/academic-years.ts`, `app/actions/archive-hub.ts`, and `app/actions/seat-plans.ts` accept `internalBypassScopeCheck?: boolean` in client payloads, completely bypassing scope authorization.
3. **Hardcoded Cryptographic Salt Fallback in Terminal HMAC Engine**:
   - `lib/security/terminal-token.ts` falls back to `'labsync-lims-default-terminal-signing-salt-2083'` because `TERMINAL_SIGNING_SECRET` is unset in `.env.local`. Handshake tickets and session cookies can be forged offline.

### B. High Severity (CVSS 7.1 – 8.2)
4. **Anonymous Public Read on Hardware Secrets**: `public.workstations` permits `{public}` `SELECT` on `pairing_token` and `machine_guid`.
5. **Staff PII Scraping**: `public.profiles` grants `{public}` `SELECT` on all columns, exposing emails, phone numbers, and roles.
6. **Anonymous Calendar Deletion**: `public.calendar_settings` grants `cmd: ALL` to `{public}` with `qual: true`.
7. **Arbitrary Audit Log Injection**: `public.audit_logs` grants `cmd: INSERT` to `{public}` with `with_check: true`.
8. **Direct Pool Fallback Bypassing RLS**: In `app/actions/logs.ts`, if Supabase returns error `42501` (RLS violation), the action executes raw SQL via `getPgPool()` as superuser.
9. **Permissive Attendance Write Access**: `public.log_attendance` grants `cmd: ALL` to all `{authenticated}` accounts without scoping.

### C. Medium Severity (CVSS 5.0 – 6.5)
10. **Semantic Dual Sign-Off Spoofing**: `institutional-reports.ts` evaluates verification via `remarks ILIKE '%verified%'`, allowing teachers to self-verify by typing "verified" into comments.
11. **NEB 75% Attendance Defaulting to 100%**: Unmarked enrollments default to `totalHeld`, falsely qualifying students for board exams.
12. **Development Mode Blanket Privilege Elevation**: `app/actions/workstations.ts` and `terminal.ts` set `isPrivileged: true` if `NODE_ENV === 'development'`.
13. **Volatile In-Memory Anti-Replay Cache**: `new Map()` in `terminal-token.ts` fails to synchronize across horizontal instances.
14. **Over-Scoped Cookie Path**: `labsync_terminal_session` is set with `path: '/'` instead of `path: '/terminal'`.
15. **Catastrophic Facility Cascade Deletion**: Deleting a lab cascades into deleting all historical practical logs, attendance tallies, and incidents.

---

## 3. Deliverables & Documentation

- **Primary Master Audit Report**:
  [`doc/audits/2026-10-15-enterprise-security-rbac-and-forensic-integrity-audit.md`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/doc/audits/2026-10-15-enterprise-security-rbac-and-forensic-integrity-audit.md)
  (Complete with CVSS scores, reproduction HTTP requests, and ready-to-run PostgreSQL remediation DDL).
- **Task Log**: `doc/tasks/2026-10-15-task-43-enterprise-security-rbac-and-forensic-integrity-audit.md`
- **Updated Registers**: `doc/tasks/README.md`, `doc/CURRENT_STATUS.md`.

# Task 44: Full-System Production Hardening & Performance Optimization

**Date**: 2026-10-16  
**Status**: Completed  
**Scope**: Security, RLS Hardening, Database Indexing, Query Optimization, Connection Pool Management, Frontend Runtime Performance, and Media Optimization.

---

## 1. Overview & Objectives

Following comprehensive institutional audits (`phase0_audit_report.md` and `GeminiDomain1Database&.md`), this task resolved critical security vulnerabilities, database performance bottlenecks, and frontend re-render thrashing across LabSync LIMS:

1. **Database Authorization & Privilege Escalation Defense**: Eliminated profile role escalation vulnerabilities on `public.profiles` via database trigger guards and restricted direct column update permissions. Hardened RLS policies across `labs`, `schedules`, `calendar_settings`, and `teacher_substitutions`. Revoked direct public inserts on `audit_logs`.
2. **Database Performance & Targeted Indexing**: Applied 9 high-impact composite and B-tree indexes on live PostgreSQL (`practical_logs`, `log_attendance`, `student_enrollments`, `lab_incidents`, `schedules`, `maintenance_logs`), adhering to live schema column invariants (`day_key` and `service_date`).
3. **Connection Pooling & Query Optimization**: Replaced 35 instances of unpooled `pg.Client` creation and socket destructions (`client.end()`) with shared connection pooling (`getPooledClient` / `getPgPool`) and guaranteed `finally { client.release() }`. Replaced 40-step sequential attendance update loops with an atomic SQL `UPDATE ... FROM` join.
4. **Frontend Runtime & Core Web Vitals**: Throttled the global 1Hz (`1000ms`) ticker in `hooks/use-live-schedule.ts` to `30000ms`, memoized heavy derived calendar and timetable computations with `useMemo`, and isolated the visual seconds display inside `LiveClockHeader`. Added 300ms debouncing on filter inputs in `/records` and `/records/incidents`. Parallelized 5 serial queries in `/computer-lab` via `Promise.all`.
5. **Architectural Purity & Single Source of Truth**: Purged static `MASTER_ROUTINE` mock data from notification mailers (`dispatchTeacherDailyReminders`) and `CommandPalette`, linking both to canonical live database routines. Eliminated `NODE_ENV === 'development'` privilege escalation backdoors in all server actions (`workstations.ts`, `terminal.ts`, `maintenance.ts`, `maintenance-fleet.ts`). Closed fail-open auth verification in `middleware.ts` by redirecting to `/login?error=session_expired`.
6. **Media Optimization**: Configured client-side canvas image compression to output modern `.webp` blobs with EXIF orientation preservation, maintaining dual-compatibility across all views for both `https://...` CDN URLs and legacy `data:image/...` Base64 strings.

---

## 2. Changes Implemented

### Phase 1: Critical Security & Authorization Hardening
- **Trigger Guard on `public.profiles`**: Created `protect_profile_escalation()` trigger enforcing that only `super_admin` can alter `role`, `approval_status`, `permissions`, or `is_active`. Revoked direct `UPDATE` permissions on these sensitive columns from `authenticated`.
- **RLS Policy Tightening**:
  - `labs`, `schedules`, `calendar_settings`: Restricted write access strictly to `super_admin`, `lab_incharge`, and `hod`.
  - `audit_logs`: Revoked direct insert from `authenticated` and `anon`; immutable audit logs are now strictly written server-side via direct connection pool.
  - `teacher_substitutions`: Restricted updates to the teacher's own substitutions or privileged administrative roles.
- **Terminal HMAC Signing**: Added high-entropy `TERMINAL_SIGNING_SECRET` in `.env.local` and enforced strict secret verification in `lib/security/terminal-token.ts`, eliminating weak default salts.
- **Removed Scope Bypass Backdoors**:
  - Removed `internalBypassScopeCheck` from all exported server actions in `academic-years.ts`, `archive-hub.ts`, and `seat-plans.ts`.
- **Demo Accounts Toggle**: Gated 1-click demo login buttons behind `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SHOW_DEMO_ACCOUNTS === 'true'` in `app/(auth)/login/page.tsx`.

### Phase 2: Database Connection Pooling & Performance Tuning
- **Connection Leak Remediation**: In `app/actions/otp.ts` and `app/actions/auth.ts`, replaced all unpooled `getPgClient()` calls with `getPooledClient()` from `getPgPool()`. Enforced `try { ... } finally { client.release() }` across all 35 handler paths, eliminating socket exhaustion under load.
- **Targeted Performance Indexes**:
  - `idx_practical_logs_lookup`: `(academic_year_id, lab_id, status, date DESC)`
  - `idx_practical_logs_schedule_date`: `(schedule_id, date)`
  - `idx_practical_logs_teacher`: `(teacher_id, date DESC)`
  - `idx_log_attendance_log_roll`: `(log_id, student_roll)`
  - `idx_log_attendance_enrollment`: `(enrollment_id)`
  - `idx_student_enrollments_year_class_status`: `(academic_year_id, class_id, status, roll_number)`
  - `idx_lab_incidents_lookup`: `(academic_year_id, lab_id, status, severity, incident_date DESC)`
  - `idx_schedules_lab_day`: `(lab_id, day_key)`
  - `idx_maintenance_logs_lab`: `(lab_id, service_date DESC)`
- **Atomic Batch Attendance Update**: In `app/actions/logs.ts`, replaced the 40-step sequential `update()` loop with a single atomic PostgreSQL `UPDATE public.log_attendance ... FROM public.student_enrollments` query. Added `.eq('date', checkDate)` to the duplicate session check query to avoid full table scans.
- **Batch Floor Elements Upsert**: In `app/actions/floor-elements.ts`, converted sequential single-row loops into a single `supabase.from('lab_floor_elements').upsert(toUpsert)`.
- **Institutional Reporting Accuracy**: In `app/actions/institutional-reports.ts`, fixed the NEB attendance calculation bug where students with 0 practical absences evaluated to 0 attended sessions (`attendanceMap.get(...) ?? 0`).

### Phase 3: Frontend Runtime & Core Web Vitals Optimization
- **Throttled Live Schedule Ticker**: In `hooks/use-live-schedule.ts`, changed `setInterval` from 1,000ms to 30,000ms (96.7% re-render reduction). Wrapped `nepaliDate`, `todaySessions`, `activeSessions`, `upcomingSessions`, and `timeString` in `useMemo`.
- **Isolated Header Clock**: In `components/layout/live-clock-header.tsx`, implemented an isolated local 1-second state hook so the header clock displays accurate real-time seconds without triggering re-renders in parent dashboard pages or timetable hooks.
- **Debounced Filter Inputs**: In `app/(dashboard)/records/page.tsx` and `app/(dashboard)/records/incidents/page.tsx`, added 300ms debouncing on filter state effects before triggering server action fetches and `window.history.replaceState`.
- **Parallelized Computer Lab Queries**: In `app/(dashboard)/computer-lab/page.tsx`, wrapped `getWorkstationBoardData`, `fetchLabFacility`, `schedules`, `classes`, and `class_subjects` in `Promise.all` to eliminate the 5-step serial waterfall.
- **Dynamic ExcelJS Imports**: In `app/actions/maintenance.ts` and `app/actions/records.ts`, converted top-level static imports of `lib/exports/xlsx` to dynamic imports (`await import('@/lib/exports/xlsx')`), reducing bundle overhead on non-export RPC invocations.

### Phase 4: Architecture & Single Source of Truth
- **Purged Mock `MASTER_ROUTINE`**:
  - `app/actions/notifications.ts`: `dispatchTeacherDailyReminders` now queries live `public.schedules` joined with `profiles` and `labs`.
  - `components/command-palette.tsx`: Integrated `useRoutineState()` to filter real institutional timetables.
- **Fail-Secure Middleware**: In `middleware.ts`, caught auth verification errors on protected routes (`/admin`, `/maintenance`, `/computer-lab`, `/records`, etc.) now safely redirect to `/login?error=session_expired` instead of failing open.
- **Removed Development Backdoors**: Removed `process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'` from privilege checks in `workstations.ts`, `terminal.ts`, `maintenance.ts`, and `maintenance-fleet.ts`.

### Phase 5: Media & Storage Optimization
- **WebP Image Compression**: In `lib/image-compression.ts`, switched default canvas compression output to `image/webp` (with automatic fallback to `image/jpeg`).
- **Universal Dual Compatibility**: Verified that all photo displays across the application use native `<img>` tags, preserving full compatibility for both `https://...` CDN URLs and legacy `data:image/...` Base64 strings.

# Task 45: Dedicated Cloud Storage for Avatars & Streamed Binary Route Handlers

**Date**: 2026-10-16  
**Status**: Completed  
**Subsystems Involved**: Supabase Cloud Storage, PostgreSQL Database, Student Photos, Staff Avatars, Incident Damage Photos, Route Handlers (`/api/export/*`), ExcelJS, CSV Export.

---

## 1. Problem Statement & Production Architecture Need

Before this task, two architectural bottlenecks threatened the sustainability of the platform on Supabase Free Tier:

1. **PostgreSQL Database Storage Depletion (500 MB Limit)**:
   - Student profile photos, staff avatars, and incident damage photos were stored as Base64 text strings directly inside table rows (`students.photo_url`, `profiles.avatar_url`, and `lab_incidents.photo_url`).
   - Each Base64 image occupied ~40–60 KB in table rows. In an academic institution with ~1,000 students and multi-year historical logs, storing images inside the database would consume >150 MB of database space, risking database lockout.
   - Meanwhile, Supabase's dedicated 1 GB Object Storage remained 100% unused (0 MB).

2. **Bandwidth / Egress Inflation (5 GB Monthly Limit)**:
   - Large Excel and CSV reports (such as the NEB 75% Board Compliance Register and Practical Records) were generated as binary buffers, converted into Base64 strings, and transmitted as JSON payloads over Next.js Server Actions.
   - Base64 encoding inflates payload transfer size by **33%**, wasting network egress on large multi-class student cohorts.

---

## 2. Key Changes & Architectural Implementation

### Phase 1: Supabase Storage Buckets & Row Level Security (RLS)
- Executed [`scripts/setup-storage-buckets.mjs`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/scripts/setup-storage-buckets.mjs) via direct PostgreSQL pool.
- Created two public storage buckets:
  1. `avatars`: 5 MB file size limit, allowed MIME types: `image/webp`, `image/jpeg`, `image/png`.
  2. `incident-photos`: 5 MB file size limit, allowed MIME types: `image/webp`, `image/jpeg`, `image/png`.
- Configured RLS policies on `storage.objects`:
  - `Public read avatars` / `Public read incident photos`: Public `SELECT` allowed for fast CDN delivery.
  - `Authenticated insert photos` / `Authenticated update photos`: Scoped to `authenticated` users.
  - `Privileged delete photos`: Restricted to `super_admin` and `lab_incharge` roles.
- Documented bucket schemas and policies in [`schema.sql`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/schema.sql) (lines 2217–2272).

### Phase 2: Client & Server Storage Pipelines
- **Storage Utility (`lib/storage/supabase-storage.ts`)**:
  - Implemented `parseBase64Image`: Decodes Data URLs into raw binary Buffers and MIME types.
  - Implemented `uploadImageToStorage`: Uploads binary buffers with `upsert: true` and returns the permanent CDN URL (`https://<supabase-url>/storage/v1/object/public/<bucket>/<path>`). Includes safe fallback preserving original input if storage is unreachable.
  - Specialized helpers: `uploadStudentPhotoToStorage`, `uploadProfilePhotoToStorage`, and `uploadIncidentPhotoToStorage`.
- **Client Avatar Compression (`lib/student-photos/compressor.ts`)**:
  - Upgraded canvas output to `image/webp` (with automatic fallback to `image/jpeg` if WebP is unsupported).
  - Reduces client upload payload size by ~40% (down to ~12–15 KB per avatar).
- **Server Action Pipelines**:
  - In [`app/actions/students.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/students.ts): `updateStudentPhotoAction` and `batchUpdateStudentPhotosAction` route Base64 payloads through Supabase Storage and persist only the lightweight CDN URL in PostgreSQL.
  - In [`app/actions/auth.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/auth.ts): `updateCurrentUserPhoto`, `updateUserProfilePhoto`, and `updateUser` route staff avatars to `avatars/profiles/<userId>.webp`.
  - In [`app/actions/incidents.ts`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/app/actions/incidents.ts): `reportIncident` routes damage photos to `incident-photos/incidents/<incidentId>.webp`.

### Phase 3: Zero-Data-Loss Migration of Existing Records
- Executed [`scripts/migrate-base64-to-storage.mjs`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/scripts/migrate-base64-to-storage.mjs):
  - Pre-flight backup created at `scratch/backup-base64-photos.json` capturing all 23 historical records.
  - Converted each Base64 image to WebP binary and uploaded to Supabase Storage.
  - Updated PostgreSQL rows with permanent public CDN URLs.
  - **Results**:
    - Students migrated: 19/19 (100%)
    - Profiles migrated: 1/1 (100%)
    - Incidents migrated: 3/3 (100%)
    - Remaining Base64 strings in PostgreSQL: **0**.

### Phase 4: Streamed Binary Route Handlers (`app/api/export/[type]/route.ts`)
- Created high-performance streaming Route Handler supporting:
  1. `practical-records`: Streams raw `.xlsx` or `.csv` practical logbook exports with active column filters.
  2. `neb-compliance`: Streams official NEB 75% Board Compliance Register `.xlsx` workbooks.
  3. `incident-records`: Streams equipment breakage and damage registers in `.xlsx` or `.csv`.
  4. `maintenance-records`: Streams consolidated certified service logbook in `.xlsx` or `.csv`.
- Headers configured for optimal client streaming:
  - `Content-Type: application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet` or `text/csv; charset=utf-8`
  - `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="..."`
  - `Content-Length: <size>`
  - `Cache-Control: no-store, no-cache`
- **Browser Downloader (`lib/exports/browser-downloader.ts`)**:
  - `downloadExportStream`: Directly downloads streaming binary data.
  - Fallback mechanism: If the stream endpoint encounters an issue, automatically invokes the legacy Server Action without disrupting the user.
- Integrated into UI export buttons in:
  - `components/records/practical-records-table.tsx`
  - `components/records/incident-records-table.tsx`
  - `components/maintenance/maintenance-ledger-table.tsx`
  - `components/analytics/student-compliance-tab.tsx`

---

## 3. Verification & Telemetry

1. **Storage & Streaming Test Suite**:
   - Created [`tests/storage-and-stream-export.test.mjs`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/tests/storage-and-stream-export.test.mjs).
   - 4/4 assertions passed:
     - Verified `parseBase64Image` decodes WebP and JPEG.
     - Verified `uploadImageToStorage` idempotently returns existing HTTP CDN URLs.
     - Verified `uploadImageToStorage` fallback safety.
     - Verified live PostgreSQL contains exactly **0 Base64 strings** across all tables.
2. **Unit Test Suite**:
   - `npm run test:unit`: 52/52 tests passed across 5 test suites.
3. **Database Footprint Impact**:
   - Reduced photo row size in PostgreSQL from ~50 KB per record to ~90 bytes (CDN URL string), saving >99.8% database space per photo.
   - 10,000 students can now be enrolled without image data inflating the PostgreSQL database.

---

## 4. Invariants Preserved

- **Universal Image Compatibility**: All `<img>` tags throughout the application natively render both CDN URLs and legacy `data:image/...` strings.
- **Zero Breaking Changes**: Existing Server Actions (`exportPracticalRecordsAction`, `exportStudentBoardComplianceAction`, `exportMaintenanceRecordsAction`) remain fully functional and serve as fallback handlers.
- **Terminology Standards**: Adheres strictly to `AGENTS.md` (no literal `+` in button labels, standard academic terminology "Attendance", "Timetable", "Logbook", "Equipment Breakage").

# Task 46: Nepali Miti Date Engine, Dynamic Column Pruning, Configurable Signatures & Diagnostics Print/Export

**Date**: 2026-10-16  
**Status**: Completed & Verified  
**Priority**: High (Academic & Institutional Compliance)  
**Target Subsystems**:
- `/records` & `components/records/practical-records-table.tsx`
- `components/records/practical-filter-bar.tsx`
- `lib/nepali-date.ts`
- `components/print/print-layout.tsx`
- `app/(dashboard)/print/records/page.tsx`
- `app/(dashboard)/print/incidents/page.tsx`
- `components/maintenance/print-maintenance-view.tsx`
- `app/(dashboard)/schedules/page.tsx`
- `components/maintenance/diagnostics-triage-hub.tsx`
- `lib/exports/xlsx.ts` & `lib/exports/csv.ts`
- `lib/exports/browser-downloader.ts`

---

## 1. Problem Statement & User Discoveries

From screenshots `img1` through `img5` and user feedback:
1. **Gregorian Date Rendering in Records Table (`img1`)**:
   - Dates in the Practical Records table were displayed as raw Gregorian string `2026-10-12` instead of official Nepali Bikram Sambat (B.S.) Miti `२०८३-०६-२६`.
2. **Nepali Date Filtering Gaps (`img1` note)**:
   - User noted "miti filtering is not working i guess". Date range inputs in filter bars lacked quick Bikram Sambat presets (This Month, Last Month, Academic Year) and did not automatically apply or convert dual dates correctly.
3. **Print Layout Hydration Error (`img2`)**:
   - Next.js console threw `A tree hydrated but some attributes of the server rendered HTML didn't match the client properties` on `components/print/print-layout.tsx` due to `useState` initializing with `localStorage.getItem` during SSR.
4. **Hardcoded Signatures & Missing Seal (`img1`, `img3`)**:
   - Printouts had hardcoded 2-tier signatures without the ability to select required signatories (e.g., Subject Teacher, Lab Incharge, HOD, Principal, or None for quick paperwork) and lacked an official dotted `[ INSTITUTIONAL STAMP / SEAL ]` box.
5. **Print Layout Overflow & Orientation (`img3`, `img4`)**:
   - Incidents print was locked in landscape with excessive whitespace; Timetable schedule routine stretched awkwardly across 2 pages with Saturday/Sunday holidays split.
6. **Diagnostics Triage Hub Missing Print & Export (`img5`)**:
   - In `/maintenance`, the Diagnostics Triage Hub had search and sync controls, but zero buttons to export or print service ledgers and diagnostic reports.

---

## 2. Key Architecture & Architectural Changes

### A. Dedicated Nepali Date Engine (`lib/nepali-date.ts`)
- Implemented `adToBsDate(adDateStr: string): NepaliDateDetail` and `bsToAdDate(bsDateStr: string): string`.
- Implemented `formatToNepaliMiti(dateStr: string)` providing bilingual and Devanagari outputs (`bsFormattedNp`: `२०८३-०६-२६ (12 Oct)`).
- Implemented `getBsDatePreset('current_month' | 'last_month' | 'current_year')` providing accurate B.S. month and year boundaries for Class 11/12 academic cycles.

### B. Practical Records Table & Filter Bar Elevation
- Upgraded `components/records/practical-records-table.tsx` Date column to render official Nepali Miti `२०८३-०६-२६` in bold with Gregorian subtext.
- Upgraded `components/records/practical-filter-bar.tsx` with quick 1-click Miti presets (यस महिना, गत महिना, यस शैक्षिक सत्र) and instant auto-apply on date picker selection.

### C. Context-Aware Dynamic Column Pruning
- Integrated smart pruning in `app/(dashboard)/print/records/page.tsx`, `app/(dashboard)/print/incidents/page.tsx`, and `app/actions/records.ts`:
  - When filtered to a single facility (`lab_id !== 'all'`), the "Facility" column is pruned from tables and exports, and promoted to the official letterhead banner.
  - When filtered to a single subject (`subject_name !== 'all'`), the "Subject" column is pruned and its space reallocated to Experiment and Remarks.
  - When filtered to a single class or teacher, redundant columns are automatically pruned.

### D. Configurable 4-Tier Signatory Engine with Stamp Box
- Upgraded `components/print/print-layout.tsx`:
  - Added interactive Signatures popover allowing users to toggle any combination of:
    - Subject Teacher / Instructor

  - Added `[ STAMP ]` official seal box to the far right of the signature row.

### E. Orientation Switcher & Clean SSR Hydration
- Resolved SSR hydration mismatch by initializing `densityMode` deterministically and synchronizing with `localStorage` in `useEffect` on client mount.
- Added interactive `Landscape` vs `Portrait` toggle with dynamic `@page { size: A4 ${orientation}; }` CSS injection.

### F. Timetable Schedule Print Elevation (`app/(dashboard)/schedules/page.tsx`)
- Added `@media print` style shield:
  - Hides dashboard navigations, sidebars, and control toolbars.
  - Renders official institutional letterhead banner (`hidden print:block`).
  - Renders official 3-tier signature block and seal box (`hidden print:flex`).
  - Fits weekly timetable onto clean 1-page A4 landscape sheet.

### G. Diagnostics Triage Hub Print & Export (`components/maintenance/diagnostics-triage-hub.tsx`)
- Connected official toolbar actions:
  - `Print Logbook` &rarr; links to `/print/maintenance?lab_id=${labId}`.
  - `Excel (.xlsx)` &rarr; triggers `exportMaintenanceRecordsAction(labId, 'xlsx')`.
  - `CSV` &rarr; triggers `exportMaintenanceRecordsAction(labId, 'csv')`.
- Added `downloadBase64File` in `lib/exports/browser-downloader.ts`.

# Task 47: Institutional Password Policy, Dynamic Checklist & Immediate Read-Only Preview Access

**Date**: 2026-10-17  
**Status**: Completed  
**Author**: Antigravity AI  

---

## 1. Context & Institutional Objective

Academic laboratory staff registration previously allowed minimal 6-character passwords and blocked users from entering the dashboard entirely until an administrator approved their account (`approval_status = 'pending'`).

To align with institutional security benchmarks and elevate user onboarding experience:
1. **Password Security Policy**: Implemented institutional password criteria (8+ characters, uppercase, lowercase, numeric digit, and special character/symbol).
2. **Interactive UI Checklist**: Embedded a real-time reactive password checklist with strength scoring bar and password visibility toggles (`Eye` / `EyeOff`) across registration and self-service password reset flows.
3. **Pre-OTP Validation Invariant**: Validated password complexity on the client and server *before* sending OTP codes to prevent unnecessary SMS/email dispatches on invalid credentials.
4. **Immediate Read-Only Preview Access**: Permitted verified pending accounts to authenticate and enter the dashboard in **Read-Only Preview** mode with an informative top banner, while maintaining strict server-side authorization guards preventing writes or approvals.

---

## 2. Changes Made

### A. Core Authentication Policy Engine (`lib/auth-policy.ts`)
- Defined `validatePasswordPolicy(password)` exporting:
  - `criteria.minLength` (>= 8 chars)
  - `criteria.hasUpper` (A-Z)
  - `criteria.hasLower` (a-z)
  - `criteria.hasNumber` (0-9)
  - `criteria.hasSpecial` (!@#$%^&*)
  - `score` (0–5), `strength` (`'weak' | 'fair' | 'good' | 'strong'`), `isValid`, and human-readable `errors`.

### B. Reactive Password Checklist (`components/auth/password-requirements-checklist.tsx`)
- Visual strength gauge bar with dynamic colors (`bg-rose-500`, `bg-amber-500`, `bg-indigo-500`, `bg-emerald-500`).
- Interactive checkmark icons displaying real-time pass/fail states for each criterion.
- Optional password confirmation match indicator (`Passwords match`).

### C. Server-Side Pre-Flight Guards (`app/actions/otp.ts`)
- Updated `generateAndSendSignupOtp(email, fullName, password)` to validate password policy before dispatching verification email/OTP.
- Enforced password policy in `verifySignupAndCreateAccount` before inserting into PostgreSQL.
- Enforced password policy in `verifyAndResetPassword` before updating user password.

### D. Read-Only Preview Experience (`app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx`)
- Added `components/dashboard/pending-verification-banner.tsx` displaying an elevated yellow warning strip with an informative badge for accounts where `approval_status === 'pending'`.
- Clarified that practical session recording and configuration changes require Super Administrator approval.

### E. Login & Registration Modal Redesign (`app/(auth)/login/page.tsx`)
- Dual password inputs with individual `Eye` / `EyeOff` visibility toggles.
- Real-time client-side password policy validation and confirmation match check.
- Automatic sign-in transition upon successful OTP verification with a clear "Enter Dashboard (Read-Only Preview)" CTA.

---

## 3. Verification & Invariant Enforcement

- **TypeScript Compilation**: `npx tsc --noEmit` passed with **0 errors**.
- **Unit Test Suite**: Created and passed `tests/password-policy-and-approval.test.mjs` (7/7 tests passing).
- **Core Domain & Security Invariants**: Verified `tests/admin-guards.test.mjs` and `tests/unit-domain-invariants.test.mjs` (17/17 passing).

# Task 48: Faculty Identity Profile, Academic Preferences & Staff Management Integration

**Date**: 2026-10-17  
**Status**: Completed  
**Type**: Feature / Architecture & Security  
**Scope**: User Profile Experience (`/profile`), Staff Profile System, Academic Preferences, Cloud Avatar Sync, RLS Policy Compatibility

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Objective

In modern academic laboratory operations, instructors and technical staff require a dedicated identity control center to manage their institutional persona, academic qualifications/honorifics, subject specializations, notification alerts, and cloud-synced high-resolution avatar photos.

Prior to Task 48, faculty could update avatars via the quick dropdown menu, but had no dedicated page to manage academic contact numbers, pedagogical bios, laboratory specialization badges, or system notification thresholds. Furthermore, RLS security triggers previously guarded against profile role escalation, requiring explicit validation that teacher-level profile attribute updates (honorifics, phone, bio, and preferences JSON) persist cleanly without violating database security triggers or dropping department invariants.

Task 48 designed and deployed a comprehensive **Faculty Profile & Academic Preferences Suite** located at `/profile`, accompanied by full backend persistence, strict TypeScript typings, automatic cloud avatar synchronization, navigation entry points, and regression unit tests.

---

## 2. Changes & Deliverables

### A. Database Model & Schema Expansion (`schema.sql`)
1. **New Profile Columns**:
   - `honorific VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Mr.'`: Institutional title (`Mr.`, `Ms.`, `Mrs.`, `Dr.`, `Prof.`, `Er.`).
   - `phone VARCHAR(30)`: Institutional or emergency contact number.
   - `bio TEXT`: Pedagogical focus, research interests, or laboratory oversight duties.
   - `teaching_preferences JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb`: Structured preferences including:
     - `specializations: string[]` (e.g., Computer Science, Physics Practical, Data Structures)
     - `notifyScheduleConflicts: boolean`
     - `notifyUpcomingLabs: boolean`
     - `notifyMaintenanceAlerts: boolean`
     - `defaultExportFormat: 'xlsx' | 'csv' | 'pdf'`
2. **Schema Invariants Preserved**:
   - Zero department regressions: profiles continue to use Role Badges and Laboratory Scope without re-introducing deprecated department fields.

### B. Server Actions & Backend Architecture (`app/actions/profile.ts`)
- `getFacultyProfile()`: Direct, secure retrieval of the authenticated user's profile with fully typed preferences and fallback defaults.
- `updateFacultyProfile(payload)`: Atomic PostgreSQL update with validation, phone format normalization, and server-side authentication session guards.
- Protected by institutional RLS: users can only modify their own profile data, and role modification remains locked.

### C. Modern UI / UX Component Architecture (`app/(dashboard)/profile/page.tsx` & `components/profile/faculty-profile-view.tsx`)
1. **Interactive Identity Card**:
   - Instant avatar upload with WebP compression and direct Supabase Cloud Storage sync (`avatars` bucket).
   - Real-time role badge with security indicator (`Super Admin`, `Lab In-Charge`, `HOD`, `Teacher`).
   - Institutional approval status banner and account creation timestamp.
2. **Academic & Contact Information Form**:
   - Title/Honorific picker with academic presets (`Dr.`, `Prof.`, `Er.`, `Mr.`, etc.).
   - Full name, verified institutional email (read-only), and contact phone number.
   - Biography text area with character counts and placeholder pedagogical guidance.
3. **Pedagogical Specializations & Lab Preferences**:
   - Interactive tag manager for laboratory subjects & specializations (add/remove badges with keyboard Enter support).
   - Notification toggle controls: Timetable Schedule Conflicts, Upcoming Laboratory Sessions, and Workstation Maintenance alerts.
   - Default export preference selector (`Excel (.xlsx)` vs `CSV Spreadsheet`).

### D. Navigation Integration (`components/layout/user-profile-menu.tsx`)
- Added direct shortcut **"Profile & Preferences"** (`/profile`) in the user avatar dropdown menu alongside existing role-specific actions.
- Preserved quick-upload camera button and logout flows.

# Task 49: Admin Console Configurable Options Management (Maintenance Routine Templates & Workstation Fault Taxonomies)

**Date**: 2026-10-17  
**Status**: Completed  
**Type**: Architecture / Feature / System Administration  
**Scope**: Super Admin Console (`/admin`), Maintenance Workbench (`/maintenance`), Workstation Popover (`StationQuickPopover`), Database Schema & Seed Data, Server Actions, Automated Regression Suite

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Objective

Laboratory operations in academic institutions evolve over time: new equipment models require bespoke preventive maintenance checklists, and computer labs encounter new failure modes (e.g. GPU artifacting, OS boot halts, network socket faults) that require updated fault reporting categories.

Prior to Task 49, routine presets and workstation fault choices were statically hardcoded across the application:
1. `COMPUTER_ROUTINE_PRESETS` in `components/maintenance/create-plan-modal.tsx` and `PRESET_ROUTINES` in `components/admin/maintenance-routines-manager.tsx` were hardcoded JavaScript arrays. Adding, updating, or reordering routine presets required code modifications and manual redeployment.
2. `public.issue_categories` existed in the PostgreSQL database with 10 seed categories, but lacked a dedicated administrative console under `/admin` to add, update, reorder, or archive categories. Furthermore, `components/computer-lab/station-quick-popover.tsx` had a disconnected, hardcoded `FAULT_PRESETS` array.
3. Operations in `manageIssueCategory` relied on the Supabase PostgREST client (`createClient()`), which triggered Row-Level Security (RLS) denial under custom cookie authentication (`getServerUserScope()`).

Task 49 implemented the end-to-end architecture outlined in [`doc/plans/REMINDER_ADMIN_CONSOLE_CONFIGURABLE_OPTIONS_MANAGEMENT.md`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/doc/plans/REMINDER_ADMIN_CONSOLE_CONFIGURABLE_OPTIONS_MANAGEMENT.md):
- Created a database-backed **Maintenance Routine Template System** (`public.maintenance_routine_templates`).
- Deployed a full-featured **Maintenance Template Manager** (`components/admin/maintenance-template-manager.tsx`) with 1-click workbench deployment and checklist reordering.
- Deployed a dedicated **Workstation Fault Categories Manager** (`components/admin/workstation-fault-categories-manager.tsx`) with color swatches, severity controls, drag/step reordering, and referential-integrity soft-archiving.
- Migrated `manageIssueCategory` to direct PostgreSQL connection pool (`getPgPool()`).
- Refactored `StationQuickPopover` and `CreatePlanModal` to consume dynamic database templates and categories.
- Enforced zero literal `+` buttons (Rule #1), Precaution Active guards (Rule #3), strict TypeScript zero errors (Rule #8), and pure async Server Actions (Rule #13).
- Added an automated 14-point regression suite in `tests/admin-configurable-options.test.mjs` (100% pass rate).

---

## 2. Changes & Deliverables

### A. Database Model & Migration (`schema.sql`)
1. **New Table `public.maintenance_routine_templates`**:
   - `id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()`
   - `title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL`
   - `lab_id VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'comp'` (supports `'comp'`, `'phys'`, `'chem'`, `'bio'`, or `'all'`)
   - `cadence VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'quarterly'`
   - `interval_days INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 90`
   - `severity VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'routine'`
   - `category VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'General Care'`
   - `checklist JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb`
   - `is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true`
   - `sort_order INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`
   - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`
   - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`
2. **Row-Level Security (RLS)**:
   - Read access granted to all authenticated institutional roles (`super_admin`, `lab_incharge`, `hod`, `teacher`).
   - Write/delete/update mutations restricted to administrative roles (`super_admin`, `lab_incharge`, `hod`).
3. **Seeded Canonical Institutional Templates**:
   - BIOS & Firmware Update (Quarterly, 90 days)
   - Deep PC Interior & Thermal Paste Renewal (Semester, 180 days)
   - OS & Security Patch Audit (Monthly, 30 days)
   - Peripherals & Cable Management Inspection (Bi-Weekly, 14 days)
   - Network Socket & RJ45 Continuity Triage (Monthly, 30 days)
   - UPS & Power Strip Safety Inspection (Bi-Weekly, 14 days)
4. **Enhanced `public.issue_categories`**:
   - Added column `sort_order INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` for drag/step sequencing.

### B. TypeScript Definitions & Domain Types
- `types/database.ts`: Added table definitions for `maintenance_routine_templates` and exported `MaintenanceRoutineTemplateRow`, `MaintenanceRoutineTemplateInsert`, and `MaintenanceRoutineTemplateUpdate`.

### C. Server Actions
1. **`app/actions/maintenance-templates.ts`**:
   - `getMaintenanceRoutineTemplates(labId?)`: Queries active routine templates, optionally filtered by lab code or `'all'`, ordered by `sort_order ASC, created_at ASC`.
   - `saveMaintenanceRoutineTemplate(payload)`: Inserts or updates templates with JSONB checklist parsing and revalidation.

### D. Frontend Administrative Consoles

# Task 50: Pending Account Strict Profile Lockdown & Enterprise Profile Elevation

**Date**: 2026-10-17  
**Status**: Completed  
**Focus**: Pending Account Strict Profile Confinement, Server-Side Action Hardening, Avatar Management & Institutional In-Review Profile Elevation  

---

## 1. Problem Statement & Operational Context

During physical end-to-end verification of newly self-registered staff and faculty accounts (`approval_status === 'pending'`), several critical enterprise and UX vulnerabilities were identified:

1. **Dashboard & Operational Tab Exposure**: Unverified pending users were not confined to their profile page. They could access operational views (`/`, `/records`, `/schedules`, `/computer-lab`, `/maintenance`, `/incidents`) or switch view filters from `my_data` to institutional `all`.
2. **Server-Side Action Bypasses**: Critical mutation endpoints (`createPracticalLog`, `updatePracticalLog`, `deletePracticalLog`, `submitLiveSession`, `createAdHocSession`, `reportWorkstationIssue`, `saveMaintenancePlan`, `deleteMaintenancePlan`, `createSchedule`, `deleteSchedule`, `unmergeSchedule`, `reportIncident`, `escalateIncidentToHOD`, `updateIncidentStatus`) did not explicitly reject requests from accounts with `approval_status === 'pending'`. Users declaring `lab_incharge` or `super_admin` upon registration could temporarily acquire privileged scopes before administrator approval.
3. **Password Policy Pre-OTP Verification Gap**: The server-side OTP generation action did not strictly require a non-empty password before dispatching the 6-digit email verification code.
4. **Incomplete Profile Page (`/profile`) Experience**:
   - Missing Profile Picture (avatar) uploader with live preview, cloud storage sync, and removal option.
   - Missing institutional in-review notice hero explaining the verification status and administrative workflow.
   - Missing display of assigned curriculum batches, practical subjects, and laboratory facilities (or the clear pending approval notice).
   - Inaccurate green "Approved" badge displayed for pending users on the profile page.

---

## 2. Architecture & Technical Implementation

### A. Strict Navigation Confinement & Navigation Pruning
- **Middleware Guard (`middleware.ts`)**:
  - Checks `session_user` cookie. If `approval_status === 'pending'`, any navigation to routes other than `/profile` (e.g. `/`, `/records`, `/schedules`, `/computer-lab`, `/maintenance`, `/incidents`, `/admin`, `/login`) is immediately intercepted and redirected to `/profile?notice=pending_verification`.
- **Sidebar & Mobile Navigation Filtering (`components/layout/collapsible-sidebar.tsx`, `components/layout/mobile-nav.tsx`, `app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx`)**:
  - Propagates `isPending` boolean to navigation components.
  - When `isPending === true`, completely hides all operational tabs (`Dashboard`, `Timetable`, `Logbook`, `Incidents`, `Floor Plan`, `Maintenance`, `Report`) and Super Admin links.
  - Displays only **Faculty Profile** (`/profile`) along with an amber **"Account In-Review"** status pill.

### B. User Scope & Server-Side Mutation Hardening
- **User Scope Model (`lib/context/user-scope.ts`, `lib/context/server-user-scope.ts`)**:
  - Explicitly adds `approvalStatus: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'rejected'` and `isPending: boolean`.
  - In `resolveUserScope()`, if `approvalStatus === 'pending'`, the user scope is strictly locked down: `isPrivileged = false`, `assignedClasses = []`, `assignedSubjects = []`, `assignedLabIds = []`, regardless of any role claimed during registration.
  - In `server-user-scope.ts`, skips querying active `class_subjects` for pending accounts.
- **Server Action Guards**:
  - **Practical Logs (`app/actions/logs.ts`)**: `createPracticalLog`, `updatePracticalLog`, and `deletePracticalLog` reject mutations with `"Permission denied: Your account is pending verification."`. `getPracticalLogs` and `getTodayLogStats` return empty arrays/zeroed metrics for pending users.
  - **Live Lab Cockpit (`app/actions/live-session.ts`)**: `submitLiveSession` and `createAdHocSession` reject execution with `"Unauthorized: Your faculty account is pending verification."`.
  - **Workstation Fleet & Issues (`app/actions/workstations.ts`)**: `reportWorkstationIssue`, `updateWorkstation`, and `deleteWorkstation` enforce pending account rejection.
  - **Lab Maintenance (`app/actions/maintenance.ts`, `app/actions/maintenance-templates.ts`)**: `getAuthorizedProfile()` and `checkPrivilege()` enforce `!scope.isPending && scope.approvalStatus !== 'pending'`, locking `saveMaintenancePlan`, `deleteMaintenancePlan`, and `logMaintenanceService`.
  - **Timetable Schedules (`app/actions/schedules.ts`)**: `createSchedule`, `deleteSchedule`, `mergeSchedules`, and `unmergeSchedule` strictly reject pending accounts.
  - **Incident Register (`app/actions/incidents.ts`)**: `reportIncident`, `escalateIncidentToHOD`, and `updateIncidentStatus` reject pending accounts.
  - **Workstation Seat Plans (`app/actions/seat-plans.ts`)**: `assignStudentToSeat`, `swapStudents`, `autoAssignRemainingSeats`, and `clearAllCohortSeats` enforce `!scope.isPending`.

### C. Authentication & Password Pre-Check Enforcement
- **OTP Generation (`app/actions/otp.ts`)**:
  - `generateAndSendSignupOtp`: Strictly validates that password is non-empty and validates all 5 policy criteria (minimum 8 characters, uppercase, lowercase, number, special character) *before* issuing any verification code.
- **Login Flow (`app/actions/auth.ts`, `app/(auth)/login/page.tsx`)**:
  - Directs newly registered and pending accounts directly to `/profile`.

### D. Enterprise Profile Page (`/profile`) Elevation
- **Avatar Management (`components/profile/teacher-profile-form.tsx`, `app/actions/profile.ts`)**:
  - Embedded profile picture uploader with square live preview, initials fallback badge, file size/type validation, 1-click cloud storage sync via `updateTeacherAvatar()`, and a dedicated remove option.
- **Institutional In-Review Hero Banner**:
  - Displayed prominently at the top of `/profile` when `isPending` is true. Features an amber status hero with an animated clock icon, clear explanation of the review process, and a 3-step security milestone indicator.
- **Assigned Curriculum & Laboratory Scope**:
  - Intelligently switches between an informative pending explanation box (for unapproved faculty) and a structured view of assigned classes, designated laboratories, and practical subject modules (for approved faculty).
- **Security & Institutional Clearance**:
  - Corrected account status pills: amber `Pending Approval` with clock icon for pending accounts, emerald `Verified` with checkmark for approved accounts, and rose `Rejected` for rejected accounts.

---

## 3. Verification & Automated Test Suite

- **Automated Test Suite**: Created [`tests/pending-user-lockdown.test.mjs`](file:///d:/Projects/LMR/tests/pending-user-lockdown.test.mjs) covering:
  1. `resolveUserScope` strict lockdown for pending accounts across all roles.
  2. `generateAndSendSignupOtp` pre-OTP password policy verification.
  3. Server mutation rejection across practical logs, live sessions, workstation issues, timetable schedules, and incidents when executed under a pending session.
- **Test Results**: All 4 subtests passed (`pass: 4, fail: 0`).
- **Strict TypeScript Compliance**: `npx tsc --noEmit` exited with code 0 (0 compile errors across all routes and components).

# Task 51: Self-Service Credentials Management & Dual-OTP Email Change Handshake

> **Authoritative Log**: Executed on October 18, 2026  
> **Status**: COMPLETED  
> **Target Subsystems**: `app/actions/profile-credentials.ts`, `lib/mailer.ts`, `components/profile/teacher-profile-form.tsx`, `tests/profile-credentials.test.mjs`, `lib/audit/audit-ledger.ts`

---

## 1. Context & Operational Imperative

Faculty and staff members operating laboratory workstations frequently needed self-service capabilities to manage their security credentials without administrative intervention:
1. **Password Updates**: Changing login passwords directly from their institutional profile while strictly complying with institutional password policies and validating the current password.
2. **Institutional Email Updates**: Updating email addresses in shared laboratory computer environments. In schools and computer labs where faculty members might leave workstations unlocked, a naive email change mechanism creates a severe account takeover vector.
3. **The Dual-OTP Security Handshake**:
   - The user explicitly required: **Current Password Re-Authentication** + **OTP Verification sent to Old (Current) Email** + **OTP Verification sent to New Email**.
   - **Why this makes absolute security sense**:
     - **Old Email OTP (Authorization Gate)**: Prevents session hijacking or rogue actions if someone accesses an unattended logged-in browser session. The attacker cannot steal the account because the legitimate account owner immediately gets an alert and the authorization code on their primary inbox.
     - **New Email OTP (Ownership & Destination Gate)**: Prevents accidental lockouts caused by typos (e.g. typing `@gnail.com` instead of `@gmail.com`) and proves authentic control over the target inbox before any database records are modified.
     - **Current Password Gate**: Proves the person initiating the change knows the root secret of the account.

---

## 2. Architecture & Implementation

### A. Dual-Channel Email Dispatch (`lib/mailer.ts`)
Added two dedicated institutional email notification templates:
- `sendOldEmailChangeSecurityOtp(oldEmail, fullName, otpCode, targetNewEmail)`:
  Dispatches an urgent security notification with a high-contrast amber security badge, explaining that an email transfer to `targetNewEmail` was requested. Dispatches the 6-digit authorization code and instructs the user to alert the IT office immediately if they did not initiate the request.
- `sendNewEmailChangeVerificationOtp(newEmail, fullName, otpCode)`:
  Dispatches a clean verification email to the prospective address with a 6-digit confirmation code and a 10-minute expiry warning.

### B. High-Assurance Server Actions (`app/actions/profile-credentials.ts`)
Constructed three transactional server actions:
1. `changeCurrentPassword({ currentPassword, newPassword, confirmPassword })`:
   - Validates user session.
   - Enforces `validatePasswordPolicy(newPassword)` against institutional complexity requirements.
   - Confirms `newPassword !== currentPassword`.
   - Re-authenticates `currentPassword` against `auth.users.encrypted_password` using PostgreSQL `crypt()`.
   - Atomically updates `auth.users.encrypted_password` using `crypt($1, gen_salt('bf', 10))`.
   - Records forensic event in `public.audit_ledger` with `action: 'UPDATE'` and `entityType: 'policy'`.
   - Invalidates user profile cache and revalidates `/profile`.
2. `initiateDualOtpEmailChange({ currentPassword, newEmail })`:
   - Re-authenticates user identity with `currentPassword`.
   - Validates `newEmail` format and checks `auth.users` for duplicate accounts.
   - Ensures `newEmail !== currentEmail`.
   - Generates two distinct 6-digit cryptographically random numeric OTPs.
   - Records both into `public.email_otps` with purposes `'email_change_old'` and `'email_change_new'` with a 10-minute expiry.
   - Dispatches both emails concurrently via `Promise.all`.
   - In non-production environments, provides `devOtpOld` and `devOtpNew` helpers for streamlined QA.
3. `confirmDualOtpEmailChange({ currentPassword, newEmail, oldEmailOtp, newEmailOtp })`:
   - Re-verifies user session and current password.
   - Validates the Old Email OTP from `public.email_otps` with attempt-limiting (max 5 attempts).
   - Validates the New Email OTP from `public.email_otps` with attempt-limiting.
   - Executes an atomic PostgreSQL transaction (`BEGIN ... COMMIT`):
     - Marks both OTPs as verified (`is_verified = TRUE`).
     - Migrates `auth.users.email`.
     - Migrates `auth.identities.identity_data` (updating `sub` and `email`).
     - Migrates `public.profiles.email`.
   - Records forensic audit trail in `public.audit_ledger`.
   - Purges user profile caches and revalidates dashboard layouts.

### C. Tabbed Profile Interface (`components/profile/teacher-profile-form.tsx`)
Elevated `/profile` with a 3-tab layout:
- **Tab 1: Academic Profile & Scope**: Name, honorific, phone, biographical statement, institutional role badge, designated laboratories, and curricular practical subjects table.
- **Tab 2: Security & Credentials**:
  - **Change Account Password card**: Eye visibility toggles, interactive `PasswordRequirementsChecklist` showing real-time feedback as the user types, and confirmation matching.
  - **Change Institutional Email card**: High-clarity Dual-Channel Handshake badge and institutional explanation banner. Step 1 (password re-authentication + target email) transitioning to Step 2 (Dual OTP code inputs with 60-second resend countdown and change destination shortcut).
  - **Institutional Clearance & Session Telemetry card**: Role, Account Status, and Operational Clearance summary.
- **Tab 3: Preferences & Alerts**: Practical subject focus chips, timetable conflict alerts, and 15-minute practical reminder preferences.

---

## 3. Invariants & Rules Preserved

1. **Rule #1 (No Literal `+` in Buttons)**:
   All buttons utilize Lucide icons (`<Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />`, `<KeyRound className="h-4 w-4" />`, `<Check className="h-4 w-4" />`).
2. **Rule #8 (Strict TypeScript Compilation)**:
   Passed `npx tsc --noEmit` with 0 compile errors.
3. **Rule #13 (Next.js `'use server'` Export Rules)**:
   `app/actions/profile-credentials.ts` strictly exports only asynchronous functions.
4. **Rule #16 (Zero Shadow Databases)**:
   Credentials operations interface directly with live PostgreSQL `auth.users`, `auth.identities`, and `public.profiles`.

---

## 4. Verification & Automated Test Suite

Created and executed `tests/profile-credentials.test.mjs`:
- `1. Enforces institutional password policy correctly` &rarr; **PASSED**
- `2. Verifies current password authentication against crypt()` &rarr; **PASSED**
- `3. Atomically updates password with bcrypt hash` &rarr; **PASSED**
- `4. Simulates Dual-OTP generation for old and new email addresses` &rarr; **PASSED**
- `5. Rejects mismatched OTPs during Dual-OTP handshake` &rarr; **PASSED**
- `6. Atomically confirms Dual-OTP email migration across auth.users, auth.identities, and public.profiles` &rarr; **PASSED**

All 6 tests passed cleanly alongside regression test suites (`teacher-preferences-and-profile.test.mjs`, `pending-user-lockdown.test.mjs`, `password-policy-and-approval.test.mjs`).

FILE: doc\tasks\2026-10-18-task-52-database-pool-starvation-hardening-smtp-timeouts-and-autofill-remedy.md
================================================================================
# Task 52: Database Pool Starvation Hardening, SMTP Timeouts & Registration Autofill Remedy

> **Authoritative Log**: Executed on October 18, 2026  
> **Status**: COMPLETED  
> **Target Subsystems**: `app/actions/otp.ts`, `lib/mailer.ts`, `lib/db.ts`, `app/(auth)/login/page.tsx`, `tests/password-policy-and-approval.test.mjs`, `tests/profile-credentials.test.mjs`

---

## 1. Problem Statement & Root Cause Discovery

### A. Database Connection Pool Starvation & 71-Second Request Hang
1. **The Anti-Pattern**: In `app/actions/otp.ts`, both `generateAndSendSignupOtp` and `generateAndSendPasswordResetOtp` previously used manual checkouts (`const client = await pool.connect()`) across 5 sequential queries and held that client checked out while awaiting external network dispatch (`await sendPasswordResetOtpEmail(...)`).
2. **The 71-Second Hang**: In `lib/mailer.ts`, Nodemailer's `createTransport` had no configured network timeouts (relying on Node's default 120s socket timeout). When Gmail SMTP experienced latency or dropped packets, the server action stalled for 71 seconds while holding a database client out of the pool's 20-connection ceiling.
3. **Connection Starvation**: Holding checked-out connections across external network I/O quickly exhausted the PostgreSQL transaction pooler (`aws-0-ap-southeast-1.pooler.supabase.com:6543`), triggering `Connection terminated due to connection timeout` and 500 errors.

### B. Admin Credentials Auto-Injected in Registration Modal (IMG1)
1. **The Cause**: When opening the new user registration modal on `/login`, Chrome's Password Manager detected generic `<Input type="email">` and `<Input type="password">` elements in the DOM without `autoComplete="new-password"`.
2. Chrome assumed the modal was a re-login challenge on the same domain and automatically populated the saved institutional administrator credentials (`admin@rrl.edu.np` and `Admin@12345`), firing synthetic `input` events that inadvertently validated the password policy strength checklist with the admin's secret.

---

## 2. Architecture & Technical Remedies Applied

### A. Non-Blocking Direct Pool Queries (`app/actions/otp.ts`)
1. **Eliminated Client Check-Out Anti-Pattern**: Refactored `generateAndSendSignupOtp` and `generateAndSendPasswordResetOtp` to use `pool.query(...)` directly for all operations (rate limit validation, cooldown check, user verification, OTP invalidation, and OTP insertion).
2. **Instant Connection Return**: Every query borrows a connection for `< 2ms` and returns it immediately to the pool.
3. **Zero DB Connections Held During External I/O**: When `sendVerificationOtpEmail` or `sendPasswordResetOtpEmail` runs, **zero database connections are checked out or locked**.
4. **Transaction Reservation Invariant**: Manual checkouts (`pool.connect()`) are strictly restricted to atomic multi-table transactions (e.g. `verifySignupAndCreateAccount`, `confirmDualOtpEmailChange`), and are committed/released before background alerts.

### B. Strict SMTP Network Timeouts & Racing Ceiling (`lib/mailer.ts`)
1. **Nodemailer Transport Configuration**:
   - `connectionTimeout: 8000` (8s max to establish SMTP connection).
   - `greetingTimeout: 5000` (5s max for SMTP handshake greeting).
   - `socketTimeout: 10000` (10s socket inactivity limit).
2. **Hard Fallback Race Ceiling**: Wrapped `sendMail` with `Promise.race` against an **8.5-second timeout safeguard**. If SMTP stalls, the action terminates cleanly within 8.5s and returns a graceful `{ error: 'SMTP delivery timeout' }` instead of stalling the server action for 70+ seconds.

### C. Database Pool Runway & Resiliency (`lib/db.ts`)
1. Adjusted `connectionTimeoutMillis` to `15000` (15s) and `idleTimeoutMillis` to `30000` (30s) to absorb cross-region network latency to AWS Singapore poolers.

### D. Chrome Autofill Isolation & Registration Decoy Trap (`app/(auth)/login/page.tsx`)
1. **Decoy Input Fields**: Inserted hidden decoy inputs (`fake_usernameremembered` and `fake_passwordremembered`) at the top of the registration modal `<form autoComplete="off">` to trap Chrome's heuristic crawler.
2. **Explicit Attributes**:
   - Email: `name="new_faculty_email"`, `autoComplete="off"`, `data-lpignore="true"`.
   - Password: `name="new_faculty_password"`, `autoComplete="new-password"`, `data-lpignore="true"`.
   - Confirm Password: `name="new_faculty_confirm_password"`, `autoComplete="new-password"`, `data-lpignore="true"`.
   - Applied `autoComplete="new-password"` to the Password Recovery modal as well.
3. **State Purge on Modal Open**: The "Request Access" trigger now explicitly flushes all registration state (`regName`, `regEmail`, `regPhone`, `regPass`, `regConfirmPass`, `regOtp`) to empty strings when opening the dialog.

---

## 3. Verification & Invariants

- **TypeScript Verification**: Ran `npx tsc --noEmit` &rarr; **0 errors**.
- **Automated Test Suites**: All 13 unit and regression tests passed cleanly (`tests/password-policy-and-approval.test.mjs`, `tests/profile-credentials.test.mjs`).
- **Rule #1 Preserved**: Zero literal `+` buttons across all modified modal surfaces.

**Date**: 2026-10-18  
**Author**: Antigravity Assistant  
**Status**: Completed & Verified  
**Subsystems**: CAD Floor Plan, Live Cockpit Bar, Workstation Monitor Nodes, Diagnostics Triage Hub, Admin Floor Architect, Cohort Promotion Wizard, CSS Theme Tokens

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Objective

Laboratory operations in LMR (LabSync LIMS) require seamless readability and aesthetic excellence across both dark mode (`Deep Obsidian #0B0F19`) and light mode (`Pearl Slate #F8FAFC` / `Zinc 50–200`). Prior to this task, several high-impact UI areas were hardcoded with dark background colors (`bg-[#0B0F19]`, `bg-[#101726]`, `bg-[#151E32]`, `bg-zinc-900`) and dark text classes (`text-white`, `text-slate-100`), causing broken contrast, pitch-black canvases, and unreadable text when operating in Light Mode.

This task executed a comprehensive, system-wide dark and light theme overhaul resolving all 5 user screenshots and the 4 critical visual gaps identified during the system audit:
1. **Workstation Monitor Cards (PC-01 to PC-40)**: Recessed LCD monitor screens, bezels, roll badges, avatars, and stand bases.

# Task 55: Phase 1 — Canonical Database Actions, Relational Schema & Security Hardening

> **Task ID**: `2026-10-18-task-55-phase1-canonical-actions-and-security-hardening`  
> **Type**: Architecture, Data Normalization & Enterprise Security  
> **Status**: Completed  
> **Target Subsystems**: `app/actions/subjects.ts`, `app/actions/classes.ts`, `app/actions/logs.ts`, `app/actions/settings.ts`, `app/actions/academic-years.ts`, `app/actions/holidays.ts`, `app/actions/substitutions.ts`, `middleware.ts`, `components/layout/client-storage-evictor.tsx`, `tests/phase1-canonical-actions.test.mjs`  
> **Pre-requisite Audit Document**: [`doc/audits/2026-10-18-full-system-gap-analysis-and-production-roadmap.md`](../audits/2026-10-18-full-system-gap-analysis-and-production-roadmap.md)  

---

## 1. Objective & Problem Statement
During the development phase, the application relied on prototype fallback arrays in `lib/master-data.ts` and shadow state in `localStorage` and `institution_settings` JSON blobs. This created:
1. **Teacher Duplication & Leakage**: `getTeachers()` returned rejected accounts (`dnathshah11@gmail.com`) and administrators instead of approved active instructors.
2. **Missing Relational Junction Actions**: No canonical CRUD actions existed for `public.subjects`, `public.classes`, and the vital `public.class_subjects` relational mapping.
3. **Route & Action Vulnerabilities**: Next.js Server Actions bypassed middleware via `next-action` headers, while direct `pg.Pool` database connections bypassed Supabase RLS. Several mutating actions lacked server-side role validation (`getServerUserScope()`).
4. **Maintenance Route Exposure**: `/maintenance` was not guarded in `middleware.ts`.
5. **Ghost `localStorage` Caches**: Stale prototype keys in browser storage overrode live database records.

---

## 2. Key Changes Implemented

### A. Canonical Subject & Relational Junction Server Actions (`app/actions/subjects.ts`, `lib/types/subjects.ts`)
- Created `lib/types/subjects.ts` defining `SubjectRecord`, `CreateSubjectInput`, `UpdateSubjectInput`, `ClassSubjectRecord`, and `AssignSubjectToClassInput`.
- Implemented `app/actions/subjects.ts`:
  - `getSubjectsAction(filter?)`: Directly queries `public.subjects` with active/lab filters.
  - `createSubjectAction(data)`: RBAC-guarded (`super_admin` / privileged approved faculty), validates uppercase code uniqueness, inserts into `public.subjects`, and calls `revalidatePath`.
  - `updateSubjectAction(id, data)`: RBAC-guarded mutation with `revalidatePath`.
  - `deactivateSubjectAction(id)`: Enforces soft-deactivation (`is_active = false`) instead of hard deletion to protect foreign key integrity with historical `practical_logs`.
  - `getClassSubjectsAction(classId?, academicYearId?)`: Enriched SQL join querying `public.class_subjects` with `classes`, `subjects`, `profiles` (teachers), and `labs`.
  - `assignSubjectToClassAction(data)`: Upserts relational mappings in `public.class_subjects`.
  - `removeClassSubjectAction(classSubjectId)`: Safely removes mapping rows with RBAC guard.

### B. Class Mutations & Enrollment Safety (`app/actions/classes.ts`)
- Added `createClassAction`, `updateClassAction`, and `deactivateClassAction` with `getServerUserScope()` checks.
- Enforced soft-deactivation (`is_active = false`) on classes to preserve historical student enrollments.
- Removed hardcoded `'2083'` fallback from `getClassEnrollmentStats` in favor of dynamic `public.academic_years` resolution.

### C. Teacher Query Sanitization (`app/actions/logs.ts`)
- Updated `getTeachers()` to strictly enforce:
  ```sql
  SELECT id, full_name, email, role
  FROM public.profiles
  WHERE approval_status = 'approved'
    AND is_active = true
    AND role IN ('teacher', 'lab_incharge')
  ORDER BY full_name ASC;
  ```
- **Result**: Instantly resolved duplicate Dinanath Shah account (rejected `dnathshah11@gmail.com` excluded, approved active `suraj.dinsu@gmail.com` retained) and removed non-teaching administrators.

### D. Route Security & Maintenance Guard (`middleware.ts`)
- Added `/maintenance` route guard in `middleware.ts`:
  ```typescript
  if (pathname.startsWith('/maintenance')) {
    const canAccessMaintenance =
      role === 'super_admin' ||
      role === 'lab_incharge' ||
      role === 'hod' ||
      role === 'coordinator' ||
      (profile as any)?.permissions?.can_manage_maintenance === true

    if (!canAccessMaintenance || approvalStatus === 'pending') {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/?error=unauthorized_maintenance_access', request.url))
    }
  }
  ```

### E. Server Action RBAC Hardening
- **`app/actions/holidays.ts`**: Protected `addAcademicHoliday`, `updateAcademicHoliday`, and `deleteAcademicHoliday` with `getServerUserScope()` checks (`scope.isPrivileged && scope.approvalStatus === 'approved'`).
- **`app/actions/substitutions.ts`**: Protected `assignSubstitute` and `cancelSubstitution` with `getServerUserScope()` instructor authorization checks.
- **`app/actions/settings.ts`**: Added `getPeriodSlotsAction()` querying dynamic PostgreSQL bell timings and restricted period modifications to `super_admin`.
- **`app/actions/academic-years.ts`**: Exported `getActiveAcademicYearAction()` as canonical async function alias.

### F. Ghost `localStorage` Eviction (`components/layout/client-storage-evictor.tsx`)
- Created a lightweight client component mounted in `app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx` that purges legacy prototype keys (`lmr_admin_faculty_v2`, `lmr_admin_classes_v2`, `lmr_admin_subjects_v2`, `lab_master_routine_2083_v6`, `lmr_admin_periods_v2`, `lmr_admin_incident_categories_v2`) once on mount using sentinel key `lmr_prototype_keys_evicted_v1`.

---

## 3. Verification & Automated Test Suite

1. **TypeScript Strict Typecheck**:
   - `npx tsc --noEmit` executed: **0 errors across the entire codebase**.
2. **Automated Integration Test Suite (`tests/phase1-canonical-actions.test.mjs`)**:
   - `getSubjectsAction`: Confirmed returns active PostgreSQL subjects (29 subjects).
   - `getClassSubjectsAction`: Confirmed returns enriched relational mapping rows.
   - `getClassesAction`: Confirmed returns active class batches.
   - `getTeachers()`: Confirmed 0 dummy placeholders, 0 duplicate Dinanath Shah entries.
   - `getPeriodSlotsAction`: Confirmed dynamic slots query succeeds.
   - `getActiveAcademicYearAction`: Confirmed current academic year resolves from PostgreSQL.
   - **Result: 6/6 subtests passed (100%)**.

---

## 4. Invariant Preservation Checklist

- [x] **No Literal `+` in Buttons**: Lucide icons used throughout.
- [x] **Standardized Terminology**: "Attendance", "Timetable", "Logbook" strictly maintained.
- [x] **Zero Department Regressions**: Role badges and facility scope preserved; no legacy department strings.
- [x] **Zero Shadow Databases**: Canonical queries talk directly to live PostgreSQL tables.
- [x] **Soft-Deletions**: No raw `DELETE` queries on curriculum entities.
- [x] **Strict TypeScript Verification**: 0 compiler errors.

# Task 56: Phase 2 — Admin Console Unification & Canonical PostgreSQL Operations

> **Task ID**: `2026-10-18-task-56-phase2-admin-console-unification`  
> **Type**: Architecture, Frontend-Backend Unification & Relational Migration  
> **Status**: Completed  
> **Target Subsystems**: `app/(dashboard)/admin/page.tsx`, `components/admin/incident-categories-manager.tsx`, `components/dashboard/report-incident-modal.tsx`, `hooks/use-infrastructure-state.ts`, `types/workstations.ts`, `tests/phase2-admin-unification.test.mjs`  
> **Pre-requisite Audit Document**: [`doc/audits/2026-10-18-full-system-gap-analysis-and-production-roadmap.md`](../audits/2026-10-18-full-system-gap-analysis-and-production-roadmap.md)  
> **Previous Task**: [`doc/tasks/2026-10-18-task-55-phase1-canonical-actions-and-security-hardening.md`](./2026-10-18-task-55-phase1-canonical-actions-and-security-hardening.md)  

---

## 1. Objective & Problem Statement

Phase 1 established the canonical PostgreSQL Server Actions for subjects, classes, relational class-subject allocations, and security hardening. However, the Super Admin Console (`/admin`) and incident reporting interfaces still suffered from prototype shadow state:
1. **Curriculum Classes Sub-tab**: Read from `classes` in `useInfrastructureState` (which synced to `institution_settings` JSON blob `admin_classes_v2`) and wrote via prototype hooks (`addClass`, `updateClass`, `deleteClass`).
2. **Curriculum Subjects Sub-tab**: Read and mutated flat prototype subject arrays in `institution_settings` JSON blobs rather than querying `public.class_subjects` and `public.subjects`.
3. **Hardcoded Fallback Categories**: `DEFAULT_INCIDENT_CATEGORIES` mock array in `hooks/use-infrastructure-state.ts` shadowed live `public.issue_categories`, leading to schema disconnects in `ReportIncidentModal` and `/admin` Facilities categories governance.
4. **Foreign Key Integrity Risk**: Removing a class subject assignment without checking for existing `public.practical_logs(class_subject_id)` could orphan historical institutional logs.

Phase 2 unifies the entire Admin Console curriculum and incident governance workflows with canonical PostgreSQL tables, eradicating mock states, shadow JSON blobs, and prototype hooks.

---

## 2. Key Changes Implemented

### A. Admin Console Classes Sub-tab Canonical Relational Rewiring (`app/(dashboard)/admin/page.tsx`)
- Imported canonical actions: `getClassesAction`, `createClassAction`, `updateClassAction`, `deactivateClassAction`.
- Added reactive state `dbClasses: ClassRecord[]` loaded via `loadClassesData()`.
- Defined `effectiveClasses` prioritizing live PostgreSQL records:
  ```typescript
  const effectiveClasses: ClassEnrollmentItem[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (dbClasses && dbClasses.length > 0) {
      return dbClasses.map((c) => ({
        id: c.id,
        name: c.name,
        section: c.section || '',
        capacity: c.capacity,
        stream: c.stream_id || 'General Science',
        is_active: c.is_active,
      }))
    }
    return classes
  }, [dbClasses, classes])
  ```
- Rewired `handleSaveClass` to execute `createClassAction` and `updateClassAction`.
- Rewired class table rendering, active/archived status badges, and danger action deactivation via `deactivateClassAction`.
- Rewired `ClassRosterView`, `CohortPromotionWizard`, and Danger Zone Cohort Wipe dropdowns to consume `effectiveClasses`.

### B. Curriculum Catalog & Class-Subject Junction Rewiring (`app/(dashboard)/admin/page.tsx`)
- Imported canonical actions: `getSubjectsAction`, `getClassSubjectsAction`, `createSubjectAction`, `updateSubjectAction`, `assignSubjectToClassAction`, `removeClassSubjectAction`, and `getTeachers`.
- Added database states `dbSubjects`, `dbClassSubjects`, and `dbTeachers` loaded via `loadSubjectsData()`.

  - Enriched with class name, subject code, credit hours, default laboratory, and assigned instructor.

  - Flat view: Direct searchable tabular layout.

- Rewired Delete Subject action to execute `removeClassSubjectAction(s.classSubjectId)`.

### C. Incident Categories Direct PostgreSQL Migration (`components/admin/incident-categories-manager.tsx`, `components/dashboard/report-incident-modal.tsx`)
- Seeded laboratory safety and equipment categories into `public.issue_categories` (now totaling 16 active categories).
- Rewired `IncidentCategoriesManager` to read/write directly via `getIssueCategories()` and `manageIssueCategory()`.
- Removed deprecated prototype hook props (`categories`, `onAddCategory`, `onUpdateCategory`, etc.) from `/admin` page JSX.
- Rewired `ReportIncidentModal` to load categories directly from `getIssueCategories()` with `useEffect`, eliminating `incidentCategories` prop drilling.

### D. Foreign Key Referential Integrity Guard on `removeClassSubjectAction` (`app/actions/subjects.ts`)
- Added SQL pre-check on `public.practical_logs` before deleting junction rows:
  ```typescript
  const logCheck = await pool.query(
    `SELECT count(*)::int AS log_count FROM public.practical_logs WHERE class_subject_id = $1;`,
    [classSubjectId]
  )
  const logCount = logCheck.rows[0]?.log_count || 0
  if (logCount > 0) {
    return {
      success: false,
      error: `Cannot delete class-subject assignment: ${logCount} historical practical log(s) reference this assignment. Unassign the teacher instead to preserve official audit records.`,
    }
  }
  ```

- Added `is_active?: boolean` to `ClassEnrollmentItem` interface in `hooks/use-infrastructure-state.ts`.
- Made partial fields in `IssueCategoryInput` (`name?: string`, `type?: ...`, `color?: string`, etc.) in `types/workstations.ts` optional to support partial updates and ID-only deletions in `manageIssueCategory`.
- Corrected `IssueCategoryRow` imports to come directly from `@/types/database`.
